================================================================================
FILE: dump_project
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
tbr PRO / tbr-widget — Project Context Collector
Скрипт собирает все текстовые файлы проекта с учётом .gitignore в единый файл для промпта.
"""

import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# Бинарники и служебный мусор, который точно не надо слать в LLM
BINARY_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".exe", ".apk", ".aab", ".zip", ".tar", ".gz", ".7z",
    ".db", ".sqlite", ".sqlite3", ".wal", ".shm",
    ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".ico", ".webp", ".gif",
    ".keystore", ".jks", ".pyc", ".pyo", ".pyd",
    ".dll", ".so", ".dylib", ".bin", ".dat"
}

OUTPUT_FILE = "project_dump.txt"


def get_git_files() -> list[str]:
    """Получает список файлов проекта, исключая .gitignore."""
    try:
        cmd = ["git", "ls-files", "--cached", "--others", "--exclude-standard"]
        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
        return [line.strip() for line in result.stdout.splitlines() if line.strip()]
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[!] Ошибка вызова git: {e}")
        print("[*] Убедись, что Git установлен и ты запускаешь скрипт в корне репозитория.")
        return []


def is_text_file(filepath: Path) -> bool:
    """Проверяет расширение и пытается прочитать первый чанк файла."""
    if filepath.suffix.lower() in BINARY_EXTENSIONS:
        return False
    
    # Игнорируем сам файл дампа и сам этот скрипт
    if filepath.name in (OUTPUT_FILE, "dump_project.py"):
        return False

    try:
        with open(filepath, "tr", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.read(1024)
            return True
    except (UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
        return False


def main():
    root_dir = Path.cwd()
    print(f"[*] Сканирую репозиторий в: {root_dir}")

    files = get_git_files()
    if not files:
        print("[-] Список файлов пуст или проект не инициализирован как git-репо.")
        sys.exit(1)

    collected_count = 0
    skipped_count = 0

    with open(OUTPUT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
        for rel_path in files:
            full_path = root_dir / rel_path
            
            if not full_path.is_file():
                continue

            if not is_text_file(full_path):
                skipped_count += 1
                continue

            try:
                content = full_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
                out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
                out.write(f"FILE: {rel_path.replace(os.sep, '/')}\n")
                out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
                out.write(content)
                if not content.endswith("\n"):
                    out.write("\n")
                out.write("\n")
                collected_count += 1
            except Exception as ex:
                print(f"[!] Пропущен {rel_path}: {ex}")
                skipped_count += 1

    size_mb = os.path.getsize(OUTPUT_FILE) / (1024 * 1024)
    print("\n" + "=" * 40)
    print(f"[+] Готово! Собрано файлов: {collected_count}")
    print(f"[-] Пропущено (бинарники/кэш): {skipped_count}")
    print(f"[>] Результат сохранён в: {OUTPUT_FILE} ({size_mb:.2f} MB)")
    print("=" * 40)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
FILE: .github/workflows/release.yml
================================================================================
name: Build Windows EXE Release

on:
  push:
    tags:
      - '*'
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  build-windows:
    runs-on: windows-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout repo
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          python -m pip install --upgrade pip
          pip install -r requirements.txt pyinstaller

      - name: Build EXE with PyInstaller
        run: |
          pyinstaller --noconfirm --onefile --windowed --name tbr --hidden-import waitress --add-data "web/templates;web/templates" --add-data "web/static;web/static" main.py

      - name: Generate SHA-256 Checksum
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          $hash = (Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 dist/tbr.exe).Hash.ToLower()
          "$hash  tbr.exe" | Out-File -Encoding ascii -NoNewline dist/tbr.exe.sha256

      - name: Upload EXE and SHA256 to GitHub Release
        if: startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/')
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
        with:
          files: |
            dist/tbr.exe
            dist/tbr.exe.sha256
          generate_release_notes: true

================================================================================
FILE: Dockerfile
================================================================================
FROM python:3.12-slim

WORKDIR /app

COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt

COPY . .

ENV TBR_DATA_DIR=/data \
    TBR_BT_BACKUP=/bt_backup \
    PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1

EXPOSE 5000

CMD ["python", "main.py", "--headless", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "5000"]

> [читать на русском](README_RU.md)

# tbr pro — qbittorrent swiss-army knife

> supports **en / ru** ui toggle right in the header!

# features

1. **pause and resume all torrents at once** (groundbreaking technology, i know)

2. **export csv summaries** so you can finally show your wife, mom, dad, or boyfriend why your pc hasn't been turned off in a month

3. **fastresume stats merger with safe source cleanup:** if you're a ratio-obsessed stats nerd and don't want to lose all upload stats on a torrent—or keep 50 versions of the same game on your pc—you can transfer `uploaded` & `seeding_time` metadata to a new torrent using my *innovative* merger (safely closes and restarts qBittorrent so bencode doesn't get corrupted). Now with optional automated source deletion: it checks file paths to prevent accidental data loss and safely downgrades to torrent-only removal if folders overlap

4. **smart eta forecaster & honest speed history:** calculates when you'll hit a specific upload/ratio goal using weighted historical data (SQLite). The load chart is now continuous—no dumb hourly buckets, smoothed phantom spikes from newly added torrents, and detailed tooltips (hover over any point to see exact transfer speed and volume uploaded between snapshots)

5. **dead torrents & tracker error scanner:** multi-threaded scan through all trackers across your library, groups recurring tracker errors, and lets you pause all dead weight with a single click

6. **android home screen widget (`tbr-widget`) + PWA:** yes, you can now stare at your seedbox speeds right from your phone's home screen. Native Android widget with one-tap chart toggling (24h speed vs daily bars) and a manual `↻` refresh button, plus the web UI installs cleanly as an address-bar-free PWA

7. **profiles & RBAC (Admin / Viewer):** generate 1-click magic links with read-only Viewer permissions for friends or family—they get to admire your upload flex without being able to stop torrents or mess up your config

8. **personal spam bot disguised as automation (tg & discord):** set rules (min ratio / seeding days / upload volume / average speed in window W) so torrents auto-pause or get tagged—or just drop a discord webhook into your friends' server and forcibly subject them to your upload achievements

9. **works even without qbittorrent webui running** (well, except tab 1—obviously, it basically mirrors the webui)

10. **light as a feather** — tiny standalone `.exe`, no python required

11. **clean (for me) modular architecture** — every file strictly under 300 lines, zero spaghetti code

# why should i download your vibecode?

1. i haven't seen any real alternatives

2. the features are actually useful

3. please

# what do i need to make it work?

1. **qbittorrent webui** — password protected setups are supported! just enter your qB webui login/password in `⚙️ settings` (or leave it bypassed on localhost, up to you)

2. **qbittorrent** — haven't tested other clients (transmission doesn't count, it's kinda creepy). and if you don't use qB, why are you even here, normie?

3. **windows / linux / docker** — `.exe` is built for windows (tested on win 11), and now it also runs headless on linux & docker (`python main.py --headless` or via `Dockerfile`)

4. **want the phone widget?** grab the prebuilt `.apk` from the companion repo [tbr-widget](https://github.com/kamushek0o/tbr-widget/releases) — plug in your server URL and Viewer token, and you're good to go

5. **paranoid about random `.exe` files?** fair enough! the `.exe` in releases is auto-compiled by **github actions** straight from this open-source code (no sketchy local builds). or just run `pip install -r requirements.txt` -> `python main.py`, or build it yourself via `build.bat`

# outro

fellow pirates and piratesses, enjoy! don't eat yellow snow. if anything breaks or sucks, open an [issue](https://github.com/kamushek0o/tbr/issues) or hit me up at `loh260330@gmail.com`

# tbr pro — швейцарский нож для qbittorrent

> встроенный переключатель языка **en / ru** прямо в шапке!

# фичи

1. **останавливать и запускать все торренты разом** (нихуя себе, я считаю)

2. **выгружать сводки в csv**, чтобы вы наконец-то смогли показать жене, маме, отцу или парню, почему у вас комп не выключается уже месяц

3. **подменщик статистики (.fastresume) с умным удалением исходника:** если вы любитель подрочить на статистику и не хотите терять весь аплоад с раздачи — или держать на своём пк 50 версий одной игры — вы можете перенести метаданные `uploaded` и `seeding_time` в новый торрент через мой *инновационный* подменитель (он сам безопасно гасит и поднимает qBittorrent, чтобы бенкод не побился). А теперь ещё и умеет сам подчищать старый торрент: сверяет пути файлов, чтобы случайно не снести общий контент, и понижает удаление до «только запись в клиенте», если папки пересекаются

4. **умный калькулятор прогнозов и честная история отдачи:** считает, когда именно вы добьёте нужный аплоад или ratio по взвешенной истории (SQLite). График нагрузки теперь непрерывный — без тупых часовых бакетов, со сглаживанием фантомных всплесков и детальными тултипами (наводишь на точку — видишь точную скорость и сколько именно налило между замерами)

5. **сканер мёртвых раздач и дохлых трекеров:** в многопотоке опрашивает все трекеры в клиенте, группирует ошибки и даёт одной кнопкой отправить в нокаут (на паузу) весь мусор, который годами висит и спамит логами

6. **виджет на рабочий стол Android (`tbr-widget`) + PWA:** да-да, теперь можно залипать на скорость сидбокса прямо с экрана телефона. Есть нативный виджет с переключением графиков в один тап (скорость за 24ч / столбики по дням) и кнопкой `↻`, а веб-морда ставится как PWA-приложение без адресной строки

7. **профили и права доступа (Admin / Viewer):** можно выдать другу или маме безопасную ссылку в один клик с правами Viewer — смотреть на ваши терабайты смогут, а вот случайно стопнуть раздачи или снести конфиг уже хуй

8. **спам-бот под видом автоматизации (тг и дискорд):** задаёте правила (мин. ratio / дни сидирования / порог отдачи / средняя скорость в окне W), чтобы торренты сами стопались или получали теги — ну или просто закидываете вебхук в дискорд к друзьям и насильственно заставляете их любоваться вашими рекордами

9. **может работать и без запущенной вебморды кубита** (кроме 1 вкладки, не, ну а чо хотели, она вебморду по сути и повторяет)

10. **лёгкая, как пушинка** — маленький автономный `.exe`, python не нужен

11. **удобная (для меня) архитектура** — все модули строго до 300 строк, никакого спагетти-говна

# что мне нужно сделать, чтобы оно работало?

1. **вебморда qbittorrent** — можно с паролем! впишите логин и пароль от WebUI кубита в `⚙️ настройки` (ну или оставьте пустым для localhost, как душе угодно)

2. **qbittorrent** — на других клиентах не тестил (трансмишн не в счёт, он криповенький). а если вы пользуетесь не кубитом, то зачем вам вообще эта прога, вы же потребитель

3. **windows / linux / docker** — `.exe` сделан под винду (тестилось на 11), а ещё прога заводится на серверах без монитора и в докере (`python main.py --headless` или через `Dockerfile`)

4. **хотите виджет на телефон?** забирайте готовый `.apk` из соседнего репозитория [tbr-widget](https://github.com/kamushek0o/tbr-widget/releases) — прописываете адрес сервера, Viewer-токен, и всё тикает прямо на домашнем экране

5. **параноите из-за левых `.exe` файлов?** `.exe` в релизах автоматически собирается через **github actions** прямо из этого открытого кода (никаких левых бинарников с чужого компа). ну или если паранойя не отпускает: `pip install -r requirements.txt` -> `python main.py`, либо соберите сами через `build.bat`

# итог

братья пираты и пиратессы, пользуйтесь на здоровье, не кушайте жёлтый снег. если что-то сломается или не устраивает — пишите в [issues](https://github.com/kamushek0o/tbr/issues) или мне на почту `loh260330@gmail.com`

# verify_project.py
import os
import sys

REQUIRED_FILES = [
    "core/version.py", "core/ui_defaults.py", "core/app_state_db.py", "core/updater.py",
    "core/keep_awake.py", "core/reliability.py", "core/disaster_recovery.py",
    "core/torrent_diff.py", "core/orphan_scanner.py", "core/private_vault.py",
    "core/smart_timers.py", "core/dnd_digest.py", "core/smart_tagging.py",
    "core/torrent_targets.py", "core/tracker_inspector.py", "core/health_score.py",
    "core/analytics_insights.py", "core/achievements.py",
    "web/errors.py",
    "web/routes/system_bp.py", "web/routes/tools_bp.py",
    "web/routes/timers_bp.py", "web/routes/insights_bp.py", "web/routes/social_bp.py",
    "web/templates/error.html", "web/templates/partials/tab_tools.html",
    "web/templates/partials/modal_system.html",
    "web/static/system_tools.js", "web/static/merger_tools.js",
    "web/static/timers_ui.js", "web/static/palette_targets.js",
    "web/static/analytics_insights.js", "web/static/social_ui.js",
]

missing = [f for f in REQUIRED_FILES if not os.path.isfile(f)]
if missing:
    print("❌ НЕ ХВАТАЕТ ФАЙЛОВ:")
    for f in missing:
        print("  -", f)
    sys.exit(1)

print(f"✅ Все {len(REQUIRED_FILES)} файлов на месте!")
try:
    import web.app  # noqa: F401
    import main     # noqa: F401
    print("✅ Тестовый импорт web.app и main прошёл успешно! Можно собирать v3.0.1.")
except Exception as e:
    print(f"❌ Ошибка импорта: {e}")
    sys.exit(1)

================================================================================
FILE: build.bat
================================================================================
@echo off
chcp 65001 >nul
title Сборка tbr PRO (Self-Healing PyInstaller)

set "ROOT=%~dp0"
if "%ROOT:~-1%"=="\" set "ROOT=%ROOT:~0,-1%"

set "ENTRY=%ROOT%\main.py"
set "DATA_TPL=%ROOT%\web\templates;web\templates"
set "DATA_STC=%ROOT%\web\static;web\static"

echo ==================================================
echo            СБОРКА tbr PRO (PyInstaller)
echo ==================================================
echo.
echo [1] Только ONEFILE  (-^> dist\onefile\tbr.exe)
echo [2] Только ONEDIR   (-^> dist\onedir\tbr\tbr.exe)
echo [3] ОБА ВАРИАНТА    (-^> разложит по разным папкам)
echo.
set /p mode="Выберите режим [1, 2 или 3]: "

if "%mode%"=="1" goto prep
if "%mode%"=="2" goto prep
if "%mode%"=="3" goto prep

echo.
echo ❌ Неверный выбор! Введите 1, 2 или 3.
pause
exit /b 1

:prep
echo.
echo [🔧] Проверка среды и снятие блокировок...

taskkill /F /IM tbr.exe >nul 2>&1
timeout /t 1 /nobreak >nul

python --version >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo ❌ Python не найден в системе! Установи Python 3.10+ с галочкой Add to PATH.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

python -c "import PyInstaller, flask, pystray, PIL, waitress" >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [📦] Доустанавливаю библиотеки из requirements.txt и PyInstaller...
    python -m pip install --upgrade pip
    if exist "%ROOT%\requirements.txt" (
        python -m pip install -r "%ROOT%\requirements.txt" pyinstaller
    ) else (
        python -m pip install flask pystray pillow waitress pyinstaller
    )
    if errorlevel 1 (
        echo ❌ Не удалось установить зависимости!
        pause
        exit /b 1
    )
)

if "%mode%"=="2" goto build_onedir
goto build_onefile

:build_onefile
echo.
echo [1/2] Очистка и сборка ONEFILE в dist\onefile\...
if exist "%ROOT%\dist\onefile" rd /s /q "%ROOT%\dist\onefile" >nul 2>&1
if exist "%ROOT%\dist\onefile" (
    echo ❌ Папка dist\onefile заблокирована! Закрой её в Проводнике Windows и повтори.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)
if exist "%ROOT%\build\onefile" rd /s /q "%ROOT%\build\onefile" >nul 2>&1
mkdir "%ROOT%\build\onefile"

python -m PyInstaller --noconfirm --clean --onefile --windowed --name "tbr" ^
    --hidden-import "waitress" ^
    --distpath "%ROOT%\dist\onefile" ^
    --workpath "%ROOT%\build\onefile" ^
    --specpath "%ROOT%\build\onefile" ^
    --add-data "%DATA_TPL%" ^
    --add-data "%DATA_STC%" "%ENTRY%"
if errorlevel 1 goto build_error

if "%mode%"=="3" goto build_onedir
goto done

:build_onedir
echo.
echo [2/2] Очистка и сборка ONEDIR в dist\onedir\...
if exist "%ROOT%\dist\onedir" rd /s /q "%ROOT%\dist\onedir" >nul 2>&1
if exist "%ROOT%\dist\onedir" (
    echo ❌ Папка dist\onedir заблокирована! Закрой её в Проводнике Windows и повтори.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)
if exist "%ROOT%\build\onedir" rd /s /q "%ROOT%\build\onedir" >nul 2>&1
mkdir "%ROOT%\build\onedir"

python -m PyInstaller --noconfirm --clean --onedir --windowed --name "tbr" ^
    --hidden-import "waitress" ^
    --distpath "%ROOT%\dist\onedir" ^
    --workpath "%ROOT%\build\onedir" ^
    --specpath "%ROOT%\build\onedir" ^
    --add-data "%DATA_TPL%" ^
    --add-data "%DATA_STC%" "%ENTRY%"
if errorlevel 1 goto build_error
goto done

:done
echo.
echo ==================================================
echo ✅ Сборка успешно завершена!
if not "%mode%"=="2" echo    OneFile: dist\onefile\tbr.exe
if not "%mode%"=="1" echo    OneDir:  dist\onedir\tbr\tbr.exe
echo ==================================================
pause
exit /b 0

:build_error
echo.
echo ❌ Произошла ошибка при компиляции!
pause
exit /b 1

    {'code': 'marathon_1tb', 'icon': '⚡', 'title': 'Суточный марафон', 'title_en': 'Daily Marathon', 'desc': 'Отдать 1+ TiB за одни сутки', 'desc_en': 'Upload 1+ TiB in a single day'},
    {'code': 'surgeon', 'icon': '🩺', 'title': 'Главный хирург', 'title_en': 'Chief Surgeon', 'desc': 'Провести хотя бы один мердж .fastresume', 'desc_en': 'Complete at least one .fastresume merge'},
]

_LAST_EVAL_TS = 0.0


def _get_unlocked_map():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute("SELECT code, unlocked_at, meta_json FROM achievements").fetchall()
                return {r['code']: {'unlocked_at': r['unlocked_at'], 'meta_json': r['meta_json']} for r in rows}
        except Exception:
            return {}


def _unlock(code, meta=None):
    now = time.time()
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute(
                    "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO achievements (code, unlocked_at, meta_json) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                    (code, now, json.dumps(meta or {}, ensure_ascii=False))
                )
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log(f"unlock achievement {code}", e)


def _check_night_and_day_records():
    rows = summary_tracker.db.get_snapshots_since(time.time() - 60 * 86400)
    night_by_date = {}
    day_by_date = {}
    prev = None
    for r in rows:
        if prev is not None:
            d = max(0, int(r['total_uploaded']) - int(prev['total_uploaded']))
            dt = float(r['timestamp']) - float(prev['timestamp'])
            if dt >= 60 and d > 0 and (d / dt) < (1024 ** 3):
                dt_obj = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(r['timestamp'])
                ds = dt_obj.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
                day_by_date[ds] = day_by_date.get(ds, 0) + d
                if 0 <= dt_obj.hour < 8:
                    night_by_date[ds] = night_by_date.get(ds, 0) + d
        prev = r
    max_night = max(night_by_date.values(), default=0)
    max_day = max(day_by_date.values(), default=0)
    return max_night >= (500 * 1024 ** 3), max_day >= (1024 ** 4)


def evaluate_achievements(force=False):
    global _LAST_EVAL_TS
    now = time.time()
    if not force and (now - _LAST_EVAL_TS) < 3600:
        return
    _LAST_EVAL_TS = now

    unlocked = _get_unlocked_map()
    if len(unlocked) == len(ACHIEVEMENTS_CATALOG):
        return

    summaries = get_global_torrent_summaries()
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    live_lower = {k.lower(): v for k, v in live.items()}

    tot_up = 0
    max_act = 0
    last_seed_cnt = 0
    has_ratio_god = False

    for s in summaries:
        hl = (s.get('hash_id') or '').lower()
        lv = live_lower.get(hl, {})
        up = int(lv.get('uploaded', s.get('uploaded', 0)) or 0)
        sz = int(lv.get('total_size', s.get('size', 1)) or 1)
        act = int(s.get('active_seconds', 0) or 0)
        tot_up += up
        if act > max_act:
            max_act = act
        if sz >= (100 * 1024 ** 3) and (up / sz) >= 10.0:
            has_ratio_god = True
        if lv and compute_health_metrics(lv).get('is_last_seed'):
            last_seed_cnt += 1

    checks = {
        'first_tb': tot_up >= (1024 ** 4),
        'ten_tb': tot_up >= (10 * 1024 ** 4),
        'fifty_tb': tot_up >= (50 * 1024 ** 4),
        'veteran_100d': max_act >= (100 * 86400),
        'keeper_5': last_seed_cnt >= 5,
        'ratio_god': has_ratio_god,
        'collector_100': len(summaries) >= 100,
    }

    if 'night_shift_500g' not in unlocked or 'marathon_1tb' not in unlocked:
        hit_night, hit_day = _check_night_and_day_records()
        checks['night_shift_500g'] = hit_night
        checks['marathon_1tb'] = hit_day

    if 'surgeon' not in unlocked:
        checks['surgeon'] = len(app_state_db.get_audit_events(category='merger', limit=1)) > 0

    for code, ok in checks.items():
        if ok and code not in unlocked:
            _unlock(code)


def get_achievements_view():
    evaluate_achievements(force=False)
    unlocked = _get_unlocked_map()
    out = []
    for item in ACHIEVEMENTS_CATALOG:
        u = unlocked.get(item['code'])
        dt_str = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(u['unlocked_at']).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') if u else None
        out.append({
            **item,
            'unlocked': bool(u),
            'unlocked_at': dt_str,
        })
    return {
        'unlocked_count': sum(1 for x in out if x['unlocked']),
        'total_count': len(out),
        'items': out,
    }

================================================================================
FILE: core/analytics_insights.py

# core/analytics_insights.py
import time
import datetime
import threading
import urllib.parse
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size
from core.qb_api import get_torrent_peers, get_transfer_info
from core.scheduler import MIN_DELTA_SEC
from core.widget_service import build_detailed_speed_series

_CLIENTS_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_CLIENTS_CACHE = {'ts': 0.0, 'data': None}
_CLIENTS_TTL = 900.0
_ISP_TIERS_MBPS = (10, 20, 25, 30, 50, 75, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300, 500, 1000)


def fetch_torrent_peers_safe(ip, port, t_hash):
    if not port or not t_hash:
        return []
    raw = get_torrent_peers(ip, port, t_hash)
    peers = raw.get('peers') or {}
    if not isinstance(peers, dict):
        return []

    out = []
    for p in peers.values():
        if not isinstance(p, dict):
            continue
        up = int(p.get('up_speed', 0) or 0)
        dl = int(p.get('dl_speed', 0) or 0)
        prog = round(float(p.get('progress', 0.0) or 0.0) * 100.0, 1)
        out.append({
            'client': str(p.get('client') or 'Unknown').strip() or 'Unknown',
            'progress': prog,
            'up_speed': up,
            'up_speed_fmt': f"{format_file_size(up)}/s",
            'dl_speed': dl,
            'dl_speed_fmt': f"{format_file_size(dl)}/s",
            'flags': str(p.get('flags') or ''),
            'country_code': str(p.get('country_code') or '').upper()[:4]
        })
    out.sort(key=lambda x: x['up_speed'], reverse=True)
    return out


def _classify_peer_client(raw_client):
    low = str(raw_client or '').lower()
    if 'qbittorrent' in low:
        return 'qBittorrent'
    if 'transmission' in low:
        return 'Transmission'
    if 'utorrent' in low or 'µtorrent' in low:
        return 'uTorrent'
    if 'deluge' in low:
        return 'Deluge'
    if 'biglybt' in low:
        return 'BiglyBT'
    if 'aria2' in low:
        return 'Aria2'
    if 'tixati' in low:
        return 'Tixati'
    if 'vuze' in low or 'azureus' in low:
        return 'Vuze'
    if 'bittorrent' in low:
        return 'BitTorrent'
    if 'libtorrent' in low:
        return 'libtorrent'
    return 'Прочие'


def get_peer_clients_breakdown(ip, port, live, force=False):
    now = time.time()
    with _CLIENTS_LOCK:
        if not force and _CLIENTS_CACHE['data'] is not None and (now - _CLIENTS_CACHE['ts']) < _CLIENTS_TTL:
            return dict(_CLIENTS_CACHE['data'])

    if not port or not live:
        return {'labels': [], 'values': [], 'total_peers': 0, 'cached_at': int(now)}

    active_hashes = [
        h for h, inf in sorted(
            live.items(),
            key=lambda kv: int(kv[1].get('num_leechs', 0) or 0) + int(kv[1].get('num_seeds', 0) or 0),
            reverse=True
        )
        if int(inf.get('num_leechs', 0) or 0) + int(inf.get('num_seeds', 0) or 0) > 0
    ][:40]

    counts = {}
    total_peers = 0
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
        futs = [pool.submit(fetch_torrent_peers_safe, ip, port, h) for h in active_hashes]
        for fut in as_completed(futs):
            try:
                for p in fut.result():
                    fam = _classify_peer_client(p['client'])
                    counts[fam] = counts.get(fam, 0) + 1
                    total_peers += 1
            except Exception:
                pass

    sorted_items = sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
    res = {
        'labels': [k for k, _ in sorted_items],
        'values': [v for _, v in sorted_items],
        'total_peers': total_peers,
        'cached_at': int(now)
    }
    with _CLIENTS_LOCK:
        _CLIENTS_CACHE['ts'] = now
        _CLIENTS_CACHE['data'] = dict(res)
    return res


def get_tracker_analytics(db, summaries, live=None, period='7d', allowed_hashes=None):
    per = period if period in ('7d', '30d', 'all') else '7d'
    live_lower = {k.lower(): v for k, v in (live or {}).items()}
    allowed = {h.lower() for h in allowed_hashes} if allowed_hashes is not None else None

    domain_by_hash = {}
    all_up_by_hash = {}
    for s in summaries:
        hl = (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash') or '').lower()
        if not hl or (allowed is not None and hl not in allowed):
            continue
        lv = live_lower.get(hl, {})
        tr_url = str(lv.get('tracker') or '').strip()
        if not tr_url and s.get('trackers'):
            tr_url = str(s['trackers'][0] or '').strip()
        host = urllib.parse.urlparse(tr_url).netloc.lower().split(':')[0] if tr_url else ''
        domain_by_hash[hl] = host or 'Без трекера / DHT'
        all_up_by_hash[hl] = int(lv.get('uploaded', s.get('uploaded', 0)) or 0)

    if per == 'all':
        vol_map = all_up_by_hash
    else:
        days = 30 if per == '30d' else 7
        vol_map = db.get_torrents_positive_deltas(time.time() - days * 86400, allowed_hashes=domain_by_hash.keys())

    by_domain = {}
    for hl, dom in domain_by_hash.items():
        val = int(vol_map.get(hl, 0) or 0)
        if val > 0:
            by_domain[dom] = by_domain.get(dom, 0) + val

    sorted_dom = sorted(by_domain.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
    top = sorted_dom[:9]
    if len(sorted_dom) > 9:
        top.append(('Другие', sum(v for _, v in sorted_dom[9:])))

    return {
        'period': per,
        'labels': [k for k, _ in top],
        'values': [round(v / (1024 ** 3), 2) for _, v in top]
    }


def get_heatmap_365(db):
    since_ts = time.time() - (365 * 86400)
    day_bytes = {}
    with db._lock:
        try:
            with db._get_connection() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute("""
                    WITH diffs AS (
                        SELECT substr(datetime, 1, 10) AS day,
                               MAX(0, total_uploaded - LAG(total_uploaded, 1, total_uploaded) OVER (ORDER BY timestamp ASC)) AS delta
                        FROM snapshots
                        WHERE timestamp >= ? AND length(datetime) >= 10
                    )
                    SELECT day, SUM(delta) AS up_bytes
                    FROM diffs
                    GROUP BY day
                    ORDER BY day ASC
                """, (since_ts,)).fetchall()
                for r in rows:
                    day_bytes[r['day']] = int(r['up_bytes'] or 0)
        except Exception:
            pass

    today = datetime.date.today()
    cells = []
    max_val = max(day_bytes.values(), default=0)
    for offset in range(364, -1, -1):
        d = today - datetime.timedelta(days=offset)
        ds = d.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
        b = day_bytes.get(ds, 0)
        lvl = 0 if (b <= 0 or max_val <= 0) else (4 if (b / max_val) >= 0.75 else (3 if (b / max_val) >= 0.45 else (2 if (b / max_val) >= 0.15 else 1)))
        cells.append({'date': ds, 'weekday': d.weekday(), 'bytes': b, 'gb': round(b / (1024 ** 3), 2), 'fmt': format_file_size(b), 'level': lvl})
    return cells


def _sum_range_deltas(rows, start_ts, end_ts):
    total, prev = 0, None
    for r in rows:
        ts = float(r['timestamp'])
        if prev is not None and start_ts <= ts <= end_ts:
            dt = ts - float(prev['timestamp'])
            d = max(0, int(r['total_uploaded']) - int(prev['total_uploaded']))
            if dt >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and d > 0:
                total += d
        prev = r
    return total


def get_records_and_comparisons(db, heatmap_cells=None, force_recalc=False):
    now_dt = datetime.datetime.now()
    now_ts = now_dt.timestamp()

    # Начало сегодняшних суток и симметричный отрезок вчера (до той же секунды)
    today_start = now_dt.replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp()
    yest_start = today_start - 86400
    yest_same_end = now_ts - 86400

    # Начало текущей недели (понедельник 00:00) и симметричный отрезок прошлой недели
    week_start = (now_dt - datetime.timedelta(days=now_dt.weekday())).replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp()
    prev_week_start = week_start - (7 * 86400)
    prev_week_same_end = now_ts - (7 * 86400)

    _, min_ts = db.get_snapshot_range_info()
    rows_15d = db.get_snapshots_since(prev_week_start - 3600)

    # Честный Like-for-Like расчёт
    today_b = _sum_range_deltas(rows_15d, today_start, now_ts)
    yest_b = _sum_range_deltas(rows_15d, yest_start, yest_same_end)
    this_w_b = _sum_range_deltas(rows_15d, week_start, now_ts)
    prev_w_b = _sum_range_deltas(rows_15d, prev_week_start, prev_week_same_end)

    day_pct = round(((today_b - yest_b) / yest_b) * 100.0, 1) if (min_ts and min_ts <= yest_start + 3600 and yest_b > 0) else None
    week_pct = round(((this_w_b - prev_w_b) / prev_w_b) * 100.0, 1) if (min_ts and min_ts <= prev_week_start + 3600 and prev_w_b > 0) else None

    # Порог канала для отсечения аномальных пиков
    max_mbps = max(10, cfg.getint('Max_Channel_Mbps', 1000))
    max_physical_bps = (max_mbps * 1024 * 1024) / 8.0

    all_rows = db.get_snapshots_since(0)
    peak_bps, peak_date, prev_r = 0.0, '—', None
    for r in all_rows:
        if prev_r is not None:
            dt = float(r['timestamp']) - float(prev_r['timestamp'])
            d = max(0, int(r['total_uploaded']) - int(prev_r['total_uploaded']))
            if dt >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and d > 0:
                bps = d / dt
                if bps <= max_physical_bps and bps > peak_bps:
                    peak_bps = bps
                    dt_raw = str(r.get('datetime') or '').replace('|', '').strip()
                    peak_date = dt_raw[:16] if dt_raw else '—'
        prev_r = r

    cells = heatmap_cells if heatmap_cells is not None else get_heatmap_365(db)
    best_cell = max(cells, key=lambda c: c['bytes'], default=None)

    return {
        'today_fmt': format_file_size(today_b),
        'yesterday_fmt': format_file_size(yest_b) if (min_ts and min_ts <= yest_start + 3600) else '—',
        'day_pct': day_pct,
        'this_week_fmt': format_file_size(this_w_b),
        'prev_week_fmt': format_file_size(prev_w_b) if (min_ts and min_ts <= prev_week_start + 3600) else '—',
        'week_pct': week_pct,
        'peak_speed_fmt': f"{format_file_size(peak_bps)}/s" if peak_bps > 0 else '—',
        'peak_speed_date': peak_date,
        'best_day_fmt': best_cell['fmt'] if (best_cell and best_cell['bytes'] > 0) else '—',
        'best_day_date': best_cell['date'] if (best_cell and best_cell['bytes'] > 0) else '—',
    }


def detect_isp_throttling(db, ip, port):
    if port:
        tr = get_transfer_info(ip, port)
        if int(tr.get('up_rate_limit') or 0) > 0:
            return {'throttled': False, 'reason': 'qb_limit_set'}

    pts = build_detailed_speed_series(db, hours=48)
    active = sorted([p['speed_mbs'] for p in pts if p['speed_mbs'] >= 1.0], reverse=True)
    if len(active) < 8:
        return {'throttled': False}

    top_slice = active[:6]
    hi, lo = top_slice[0], top_slice[-1]
    if hi < 1.0 or ((hi - lo) / hi) > 0.08:
        return {'throttled': False}

    avg_mbs = sum(top_slice) / len(top_slice)
    avg_mbps = (avg_mbs * 1024 * 1024 * 8) / 1_000_000.0
    for tier in _ISP_TIERS_MBPS:
        if abs(avg_mbps - tier) / tier <= 0.12:
            return {'throttled': True, 'tier_mbps': tier, 'plateau_mbs': round(avg_mbs, 2)}
    return {'throttled': False}

================================================================================
FILE: core/app_state_db.py
================================================================================
# core/app_state_db.py
import os
import json
import time
import sqlite3
import threading
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR, write_error_log


class AppStateDB:
    def __init__(self):
        self.db_file = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "app_state.db")
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._init_db()

    def _get_conn(self):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_file, timeout=30.0)
        conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 30000;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;")
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        return conn

    def _init_db(self):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.executescript("""
                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_log (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                            created_at REAL NOT NULL,
                            category TEXT NOT NULL,
                            actor TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'system',
                            action TEXT NOT NULL,
                            details TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
                        );
                        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_cat_ts ON audit_log(category, created_at DESC);

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS smart_freeze (
                            hash_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                            frozen_at REAL NOT NULL
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS snooze_timers (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                            resume_at REAL NOT NULL,
                            mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pause',
                            active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
                            created_at REAL NOT NULL
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS expiration_timers (
                            hash_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                            expires_at REAL,
                            target_uploaded INTEGER,
                            delete_files INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            created_at REAL NOT NULL
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dnd_queue (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                            title TEXT NOT NULL,
                            lines_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                            created_at REAL NOT NULL
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS torrent_targets (
                            hash_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                            target_ratio REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            target_bytes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            achieved_at REAL,
                            updated_at REAL NOT NULL
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS private_vault_tags (
                            tag TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                            created_at REAL NOT NULL
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS achievements (
                            code TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                            unlocked_at REAL NOT NULL,
                            meta_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'
                        );

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS watchdog_state (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id = 1),
                            consecutive_restarts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            down_since REAL,
                            last_restart_at REAL,
                            halted INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            recheck_attempts_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'
                        );
                    """)
                    conn.execute(
                        """INSERT OR IGNORE INTO watchdog_state
                           (id, consecutive_restarts, down_since, last_restart_at, halted, recheck_attempts_json)
                           VALUES (1, 0, NULL, NULL, 0, '{}')"""
                    )
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Инициализация app_state.db", e)

    def add_audit_event(self, category, action, details="", actor="system", max_rows=1000):
        cat = str(category or "system").strip().lower()
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute(
                        "INSERT INTO audit_log (created_at, category, actor, action, details) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                        (time.time(), cat, str(actor or "system")[:64], str(action)[:160], str(details or "")[:1000])
                    )
                    conn.execute(
                        """DELETE FROM audit_log WHERE id NOT IN (
                               SELECT id FROM audit_log ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?
                           )""",
                        (max(100, int(max_rows)),)
                    )
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка add_audit_event", e)

    def get_audit_events(self, category="", limit=100, offset=0):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    if category:
                        cur.execute(
                            "SELECT * FROM audit_log WHERE category = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?",
                            (category.strip().lower(), int(limit), int(offset))
                        )
                    else:
                        cur.execute(
                            "SELECT * FROM audit_log ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?",
                            (int(limit), int(offset))
                        )
                    return [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
            except Exception:
                return []

    def get_watchdog_state(self):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM watchdog_state WHERE id = 1").fetchone()
                    if row:
                        return dict(row)
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка get_watchdog_state", e)
            return {
                "id": 1, "consecutive_restarts": 0, "down_since": None,
                "last_restart_at": None, "halted": 0, "recheck_attempts_json": "{}"
            }

    def update_watchdog_state(self, **fields):
        allowed = {"consecutive_restarts", "down_since", "last_restart_at", "halted", "recheck_attempts_json"}
        updates = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}
        if not updates:
            return
        with self._lock:
            try:
                set_clause = ", ".join(f"{k} = ?" for k in updates.keys())
                params = list(updates.values())
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute(f"UPDATE watchdog_state SET {set_clause} WHERE id = 1", params)
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка update_watchdog_state", e)

    def get_recheck_attempts(self):
        st = self.get_watchdog_state()
        try:
            data = json.loads(st.get("recheck_attempts_json") or "{}")
            return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
        except Exception:
            return {}

    def save_recheck_attempts(self, attempts_map):
        clean = {str(k).lower(): float(v) for k, v in (attempts_map or {}).items()}
        self.update_watchdog_state(recheck_attempts_json=json.dumps(clean))


app_state_db = AppStateDB()

================================================================================
FILE: core/automation.py

# core/automation.py
import os
import time
import sqlite3
import threading
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR, format_file_size, write_log, write_error_log
from core.qb_api import (
    detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, send_qb_command,
    add_torrent_tags, remove_torrent_tags
)
from core.torrent_parser import get_all_torrents
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.notifiers import notify_all, test_notifications
from core.system_alerts import system_alerts
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.keep_awake import keep_awake
from core.reliability import run_auto_recheck, run_watchdog
from core.disaster_recovery import maybe_run_weekly_manifest
from core.smart_timers import tick_snooze, tick_expirations
from core.smart_tagging import run_smart_tagging
from core.dnd_digest import flush_dnd_if_ready, maybe_send_digest
from core.torrent_targets import evaluate_targets
from core.achievements import evaluate_achievements

VALID_TIME_MODES = ('seeding_time', 'time_active')
VALID_SPEED_OPS = ('', '>=', '<')


def format_duration_ru(seconds):
    sec = max(0, int(seconds or 0))
    if sec >= 86400:
        return f"{sec // 86400} д {(sec % 86400) // 3600} ч"
    if sec >= 3600:
        return f"{sec // 3600} ч"
    return f"{sec // 60} мин"


class AutomationEngine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.db_file = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "automation.db")
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._running = False
        self._thread = None
        self._init_db()

    def _get_conn(self):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_file, timeout=30.0)
        conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 30000;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;")
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        return conn

    def _init_db(self):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.executescript("""
                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rules (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                            name TEXT NOT NULL, enabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
                            time_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'seeding_time',
                            min_ratio REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, min_seed_days REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            min_uploaded_bytes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            speed_op TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', speed_bytes REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            speed_window_min INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 60,
                            action_pause INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, action_tag TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                            action_remove_tag TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', notify INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1
                        );
                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notified_events (
                            rule_id INTEGER NOT NULL, hash_id TEXT NOT NULL, triggered_at REAL NOT NULL,
                            PRIMARY KEY (rule_id, hash_id), FOREIGN KEY (rule_id) REFERENCES rules(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
                        );
                    """)
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("PRAGMA table_info(rules)")
                    cols = {r['name'] for r in cur.fetchall()}
                    for col_name, col_def in [
                        ("time_mode", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'seeding_time'"),
                        ("min_uploaded_bytes", "INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0"),
                        ("speed_op", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''"),
                        ("speed_bytes", "REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0"),
                        ("speed_window_min", "INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 60"),
                        ("action_remove_tag", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''")
                    ]:
                        if col_name not in cols:
                            cur.execute(f"ALTER TABLE rules ADD COLUMN {col_name} {col_def}")
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Инициализация automation.db", e)

    def get_rules(self):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT * FROM rules ORDER BY id DESC")
                    return [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
            except Exception:
                return []

    def add_rule(self, name, min_ratio, min_seed_days, action_pause, action_tag, notify,
                 time_mode='seeding_time', min_uploaded_bytes=0, speed_op='', speed_bytes=0.0,
                 speed_window_min=60, action_remove_tag=''):
        t_mode = time_mode if time_mode in VALID_TIME_MODES else 'seeding_time'
        s_op = speed_op if speed_op in VALID_SPEED_OPS else ''
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute(
                        """INSERT INTO rules (name, enabled, time_mode, min_ratio, min_seed_days, min_uploaded_bytes,
                               speed_op, speed_bytes, speed_window_min, action_pause, action_tag, action_remove_tag, notify)
                           VALUES (?, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
                        (name, t_mode, float(min_ratio), float(min_seed_days), int(min_uploaded_bytes),
                         s_op, float(speed_bytes), max(1, int(speed_window_min)),
                         1 if action_pause else 0, action_tag.strip(), action_remove_tag.strip(), 1 if notify else 0)
                    )
                    conn.commit()
                    return cur.lastrowid
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка add_rule", e)
                return None

    def update_rule(self, rule_id, name, min_ratio, min_seed_days, action_pause, action_tag, notify,
                    time_mode='seeding_time', min_uploaded_bytes=0, speed_op='', speed_bytes=0.0,
                    speed_window_min=60, action_remove_tag=''):
        t_mode = time_mode if time_mode in VALID_TIME_MODES else 'seeding_time'
        s_op = speed_op if speed_op in VALID_SPEED_OPS else ''
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute(
                        """UPDATE rules SET name = ?, time_mode = ?, min_ratio = ?, min_seed_days = ?, min_uploaded_bytes = ?,
                               speed_op = ?, speed_bytes = ?, speed_window_min = ?,
                               action_pause = ?, action_tag = ?, action_remove_tag = ?, notify = ? WHERE id = ?""",
                        (name, t_mode, float(min_ratio), float(min_seed_days), int(min_uploaded_bytes),
                         s_op, float(speed_bytes), max(1, int(speed_window_min)),
                         1 if action_pause else 0, action_tag.strip(), action_remove_tag.strip(), 1 if notify else 0, int(rule_id))
                    )
                    updated = cur.rowcount > 0
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM notified_events WHERE rule_id = ?", (int(rule_id),))
                    conn.commit()
                    return updated
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка update_rule", e)
                return False

    def toggle_rule(self, rule_id):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute("UPDATE rules SET enabled = 1 - enabled WHERE id = ?", (int(rule_id),))
                    conn.commit()
                    return True
            except Exception:
                return False

    def delete_rule(self, rule_id):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute("DELETE FROM rules WHERE id = ?", (int(rule_id),))
                    conn.commit()
                    return True
            except Exception:
                return False

    def _is_triggered(self, rule_id, t_hash):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT 1 FROM notified_events WHERE rule_id = ? AND hash_id = ?", (rule_id, t_hash))
                    return cur.fetchone() is not None
            except Exception:
                return False

    def _mark_triggered(self, rule_id, t_hash):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_conn() as conn:
                    conn.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO notified_events (rule_id, hash_id, triggered_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", (rule_id, t_hash, time.time()))
                    conn.commit()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def test_notifications(self):
        return test_notifications()

    def run_system_tick(self):
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
        qb_up = bool(port and live)
        keep_awake.evaluate(live)
        system_alerts.check(qb_up, live=live)
        run_watchdog(qb_up)
        maybe_run_weekly_manifest()
        tick_snooze()
        flush_dnd_if_ready(notify_all)
        maybe_send_digest(notify_all)
        evaluate_achievements(force=False)
        if qb_up:
            evaluate_targets(live)
        if qb_up and time.time() >= cfg.getint('Autopilot_Frozen_Until', 0):
            tick_expirations(ip, port, live)
            run_smart_tagging(ip, port, live, get_all_torrents(use_cache=True))
        return ip, port, live

    def run_cycle(self):
        ip, port, live = self.run_system_tick()
        if not live:
            return 0

        now_ts = time.time()
        if now_ts < cfg.getint('Autopilot_Frozen_Until', 0):
            return 0

        run_auto_recheck(ip, port, live)
        rules = [r for r in self.get_rules() if r.get('enabled')]
        if not rules:
            return 0

        triggered_count = 0
        for r in rules:
            t_mode = r.get('time_mode') if r.get('time_mode') in VALID_TIME_MODES else 'seeding_time'
            min_r, min_d = float(r.get('min_ratio', 0) or 0), float(r.get('min_seed_days', 0) or 0)
            min_up = int(r.get('min_uploaded_bytes', 0) or 0)
            s_op, s_bytes = r.get('speed_op', '') or '', float(r.get('speed_bytes', 0) or 0)
            w_sec = max(60, int(r.get('speed_window_min', 60) or 60) * 60)
            use_speed = (s_op in ('>=', '<') and s_bytes > 0)
            if min_r <= 0 and min_d <= 0 and min_up <= 0 and not use_speed:
                continue

            for t_hash, info in live.items():
                ratio = float(info.get('ratio', 0) or 0)
                uploaded = int(info.get('uploaded', 0) or 0)
                time_sec = float(info.get(t_mode, 0) or 0)
                if (min_r > 0 and ratio < min_r) or (min_d > 0 and (time_sec / 86400.0) < min_d) or (min_up > 0 and uploaded < min_up):
                    continue

                v_avg = None
                if use_speed:
                    first, last, cnt = summary_tracker.db.get_torrent_window_bounds(t_hash, now_ts - w_sec, now_ts)
                    if cnt < 2 or not first or not last or (last[0] - first[0]) < (0.5 * w_sec):
                        continue
                    v_avg = max(0.0, (last[1] - first[1]) / (last[0] - first[0]))
                    if (s_op == '>=' and v_avg < s_bytes) or (s_op == '<' and v_avg >= s_bytes):
                        continue

                if self._is_triggered(r['id'], t_hash):
                    continue

                cur_tags = {tg.strip().lower() for tg in str(info.get('tags', '') or '').split(',') if tg.strip()}
                add_tag, rem_tag = str(r.get('action_tag', '') or '').strip(), str(r.get('action_remove_tag', '') or '').strip()
                want_pause = bool(r.get('action_pause'))
                actions_done = []
                if want_pause and send_qb_command(ip, port, 'stop', t_hash):
                    actions_done.append("⏸️ Поставлено на паузу")
                if add_tag and add_tag.lower() not in cur_tags and add_torrent_tags(ip, port, t_hash, add_tag):
                    actions_done.append(f"🏷️ +Тег: {add_tag}")
                if rem_tag and rem_tag.lower() in cur_tags and remove_torrent_tags(ip, port, t_hash, rem_tag):
                    actions_done.append(f"✂️ -Тег: {rem_tag}")

                if not want_pause and (add_tag or rem_tag) and not actions_done:
                    self._mark_triggered(r['id'], t_hash)
                    continue

                self._mark_triggered(r['id'], t_hash)
                triggered_count += 1
                t_name = info.get('name', t_hash)
                time_lbl, dur_fmt = ("Сид" if t_mode == 'seeding_time' else "Актив"), format_duration_ru(time_sec)
                write_log('automation', f"Правило '{r['name']}' -> '{t_name}' (Ratio: {ratio:.2f}, {time_lbl}: {dur_fmt})")
                app_state_db.add_audit_event('automation', f"Правило: {r['name']}", t_name)
                if r.get('notify'):
                    stat_line = f"Отдано: {format_file_size(uploaded)} | Ratio: {ratio:.2f}x | {time_lbl}: {dur_fmt}"
                    if v_avg is not None:
                        stat_line += f" | Ср. скор.: {format_file_size(v_avg)}/s"
                    msg_lines = [f"Раздача: {t_name}", stat_line] + (["Действия: " + ", ".join(actions_done)] if actions_done else [])
                    notify_all(f"🤖 Сработало правило: {r['name']}", msg_lines)

        return triggered_count

    def start(self):
        if self._running:
            return
        self._running = True

        def loop():
            time.sleep(8)
            last_auto_ts = 0.0
            while self._running:
                now = time.time()
                try:
                    auto_due = cfg.getboolean('Auto_Enabled', False) and (now - last_auto_ts) >= max(1, cfg.getint('Auto_Interval_Min', 5)) * 60
                    if auto_due:
                        self.run_cycle()
                        last_auto_ts = time.time()
                    else:
                        self.run_system_tick()
                except Exception as e:
                    write_error_log("AutomationEngine loop", e)
                for _ in range(30):
                    if not self._running:
                        return
                    time.sleep(1)

        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=loop, daemon=True)
        self._thread.start()


automation_engine = AutomationEngine()

# core/autostart.py
import os
import sys


def get_exe_or_script_command():
    if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        return f'"{sys.executable}"'
    else:
        main_script = os.path.abspath(sys.argv[0])
        return f'"{sys.executable}" "{main_script}"'


def set_autostart(enable=True):
    if os.name != 'nt':
        return False, "Автозагрузка через реестр доступна только на Windows"
    try:
        import winreg
        key = winreg.OpenKey(
            winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run",
            0, winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE | winreg.KEY_QUERY_VALUE
        )
        app_name = "tbr_swiss_knife"
        if enable:
            cmd = get_exe_or_script_command()
            winreg.SetValueEx(key, app_name, 0, winreg.REG_SZ, cmd)
        else:
            try:
                winreg.DeleteValue(key, app_name)
            except FileNotFoundError:
                pass
        winreg.CloseKey(key)
        return True, "Автозагрузка включена" if enable else "Автозагрузка отключена"
    except Exception as e:
        return False, str(e)


def is_autostart_enabled():
    if os.name != 'nt':
        return False
    try:
        import winreg
        key = winreg.OpenKey(
            winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
            r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run",
            0, winreg.KEY_READ
        )
        try:
            val, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(key, "tbr_swiss_knife")
            winreg.CloseKey(key)
            return bool(val)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            winreg.CloseKey(key)
            return False
    except Exception:
        return False

# core/backup_manager.py
import os
import io
import shutil
import sqlite3
import zipfile
import tempfile
import configparser
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR, LOG_FOLDER
from utils.config import CONFIG_FILE

DB_TARGETS = [
    ('profiles.db', os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, 'profiles.db')),
    ('automation.db', os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, 'automation.db')),
    ('app_state.db', os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, 'app_state.db')),
    ('summary_history.db', os.path.join(LOG_FOLDER, 'auto_hourly', 'summary_history.db')),
]
PENDING_DIR = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, '_restore_pending')
SECRET_KEYS = ('Web_Password', 'WebUI_Password', 'TG_Bot_Token', 'Discord_Webhook')


def apply_pending_restore_if_any():
    """Вызывается при старте main.py ДО открытия любых БД."""
    if not os.path.isdir(PENDING_DIR):
        return
    try:
        for arc_name, dest_path in DB_TARGETS:
            src = os.path.join(PENDING_DIR, arc_name)
            if os.path.isfile(src):
                os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dest_path), exist_ok=True)
                for ext in ('-wal', '-shm'):
                    if os.path.exists(dest_path + ext):
                        try:
                            os.remove(dest_path + ext)
                        except OSError:
                            pass
                shutil.move(src, dest_path)
        cfg_src = os.path.join(PENDING_DIR, 'config.ini')
        if os.path.isfile(cfg_src):
            shutil.move(cfg_src, CONFIG_FILE)
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(PENDING_DIR, ignore_errors=True)


def create_backup_zip(exclude_history=False, strip_secrets=False):
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(buf, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
            for arc_name, src_path in DB_TARGETS:
                if exclude_history and arc_name == 'summary_history.db':
                    continue
                if not os.path.isfile(src_path):
                    continue
                dst_tmp = os.path.join(tmpdir, arc_name)
                src_conn = sqlite3.connect(src_path, timeout=30.0)
                dst_conn = sqlite3.connect(dst_tmp)
                try:
                    src_conn.backup(dst_conn)
                finally:
                    dst_conn.close()
                    src_conn.close()
                zf.write(dst_tmp, arcname=arc_name)

            if os.path.isfile(CONFIG_FILE):
                if strip_secrets:
                    cp = configparser.ConfigParser()
                    cp.read(CONFIG_FILE, encoding='utf-8')
                    if 'Settings' in cp:
                        for k in SECRET_KEYS:
                            if k in cp['Settings']:
                                cp['Settings'][k] = ''
                    s_buf = io.StringIO()
                    cp.write(s_buf)
                    zf.writestr('config.ini', s_buf.getvalue().encode('utf-8'))
                else:
                    zf.write(CONFIG_FILE, arcname='config.ini')

    return buf.getvalue()


def stage_restore_zip(file_bytes):
    allowed = {name for name, _ in DB_TARGETS} | {'config.ini'}
    os.makedirs(PENDING_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    extracted = []
    try:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(file_bytes), 'r') as zf:
            for info in zf.infolist():
                base = os.path.basename(info.filename)
                if not base or base not in allowed or info.file_size > 512 * 1024 * 1024:
                    continue
                data = zf.read(info.filename)
                with open(os.path.join(PENDING_DIR, base), 'wb') as out:
                    out.write(data)
                extracted.append(base)
        if not extracted:
            shutil.rmtree(PENDING_DIR, ignore_errors=True)
            return False, "В архиве не найдено ни одного файла tbr PRO (.db / config.ini)"
        return True, f"Подготовлено к восстановлению ({', '.join(extracted)}). Перезапустите tbr PRO для применения."
    except zipfile.BadZipFile:
        return False, "Некорректный ZIP-архив"
    except Exception as e:
        return False, str(e)

# core/disaster_recovery.py
import os
import json
import time
import datetime
import urllib.parse
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR, format_file_size, write_log, write_error_log
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.notifiers import notify_all
from core.torrent_parser import get_all_torrents
from core.qb_api import (
    detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, send_qb_command,
    set_global_speed_limits, shutdown_qb_webui, close_qbittorrent
)

MANIFESTS_DIR = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, "manifests")
os.makedirs(MANIFESTS_DIR, exist_ok=True)


def execute_panic_killswitch(kill_qb=False, actor="admin"):
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    details = []

    if port:
        if send_qb_command(ip, port, "stop", "all"):
            details.append("Все раздачи поставлены на паузу")
        if set_global_speed_limits(ip, port, 1, 1):
            details.append("Глобальный лимит скорости зажат до 1 B/s")

    frozen_until = int(time.time() + 3600)
    cfg.set("Autopilot_Frozen_Until", str(frozen_until))
    cfg.save()
    details.append("Автопилот заморожен на 1 час")

    if kill_qb:
        if port:
            shutdown_qb_webui(ip, port)
        if os.name == "nt":
            close_qbittorrent()
        details.append("Отправлена команда завершения qBittorrent")

    summary = "; ".join(details)
    write_log("app", f"PANIC KILLSWITCH ({actor}): {summary}")
    app_state_db.add_audit_event("system", "Panic Killswitch", summary, actor=actor)
    notify_all("🚨 PANIC KILLSWITCH АКТИВИРОВАН", details, critical=True)

    return {"success": True, "details": details, "frozen_until": frozen_until}


def _build_magnet(t_hash, name, trackers):
    params = [("xt", f"urn:btih:{t_hash}"), ("dn", name)]
    for tr in trackers:
        if tr:
            params.append(("tr", tr))
    return "magnet:?" + "&".join(f"{k}={urllib.parse.quote(str(v), safe=':')}" for k, v in params)


def _prune_old_manifests(max_keep=2):
    try:
        files = [
            os.path.join(MANIFESTS_DIR, f)
            for f in os.listdir(MANIFESTS_DIR)
            if f.startswith("tbr_manifest_") and f.endswith(".json")
        ]
        files.sort(key=lambda p: os.path.getmtime(p), reverse=True)
        for old_path in files[max_keep:]:
            try:
                os.remove(old_path)
            except OSError:
                pass
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("Ротация аварийных манифестов", e)


def create_disaster_manifest(actor="admin"):
    from core.version import APP_VERSION
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    parsed = get_all_torrents(use_cache=True)

    items = []
    seen = set()
    for h, st in parsed.items():
        seen.add(h.lower())
        lv = live.get(h) or live.get(h.lower()) or {}
        name = str(lv.get("name") or st.get("name") or h)
        size = int(lv.get("total_size") or st.get("size") or 0)
        trackers = list(st.get("trackers") or [])
        tr_live = str(lv.get("tracker") or "").strip()
        if tr_live and tr_live not in trackers:
            trackers.insert(0, tr_live)
        items.append({
            "name": name,
            "hash": h.lower(),
            "size": size,
            "size_fmt": format_file_size(size),
            "trackers": trackers,
            "magnet": _build_magnet(h.lower(), name, trackers),
        })

    for h, lv in live.items():
        hl = h.lower()
        if hl in seen:
            continue
        name = str(lv.get("name") or hl)
        size = int(lv.get("total_size") or 0)
        tr_live = str(lv.get("tracker") or "").strip()
        trackers = [tr_live] if tr_live else []
        items.append({
            "name": name,
            "hash": hl,
            "size": size,
            "size_fmt": format_file_size(size),
            "trackers": trackers,
            "magnet": _build_magnet(hl, name, trackers),
        })

    items.sort(key=lambda x: x["name"].lower())
    ts_str = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    fname = f"tbr_manifest_{ts_str}.json"
    fpath = os.path.join(MANIFESTS_DIR, fname)

    payload = {
        "generated_at": datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        "app_version": APP_VERSION,
        "count": len(items),
        "torrents": items,
    }
    with open(fpath, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(payload, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    _prune_old_manifests(max_keep=2)
    cfg.set("Manifest_Last_Run", str(int(time.time())))
    cfg.save()
    app_state_db.add_audit_event("system", "Аварийный манифест", f"Создан {fname} ({len(items)} раздач)", actor=actor)
    return {"filename": fname, "count": len(items), "size": os.path.getsize(fpath)}


def list_manifests():
    res = []
    try:
        for f in os.listdir(MANIFESTS_DIR):
            if f.startswith("tbr_manifest_") and f.endswith(".json"):
                p = os.path.join(MANIFESTS_DIR, f)
                res.append({
                    "filename": f,
                    "size_fmt": format_file_size(os.path.getsize(p)),
                    "mtime": int(os.path.getmtime(p)),
                    "date": datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(os.path.getmtime(p)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                })
        res.sort(key=lambda x: x["mtime"], reverse=True)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return res[:2]


def get_manifest_path(filename):
    clean = os.path.basename(str(filename or "").replace("\\", "/"))
    if not clean or not clean.startswith("tbr_manifest_") or not clean.endswith(".json"):
        return None
    base = os.path.abspath(MANIFESTS_DIR)
    full = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(base, clean))
    if os.path.commonpath([base, full]) == base and os.path.isfile(full):
        return full
    return None


def maybe_run_weekly_manifest():
    if not cfg.getboolean("Manifest_Weekly", False):
        return
    last_ts = cfg.getint("Manifest_Last_Run", 0)
    if (time.time() - last_ts) >= 7 * 86400:
        try:
            create_disaster_manifest(actor="scheduler")
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("Еженедельный манифест", e)

================================================================================

# core/dnd_digest.py
import json
import time
import shutil
import datetime
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import BT_BACKUP_PATH, APP_DATA_DIR, format_file_size, write_error_log
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.torrent_parser import get_all_torrents
from core.scheduler import MIN_DELTA_SEC


def _parse_hhmm(val, default_min):
    try:
        parts = str(val or "").strip().split(":")
        h, m = int(parts[0]), int(parts[1])
        if 0 <= h <= 23 and 0 <= m <= 59:
            return h * 60 + m
    except Exception:
        pass
    return default_min


def get_dnd_settings():
    raw_days = str(cfg.get("DND_Days", "0,1,2,3,4,5,6"))
    days = sorted({int(x) for x in raw_days.split(",") if x.strip().isdigit() and 0 <= int(x) <= 6})
    return {
        "enabled": cfg.getboolean("DND_Enabled", False),
        "start": cfg.get("DND_Start", "23:00") or "23:00",
        "end": cfg.get("DND_End", "08:00") or "08:00",
        "days": days,
    }


def is_dnd_active_now(now_dt=None):
    st = get_dnd_settings()
    if not st["enabled"] or not st["days"]:
        return False

    dt = now_dt or datetime.datetime.now()
    cur_min = dt.hour * 60 + dt.minute
    start_min = _parse_hhmm(st["start"], 23 * 60)
    end_min = _parse_hhmm(st["end"], 8 * 60)
    wd = dt.weekday()

    if start_min == end_min:
        return False
    if start_min < end_min:
        return (wd in st["days"]) and (start_min <= cur_min < end_min)
    # Ночной переход через полночь (например 23:00 -> 08:00)
    if cur_min >= start_min:
        return wd in st["days"]
    if cur_min < end_min:
        prev_wd = (wd - 1) % 7
        return prev_wd in st["days"]
    return False


def queue_dnd_message(title, lines):
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO dnd_queue (title, lines_json, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                    (str(title), json.dumps(list(lines or []), ensure_ascii=False), time.time())
                )
                conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("queue_dnd_message", e)


def flush_dnd_if_ready(send_fn):
    if is_dnd_active_now():
        return 0
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM dnd_queue ORDER BY id ASC").fetchall()
                if not rows:
                    return 0
                conn.execute("DELETE FROM dnd_queue")
                conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            return 0

    lines = []
    for r in rows[:25]:
        ts_str = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(r["created_at"]).strftime("%H:%M")
        try:
            sub = json.loads(r["lines_json"] or "[]")
            preview = sub[0] if sub else ""
        except Exception:
            preview = ""
        lines.append(f"[{ts_str}] {r['title']}" + (f" — {preview}" if preview else ""))
    if len(rows) > 25:
        lines.append(f"...и ещё событий: {len(rows) - 25}")

    send_fn(f"🌙 Итоги «тихого часа» ({len(rows)} событий)", lines, critical=True)
    return len(rows)


def _sum_positive_delta(since_ts, until_ts):
    rows = summary_tracker.db.get_snapshots_since(since_ts)
    total = 0
    prev = None
    for r in rows:
        ts = float(r["timestamp"])
        if ts > until_ts:
            break
        if prev is not None:
            dt = ts - float(prev["timestamp"])
            d = max(0, int(r["total_uploaded"]) - int(prev["total_uploaded"]))
            if dt >= MIN_DELTA_SEC and d > 0:
                total += d
        prev = r
    return total


def _free_disk_fmt():
    for p in (BT_BACKUP_PATH, APP_DATA_DIR):
        try:
            if p and shutil.disk_usage(p).free >= 0:
                return format_file_size(shutil.disk_usage(p).free)
        except Exception:
            continue
    return "—"


def get_digest_settings():
    return {
        "mode": cfg.get("Digest_Mode", "off"),
        "hour": max(0, min(23, cfg.getint("Digest_Hour", 21))),
        "weekday": max(0, min(6, cfg.getint("Digest_Weekday", 6))),
    }


def _last_scheduled_ts(now_dt, mode, target_hour, target_wd):
    cand = now_dt.replace(hour=target_hour, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
    if mode == "daily":
        if cand > now_dt:
            cand -= datetime.timedelta(days=1)
        return cand.timestamp()
    # weekly
    days_back = (now_dt.weekday() - target_wd) % 7
    cand = (now_dt - datetime.timedelta(days=days_back)).replace(hour=target_hour, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
    if cand > now_dt:
        cand -= datetime.timedelta(days=7)
    return cand.timestamp()


def maybe_send_digest(send_fn):
    st = get_digest_settings()
    mode = st["mode"]
    if mode not in ("daily", "weekly"):
        return False

    now_dt = datetime.datetime.now()
    now_ts = now_dt.timestamp()
    sched_ts = _last_scheduled_ts(now_dt, mode, st["hour"], st["weekday"])
    last_sent = float(cfg.getint("Digest_Last_Sent_Ts", 0) or 0)

    if last_sent == 0:
        cfg.set("Digest_Last_Sent_Ts", str(int(now_ts)))
        cfg.save()
        return False

    if last_sent >= sched_ts:
        return False

    period_sec = 7 * 86400 if mode == "weekly" else 86400
    delayed_sec = now_ts - sched_ts
    since_ts = max(last_sent, now_ts - (30 * 86400)) if delayed_sec > 3600 else (now_ts - period_sec)
    span_sec = max(3600.0, now_ts - since_ts)

    cur_vol = _sum_positive_delta(since_ts, now_ts)
    prev_vol = _sum_positive_delta(since_ts - span_sec, since_ts)
    if prev_vol > 0:
        diff_pct = ((cur_vol - prev_vol) / prev_vol) * 100.0
        pct_str = f" ({'+' if diff_pct >= 0 else ''}{diff_pct:.1f}% к прошл. периоду)"
    else:
        pct_str = ""

    torrents = get_all_torrents(use_cache=True)
    deltas = summary_tracker.db.get_torrents_positive_deltas(since_ts)
    top3 = sorted(deltas.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:3]

    span_hours = int(round(span_sec / 3600.0))
    lbl = "Недельный дайджест" if mode == "weekly" else "Дневной дайджест"
    lines = [
        f"⏱️ Интервал: {span_hours} ч" + (" (дослан после простоя)" if delayed_sec > 3600 else ""),
        f"📤 Отдано: {format_file_size(cur_vol)}{pct_str}",
        f"💾 Свободно на диске: {_free_disk_fmt()}",
    ]
    if top3:
        lines.append("🏆 Топ-3 раздачи:")
        for idx, (h, val) in enumerate(top3, 1):
            nm = (torrents.get(h) or {}).get("name") or h[:10]
            lines.append(f"  {idx}. {nm[:36]} — {format_file_size(val)}")

    cfg.set("Digest_Last_Sent_Ts", str(int(now_ts)))
    cfg.save()
    send_fn(f"📊 tbr PRO — {lbl}", lines, critical=False)
    app_state_db.add_audit_event("automation", lbl, f"Отдано {format_file_size(cur_vol)}")
    return True

================================================================================
FILE: core/fastresume_merger.py
