# core/fastresume_merger.py
import os
import time
import shutil
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import BT_BACKUP_PATH, write_error_log, write_log
from utils.bencode import decode_bencode, encode_bencode
from core.qb_api import (
    detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, delete_torrents,
    is_qb_running, close_qbittorrent, launch_qbittorrent, get_qb_executable_path
)
from core.torrent_parser import force_clear_cache
from core.torrent_targets import transfer_target_on_merge

VALID_DELETE_MODES = ('none', 'safe', 'force')
STAT_FIELDS = ('total_uploaded', 'total_downloaded', 'active_time', 'seeding_time', 'added_time', 'completed_time')


def _find_fastresume_file(t_hash):
    if not os.path.exists(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        return None
    target = t_hash.lower() + '.fastresume'
    for f in os.listdir(BT_BACKUP_PATH):
        if f.lower() == target:
            return os.path.join(BT_BACKUP_PATH, f)
    return None


def _get_bencode_val(data, key):
    if key in data:
        return data[key]
    return data.get(key.encode('ascii'))


def _norm_path(p):
    if not p or not str(p).strip():
        return ''
    return os.path.normcase(os.path.normpath(os.path.abspath(str(p).strip())))


def paths_overlap(path_a, path_b):
    na = _norm_path(path_a)
    nb = _norm_path(path_b)
    if not na or not nb:
        return True
    if na == nb:
        return True
    try:
        common = os.path.commonpath([na, nb])
        return common == na or common == nb
    except ValueError:
        return False


def _wait_for_target_live(ip, port, new_hash, timeout_sec=12.0):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout_sec
    nh = new_hash.lower()
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port)
        if live:
            for k, v in live.items():
                if k.lower() == nh:
                    return live, v
        time.sleep(1.2)
    return {}, None


def _evaluate_delete_plan(old_hash, new_hash, delete_mode, expected_up, live_before):
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live_after, new_live = _wait_for_target_live(ip, port, new_hash, timeout_sec=12.0) if port else ({}, None)
    oh = old_hash.lower()

    if new_live is not None:
        new_up = int(new_live.get('uploaded', 0) or 0)
        if expected_up > 0 and new_up < expected_up:
            return None, f"⚠️ WebUI: статистика получателя не подтверждена ({new_up} < {expected_up}). Удаление заблокировано."

        old_live = next((v for k, v in live_after.items() if k.lower() == oh), None) or \
                   next((v for k, v in live_before.items() if k.lower() == oh), {})
        src_path = str(old_live.get('content_path') or old_live.get('save_path') or '')
        dst_path = str(new_live.get('content_path') or new_live.get('save_path') or '')

        if delete_mode == 'safe':
            overlap = paths_overlap(src_path, dst_path)
            delete_files = not overlap
            downgraded = overlap
        else:
            delete_files = True
            downgraded = False

        return {
            'method': 'webui',
            'old_hash': old_hash,
            'new_hash': new_hash,
            'old_name': old_live.get('name', old_hash),
            'delete_mode': delete_mode,
            'delete_files': delete_files,
            'downgraded': downgraded,
            'src_path': src_path,
            'dst_path': dst_path
        }, None

    new_file = _find_fastresume_file(new_hash)
    if new_file and os.path.exists(new_file):
        with open(new_file, 'rb') as f:
            disk_data = decode_bencode(f.read()) or {}
        disk_up = int(_get_bencode_val(disk_data, 'total_uploaded') or 0)
        if disk_up >= expected_up:
            return {
                'method': 'disk_only',
                'old_hash': old_hash,
                'new_hash': new_hash,
                'old_name': old_hash,
                'delete_mode': delete_mode,
                'delete_files': False,
                'downgraded': True,
                'src_path': '',
                'dst_path': ''
            }, None

    return None, "⚠️ WebUI недоступен и диск-проверка не пройдена — удаление заблокировано."


def run_fastresume_merge(old_hash, new_hash, delete_mode='none'):
    mode = delete_mode if delete_mode in VALID_DELETE_MODES else 'none'
    log = []

    old_file = _find_fastresume_file(old_hash)
    new_file = _find_fastresume_file(new_hash)
    if not old_file or not os.path.exists(old_file):
        return False, [f'❌ Файл {old_hash}.fastresume не найден!'], None, 404
    if not new_file or not os.path.exists(new_file):
        return False, [f'❌ Файл {new_hash}.fastresume не найден!'], None, 404

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live_before = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if (mode != 'none' and port) else {}
    exe_path = get_qb_executable_path()
    was_running = is_qb_running()
    expected_up = 0

    try:
        if was_running:
            log.append("🛑 Завершаем qBittorrent во избежание порчи данных...")
            if not close_qbittorrent():
                raise RuntimeError("Не удалось завершить qBittorrent (возможно, клиент запущен от имени Администратора).")
            time.sleep(1.0)

        with open(old_file, 'rb') as f:
            old_data = decode_bencode(f.read())
        with open(new_file, 'rb') as f:
            new_data = decode_bencode(f.read())

        if not isinstance(old_data, dict) or not isinstance(new_data, dict):
            raise ValueError("Повреждённый формат .fastresume (ожидался словарь bencode).")

        copied = 0
        for fld in STAT_FIELDS:
            fld_b = fld.encode('ascii')
            val = _get_bencode_val(old_data, fld)
            if val is not None:
                if fld == 'total_uploaded':
                    expected_up = int(val)
                if fld_b in new_data or any(isinstance(k, bytes) for k in new_data.keys()):
                    new_data[fld_b] = val
                    new_data.pop(fld, None)
                else:
                    new_data[fld] = val
                    new_data.pop(fld_b, None)
                log.append(f"✅ Скопировано: {fld} = {val}")
                copied += 1

        if copied == 0:
            raise ValueError("Не найдено подходящих полей статистики для копирования!")

        encoded_bytes = encode_bencode(new_data)
        validated = decode_bencode(encoded_bytes)
        if not isinstance(validated, dict) or len(validated) != len(new_data):
            raise ValueError("Контрольная проверка нового bencode не пройдена!")
        log.append("🔍 Валидация нового bencode пройдена.")

        bak = new_file + '.bak'
        if not os.path.exists(bak):
            shutil.copy2(new_file, bak)
            log.append(f"📦 Бэкап создан: {os.path.basename(bak)}")

        tmp_file = new_file + '.tmp'
        try:
            with open(tmp_file, 'wb') as f:
                f.write(encoded_bytes)
                f.flush()
                os.fsync(f.fileno())
            os.replace(tmp_file, new_file)
        finally:
            if os.path.exists(tmp_file):
                try:
                    os.remove(tmp_file)
                except OSError:
                    pass

        force_clear_cache()
        if transfer_target_on_merge(old_hash, new_hash):
            log.append("🎯 Целевая планка (Goal) автоматически перенесена на новый хэш.")
        log.append("🎉 Статистика успешно и атомарно записана в .fastresume!")
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("Ошибка merger API", e)
        log.append(f"❌ Ошибка: {e}")
        if was_running and not is_qb_running():
            log.append("🚀 Перезапуск qBittorrent...")
            launch_qbittorrent(exe_path)
        return False, log, None, 500

    if was_running and not is_qb_running():
        log.append("🚀 Перезапуск qBittorrent...")
        launch_qbittorrent(exe_path)

    confirm_info = None
    if mode != 'none':
        log.append("🔎 Проверка подхвата получателя и пересечения путей...")
        confirm_info, warn = _evaluate_delete_plan(old_hash, new_hash, mode, expected_up, live_before)
        if warn:
            log.append(warn)
        elif confirm_info:
            if confirm_info['method'] == 'disk_only':
                log.append("ℹ️ WebUI недоступен: удаление файлов на диске заблокировано (доступно только удаление метаданных из BT_backup).")
            elif confirm_info['downgraded']:
                log.append("🛡️ Пути совпадают или вложены — режим понижен до удаления только торрента (deleteFiles=false).")
            else:
                log.append("⚡ Проверка пройдена. Ожидание подтверждения удаления исходника...")

    return True, log, confirm_info, 200


def execute_source_deletion(old_hash, new_hash, delete_mode):
    mode = delete_mode if delete_mode in ('safe', 'force') else 'safe'
    old_file = _find_fastresume_file(old_hash)
    new_file = _find_fastresume_file(new_hash)
    if not new_file or not os.path.exists(new_file):
        return False, ["❌ Получатель не найден на диске, удаление отменено."]

    with open(new_file, 'rb') as f:
        new_disk = decode_bencode(f.read()) or {}
    expected_up = int(_get_bencode_val(new_disk, 'total_uploaded') or 0)

    plan, err = _evaluate_delete_plan(old_hash, new_hash, mode, expected_up, {})
    if err or not plan:
        return False, [err or "❌ Не удалось подтвердить состояние получателя."]

    if plan['method'] == 'webui':
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        ok = delete_torrents(ip, port, old_hash, delete_files=plan['delete_files'])
        force_clear_cache()
        if ok:
            act = "с файлами на диске (deleteFiles=true)" if plan['delete_files'] else "без удаления файлов (deleteFiles=false)"
            write_log('merger', f"Удалён исходник {old_hash} ({act}) после мерджа в {new_hash}")
            return True, [f"🗑️ Исходник удалён через WebUI {act}."]
        return False, ["❌ WebUI отклонил удаление исходника."]

    removed = []
    if old_file and os.path.exists(old_file):
        os.remove(old_file)
        removed.append(os.path.basename(old_file))
    for ext in ('.torrent', '.fastresume.bak'):
        cand = os.path.join(BT_BACKUP_PATH, old_hash.lower() + ext)
        if os.path.exists(cand):
            os.remove(cand)
            removed.append(os.path.basename(cand))
    force_clear_cache()
    write_log('merger', f"WebUI недоступен; удалены метаданные исходника {old_hash}: {removed}")
    return True, [f"🗑️ WebUI недоступен. Удалены только метаданные из BT_backup: {', '.join(removed) or 'ничего'}."]

================================================================================
FILE: core/forecaster.py

# core/forecaster.py
import os
import time
import csv
import datetime
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size, parse_bytes, parse_speed, sanitize_csv_cell, write_error_log


def fmt_ratio_h(val):
    if val is None or val <= 0:
        return "0.000 /ч"
    if val < 0.001:
        return f"{val:.4f} /ч"
    if val < 1.0:
        return f"{val:.3f} /ч"
    return f"{val:.2f} /ч"


def fmt_speed(bps):
    if bps is None or bps <= 0:
        return "0 B/s"
    return f"{format_file_size(bps)}/s"


def calculate_smart_forecast(db, target_type, target_hash, target_str, speed_str, summaries):
    try:
        is_seedbox = (target_type == 'all' or not target_hash)
        t_hash_lower = (target_hash or '').lower()

        if is_seedbox:
            current_up = sum(s.get('uploaded', 0) for s in summaries)
            total_sz = sum(s.get('size', 0) for s in summaries)
            name = "🌐 Весь сидбокс (суммарно)"
            target_bytes = parse_bytes(target_str, torrent_size=total_sz)
        else:
            t_item = next(
                (s for s in summaries if (str(s.get('hash_id', '')).lower() == t_hash_lower or
                                         str(s.get('hash', '')).lower() == t_hash_lower)),
                None
            )
            if not t_item:
                return {'success': False, 'error': "Выбранная раздача не найдена!"}
            current_up = t_item.get('uploaded', 0)
            total_sz = t_item.get('size', 0)
            name = t_item.get('name', target_hash)
            target_bytes = parse_bytes(target_str, torrent_size=total_sz)

        remaining_bytes = max(0, target_bytes - current_up)
        if remaining_bytes == 0:
            return {
                'success': True, 'achieved': True, 'name': name,
                'current_up_fmt': format_file_size(current_up),
                'target_fmt': format_file_size(target_bytes),
                'message': "Цель уже достигнута!"
            }

        is_manual = bool(speed_str and speed_str.strip().lower() not in ('авто', 'auto', ''))
        manual_speed = 0
        if is_manual:
            manual_speed = parse_speed(speed_str)
            if manual_speed <= 0:
                return {'success': False, 'error': "Скорость должна быть больше 0!"}

        raw_w1 = max(0, cfg.getint('Weight_W1', 70))
        raw_w2 = max(0, cfg.getint('Weight_W2', 30))
        raw_w3 = max(0, cfg.getint('Weight_W3', 0))
        total_w = raw_w1 + raw_w2 + raw_w3

        if not is_manual and total_w <= 0:
            return {'success': False, 'error': "Все веса расчёта равны 0! Задайте их в ⚙️ Настройки."}

        nw1 = raw_w1 / total_w if total_w > 0 else 0.0
        nw2 = raw_w2 / total_w if total_w > 0 else 0.0
        nw3 = raw_w3 / total_w if total_w > 0 else 0.0

        req_days = 21 if raw_w3 > 0 else (14 if raw_w2 > 0 else 7)
        now_ts = time.time()

        cnt, min_ts = db.get_snapshot_range_info()
        days_available = (now_ts - min_ts) / 86400.0 if (cnt and min_ts) else 0.0

        if not is_manual and (cnt < 2 or days_available < (req_days - 0.15)):
            return {
                'success': False, 'need_manual': True,
                'days_available': round(days_available, 1),
                'req_days': req_days,
                'error': f"Недостаточно данных (накоплено {days_available:.1f} из {req_days} дн.). Укажите скорость вручную."
            }

        forecast_24h_rows = []
        projected_24h_volume = 0

        for i in range(24):
            h_start = now_ts + (i * 3600)
            h_end = h_start + 3600
            s_dt = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(h_start)
            e_dt = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(h_end)

            if is_manual:
                vol_hour = int(manual_speed * 3600)
                speed_hour = manual_speed
                basis_text = "Ручной ввод скорости"
            else:
                d1 = db.get_hist_delta(h_start - (168 * 3600), h_end - (168 * 3600), is_seedbox, t_hash_lower) if nw1 > 0 else 0
                d2 = db.get_hist_delta(h_start - (336 * 3600), h_end - (336 * 3600), is_seedbox, t_hash_lower) if nw2 > 0 else 0
                d3 = db.get_hist_delta(h_start - (504 * 3600), h_end - (504 * 3600), is_seedbox, t_hash_lower) if nw3 > 0 else 0
                vol_hour = int((nw1 * d1) + (nw2 * d2) + (nw3 * d3))
                speed_hour = vol_hour / 3600.0
                parts = []
                if nw1 > 0: parts.append(f"1нед: {d1/(1024**2):.1f}МБ ({nw1*100:.0f}%)")
                if nw2 > 0: parts.append(f"2нед: {d2/(1024**2):.1f}МБ ({nw2*100:.0f}%)")
                if nw3 > 0: parts.append(f"3нед: {d3/(1024**2):.1f}МБ ({nw3*100:.0f}%)")
                basis_text = " + ".join(parts)

            projected_24h_volume += vol_hour
            forecast_24h_rows.append({
                'date': s_dt.strftime('%d.%m'),
                'hour': f"{s_dt.strftime('%H:00')} – {e_dt.strftime('%H:00')}",
                'volume_bytes': vol_hour,
                'volume_mb': f"{vol_hour / (1024 * 1024):.2f} МБ",
                'speed_fmt': fmt_speed(speed_hour),
                'basis': basis_text
            })

        max_seconds = 3650 * 86400
        eta_dt = None
        eta_str = ""

        if is_manual:
            seconds_needed = int(remaining_bytes / manual_speed) if manual_speed > 0 else max_seconds + 1
            if seconds_needed <= max_seconds:
                eta_dt = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(now_ts + seconds_needed)
            else:
                eta_str = f"Более 10 лет (~{seconds_needed // (365*86400)} лет)"
        else:
            simulated_ts = now_ts
            simulated_rem = remaining_bytes
            idx = 0
            while simulated_rem > 0 and (simulated_ts - now_ts) < (365 * 86400):
                hour_vol = forecast_24h_rows[idx % 24]['volume_bytes']
                if hour_vol <= 0:
                    hour_vol = max(1, int(projected_24h_volume / 24)) if projected_24h_volume > 0 else 1024
                if simulated_rem <= hour_vol:
                    simulated_ts += int((simulated_rem / hour_vol) * 3600)
                    simulated_rem = 0
                    break
                simulated_rem -= hour_vol
                simulated_ts += 3600
                idx += 1
            if simulated_rem == 0:
                eta_dt = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(simulated_ts)
            else:
                eta_str = "Более 1 года при текущем темпе"

        if eta_dt:
            eta_str = eta_dt.strftime('%A, %d.%m.%Y в %H:%M')
            ru_days = {'Monday': 'Понедельник', 'Tuesday': 'Вторник', 'Wednesday': 'Среда',
                       'Thursday': 'Четверг', 'Friday': 'Пятница', 'Saturday': 'Суббота', 'Sunday': 'Воскресенье'}
            for eng, ru in ru_days.items():
                eta_str = eta_str.replace(eng, ru)

        weights_desc = f"{nw1*100:.0f}% / {nw2*100:.0f}% / {nw3*100:.0f}% ({req_days} дн.)"

        return {
            'success': True, 'achieved': False, 'name': name,
            'target_type': target_type, 'is_seedbox': is_seedbox, 'is_manual': is_manual,
            'weights_desc': weights_desc,
            'current_up_fmt': format_file_size(current_up),
            'target_fmt': format_file_size(target_bytes),
            'remaining_fmt': format_file_size(remaining_bytes),
            'forecast_24h_vol_fmt': format_file_size(projected_24h_volume),
            'ratio_current': round(current_up / total_sz, 2) if total_sz > 0 else 0.0,
            'ratio_after_24h': round((current_up + projected_24h_volume) / total_sz, 2) if total_sz > 0 else 0.0,
            'eta_str': eta_str,
            'forecast_rows': forecast_24h_rows
        }
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("SmartCalc Error", e)
        return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}


def export_forecast_csv(manual_folder, forecast_data):
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
    raw_name = str(forecast_data.get('name', 'forecast'))
    clean_name = "".join(c for c in raw_name if c.isalnum() or c in (' ', '_', '-')).strip()[:30] or 'forecast'
    filename = f"forecast_24h_{clean_name}_{ts}.csv"
    filepath = os.path.join(manual_folder, filename)

    lines = [
        f"# === ПОЧАСОВОЙ ПРОГНОЗ ОТДАЧИ НА 24 ЧАСА ===",
        f"# Объект: {sanitize_csv_cell(forecast_data.get('name', ''))}",
        f"# Дата расчета: {datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}",
        f"# Цель: {forecast_data.get('target_fmt', '')} | Осталось: {forecast_data.get('remaining_fmt', '')}",
        f"# Ожидаемая отдача за следующие 24ч: {forecast_data.get('forecast_24h_vol_fmt', '')}",
        f"# Ожидаемое время достижения цели: {forecast_data.get('eta_str', '')}",
        f"# =========================================="
    ]

    try:
        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8-sig', newline='') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(lines) + '\n')
            w = csv.writer(f, delimiter=';')
            w.writerow(['Дата', 'Интервал (час)', 'Ожидаемый объём (МБ)', 'Ожидаемая скорость', 'Обоснование расчёта'])
            for r in forecast_data.get('forecast_rows', [])[:100]:
                w.writerow([
                    sanitize_csv_cell(r.get('date', '')),
                    sanitize_csv_cell(r.get('hour', '')),
                    sanitize_csv_cell(r.get('volume_mb', '')),
                    sanitize_csv_cell(r.get('speed_fmt', '')),
                    sanitize_csv_cell(r.get('basis', ''))
                ])
        return filepath, filename
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("Ошибка экспорта прогноза в CSV", e)
        return None, None

# core/health_score.py
from core.torrent_targets import compute_target_progress


def compute_health_metrics(live_item):
    if not live_item:
        return {
            'health_score': -1.0,
            'health_label': 'нет данных',
            'num_complete': -1,
            'num_incomplete': -1,
            'is_last_seed': False,
        }

    num_complete = int(live_item.get('num_complete', -1) if live_item.get('num_complete') is not None else -1)
    num_incomplete = int(live_item.get('num_incomplete', -1) if live_item.get('num_incomplete') is not None else -1)
    progress = float(live_item.get('progress', 0.0) or 0.0)

    if num_complete < 0 or num_incomplete < 0:
        return {
            'health_score': -1.0,
            'health_label': 'нет данных',
            'num_complete': num_complete,
            'num_incomplete': num_incomplete,
            'is_last_seed': False,
        }

    if num_complete == 0 and num_incomplete > 0:
        score = round(num_incomplete * 100.0 + 50.0, 1)
    else:
        score = round((num_incomplete * 100.0) / (num_complete + 1), 1)

    is_last_seed = (progress >= 0.9999 and num_complete == 0)
    return {
        'health_score': score,
        'health_label': f"{score:.1f}",
        'num_complete': num_complete,
        'num_incomplete': num_incomplete,
        'is_last_seed': is_last_seed,
    }


def enrich_live_metrics(item, live_item, target_row=None):
    hm = compute_health_metrics(live_item)
    item.update(hm)
    item['upspeed'] = int((live_item or {}).get('upspeed', 0) or 0)
    item['dlspeed'] = int((live_item or {}).get('dlspeed', 0) or 0)
    item['progress'] = float((live_item or {}).get('progress', 0.0) or 0.0)
    item['target'] = compute_target_progress(
        target_row,
        int(item.get('uploaded', 0) or 0),
        float(item.get('ratio', 0.0) or 0.0)
    )
    return item

# core/history_db.py
import os
import time
import sqlite3
import datetime
import threading
from utils.helpers import LOG_FOLDER, write_log, write_error_log
from core.history_migrations import migrate_json_if_needed, migrate_old_csvs_if_needed

AUTO_FOLDER = os.path.join(LOG_FOLDER, "auto_hourly")
MANUAL_FOLDER = os.path.join(LOG_FOLDER, "manual_exports")

os.makedirs(AUTO_FOLDER, exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(MANUAL_FOLDER, exist_ok=True)


class HistoryDB:
    def __init__(self, auto_folder=AUTO_FOLDER):
        self.auto_folder = auto_folder
        self.db_file = os.path.join(self.auto_folder, "summary_history.db")
        self.old_json_file = os.path.join(self.auto_folder, "summary_history.json")
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

        self._init_db()
        with self._lock:
            migrate_json_if_needed(self._get_connection, self.old_json_file)
            migrate_old_csvs_if_needed(self._get_connection, self.auto_folder)

    def _get_connection(self):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_file, timeout=30.0)
        conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 30000;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;")
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        return conn

    def _init_db(self):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    conn.executescript("""
                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS snapshots (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                            timestamp REAL NOT NULL,
                            datetime TEXT NOT NULL,
                            total_uploaded INTEGER NOT NULL,
                            total_size INTEGER NOT NULL,
                            count INTEGER NOT NULL
                        );
                        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_snapshots_ts ON snapshots(timestamp);

                        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS torrent_snapshots (
                            snapshot_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                            hash_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                            uploaded INTEGER NOT NULL,
                            FOREIGN KEY(snapshot_id) REFERENCES snapshots(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
                        );
                        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tor_snap ON torrent_snapshots(snapshot_id, hash_id);
                        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tor_hash ON torrent_snapshots(hash_id);
                    """)
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Инициализация SQLite summary_history.db", e)

    def get_last_snapshot_ts(self):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT MAX(timestamp) FROM snapshots")
                    row = cur.fetchone()
                    return row[0] if row and row[0] is not None else None
            except Exception:
                return None

    def record_snapshot(self, summaries, min_delta_sec=60):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                now = time.time()
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT MAX(timestamp) FROM snapshots")
                    row = cur.fetchone()
                    last_ts = row[0] if row and row[0] is not None else None
                    if last_ts is not None and (now - last_ts) < min_delta_sec:
                        return False

                    dt_str = datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
                    tot_up = sum(s.get('uploaded', 0) for s in summaries)
                    tot_sz = sum(s.get('size', 0) for s in summaries)
                    cur.execute(
                        "INSERT INTO snapshots (timestamp, datetime, total_uploaded, total_size, count) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                        (now, dt_str, tot_up, tot_sz, len(summaries))
                    )
                    snap_id = cur.lastrowid

                    tor_rows = [
                        (snap_id, (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash') or '').lower(), int(s.get('uploaded', 0)))
                        for s in summaries if (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash'))
                    ]
                    if tor_rows:
                        cur.executemany(
                            "INSERT INTO torrent_snapshots (snapshot_id, hash_id, uploaded) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                            tor_rows
                        )
                    conn.commit()
                    return True
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка записи record_snapshot в SQLite", e)
                return False

    def downsample_old_snapshots(self):
        now = time.time()
        # Сохраняем детальные записи по раздачам минимум на 31 день (защита 30-дневного графика)
        cutoff_hourly = now - (31 * 86400)
        cutoff_daily = now - (365 * 86400)

        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT id, timestamp, datetime FROM snapshots WHERE timestamp < ? ORDER BY timestamp ASC", (cutoff_hourly,))
                    rows = cur.fetchall()
                    if not rows:
                        return 0

                    buckets = {}
                    for r in rows:
                        ts = r['timestamp']
                        dt_str = r['datetime'] or datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(ts).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
                        key = ('day', dt_str[:10]) if ts < cutoff_daily else ('hour', dt_str[:13])
                        buckets.setdefault(key, []).append((ts, r['id']))

                    delete_ids = []
                    for items in buckets.values():
                        if len(items) > 1:
                            items.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
                            delete_ids.extend(item[1] for item in items[:-1])

                    if delete_ids:
                        for i in range(0, len(delete_ids), 500):
                            chunk = delete_ids[i:i + 500]
                            placeholders = ','.join('?' for _ in chunk)
                            cur.execute(f"DELETE FROM snapshots WHERE id IN ({placeholders})", chunk)
                        conn.commit()
                        write_log("summary", f"Даунсэмплинг SQLite: сжато старых снимков: {len(delete_ids)}")
                    return len(delete_ids)
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка downsample_old_snapshots", e)
                return 0

    def get_stats_snapshots(self, now_ts):
        latest_snap = prev_snap = snap_1h = snap_24h = None
        snap_1h_torrents, snap_24h_torrents = {}, {}

        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT id, timestamp, total_uploaded FROM snapshots ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT 2")
                    last_rows = cur.fetchall()
                    if last_rows:
                        latest_snap = dict(last_rows[0])
                        if len(last_rows) > 1:
                            prev_snap = dict(last_rows[1])

                    cur.execute("SELECT id, timestamp, total_uploaded FROM snapshots WHERE timestamp BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY ABS((? - timestamp) - 3600) ASC LIMIT 1", (now_ts - 7200, now_ts - 1800, now_ts))
                    r_1h = cur.fetchone()
                    if r_1h:
                        snap_1h = dict(r_1h)
                        cur.execute("SELECT hash_id, uploaded FROM torrent_snapshots WHERE snapshot_id = ?", (snap_1h['id'],))
                        snap_1h_torrents = {row['hash_id']: row['uploaded'] for row in cur.fetchall()}

                    cur.execute("SELECT id, timestamp, total_uploaded FROM snapshots WHERE timestamp BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY ABS((? - timestamp) - 86400) ASC LIMIT 1", (now_ts - 100000, now_ts - 72000, now_ts))
                    r_24h = cur.fetchone()
                    if r_24h:
                        snap_24h = dict(r_24h)
                        cur.execute("SELECT hash_id, uploaded FROM torrent_snapshots WHERE snapshot_id = ?", (snap_24h['id'],))
                        snap_24h_torrents = {row['hash_id']: row['uploaded'] for row in cur.fetchall()}
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка get_stats_snapshots из SQLite", e)

        return latest_snap, prev_snap, snap_1h, snap_1h_torrents, snap_24h, snap_24h_torrents

    def get_snapshots_since(self, since_ts):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT timestamp, datetime, total_uploaded, count FROM snapshots WHERE timestamp >= ? ORDER BY timestamp ASC", (since_ts,))
                    return [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка get_snapshots_since из SQLite", e)
                return []

    def get_torrents_positive_deltas(self, since_ts, allowed_hashes=None):
        totals = {}
        allowed = {h.lower() for h in allowed_hashes} if allowed_hashes is not None else None
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("""
                        SELECT ts.hash_id, s.timestamp, ts.uploaded
                        FROM torrent_snapshots ts
                        JOIN snapshots s ON s.id = ts.snapshot_id
                        WHERE s.timestamp >= ?
                        ORDER BY ts.hash_id ASC, s.timestamp ASC
                    """, (since_ts,))
                    cur_hash = prev_up = prev_ts = None
                    for row in cur:
                        h = row['hash_id']
                        if allowed is not None and h not in allowed:
                            continue
                        up, ts = int(row['uploaded'] or 0), float(row['timestamp'] or 0)
                        if h != cur_hash:
                            cur_hash, prev_up, prev_ts = h, up, ts
                            continue
                        d, dt = up - prev_up, max(1.0, ts - prev_ts)
                        if d > 0:
                            bps = d / dt
                            if not ((bps > 1073741824) or (prev_up < 104857600 and d > 5368709120 and bps > 314572800)):
                                totals[h] = totals.get(h, 0) + d
                        prev_up, prev_ts = up, ts
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка get_torrents_positive_deltas", e)
        return totals

    def get_oldest_torrents_since(self, since_ts):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT id FROM snapshots WHERE timestamp >= ? ORDER BY timestamp ASC LIMIT 1", (since_ts,))
                    r_old = cur.fetchone()
                    if not r_old:
                        return {}
                    cur.execute("SELECT hash_id, uploaded FROM torrent_snapshots WHERE snapshot_id = ?", (r_old['id'],))
                    return {row['hash_id']: row['uploaded'] for row in cur.fetchall()}
            except Exception:
                return {}

    def get_snapshot_range_info(self):
        with self._lock:
            with self._get_connection() as conn:
                cur = conn.cursor()
                cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*), MIN(timestamp) FROM snapshots")
                return cur.fetchone()

    def get_hist_delta(self, t_start, t_end, is_seedbox, t_hash_lower=''):
        with self._lock:
            with self._get_connection() as conn:
                cur = conn.cursor()
                cur.execute("SELECT id, total_uploaded FROM snapshots ORDER BY ABS(timestamp - ?) ASC LIMIT 1", (t_start,))
                r1 = cur.fetchone()
                cur.execute("SELECT id, total_uploaded FROM snapshots ORDER BY ABS(timestamp - ?) ASC LIMIT 1", (t_end,))
                r2 = cur.fetchone()
                if not r1 or not r2:
                    return 0
                if is_seedbox:
                    return max(0, r2['total_uploaded'] - r1['total_uploaded'])
                cur.execute("SELECT uploaded FROM torrent_snapshots WHERE snapshot_id = ? AND hash_id = ? LIMIT 1", (r1['id'], t_hash_lower))
                u1_row = cur.fetchone()
                u1 = u1_row['uploaded'] if u1_row else 0
                cur.execute("SELECT uploaded FROM torrent_snapshots WHERE snapshot_id = ? AND hash_id = ? LIMIT 1", (r2['id'], t_hash_lower))
                u2_row = cur.fetchone()
                u2 = u2_row['uploaded'] if u2_row else 0
                return max(0, u2 - u1)

    def get_torrent_history_rows(self, t_hash, limit_hours=36):
        t_hash_lower = (t_hash or '').lower()
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    if t_hash == 'all':
                        cur.execute("SELECT datetime, timestamp, total_uploaded AS uploaded FROM snapshots ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT ?", (limit_hours,))
                    else:
                        cur.execute("SELECT s.datetime, s.timestamp, ts.uploaded FROM snapshots s JOIN torrent_snapshots ts ON s.id = ts.snapshot_id WHERE ts.hash_id = ? ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT ?", (t_hash_lower, limit_hours))
                    return [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка get_torrent_history_rows из SQLite", e)
                return []

    def get_torrent_window_bounds(self, t_hash, since_ts, until_ts):
        t_hash_lower = (t_hash or '').lower()
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT s.timestamp, ts.uploaded FROM snapshots s JOIN torrent_snapshots ts ON s.id = ts.snapshot_id WHERE ts.hash_id = ? AND s.timestamp BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY s.timestamp ASC", (t_hash_lower, since_ts, until_ts))
                    rows = cur.fetchall()
                    if not rows:
                        return None, None, 0
                    return (rows[0]['timestamp'], rows[0]['uploaded']), (rows[-1]['timestamp'], rows[-1]['uploaded']), len(rows)
            except Exception:
                return None, None, 0

    def get_available_snapshot_days(self, limit=90):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT DISTINCT substr(datetime, 1, 10) AS day FROM snapshots WHERE length(datetime) >= 10 ORDER BY day DESC LIMIT ?", (int(limit),))
                    return [r['day'] for r in cur.fetchall() if r['day']]
            except Exception:
                return []

    def get_snapshots_by_day_paginated(self, start_ts, end_ts, page=1, per_page=15):
        page, per_page = max(1, int(page)), max(1, min(100, int(per_page)))
        offset = (page - 1) * per_page
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM snapshots WHERE timestamp BETWEEN ? AND ?", (start_ts, end_ts))
                    total = cur.fetchone()[0]
                    cur.execute("SELECT id, timestamp, datetime, total_uploaded, total_size, count FROM snapshots WHERE timestamp BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?", (start_ts, end_ts, per_page, offset))
                    return {'items': [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()], 'total': total, 'page': page, 'per_page': per_page, 'total_pages': max(1, (total + per_page - 1) // per_page)}
            except Exception as e:
                write_error_log("Ошибка get_snapshots_by_day_paginated", e)
                return {'items': [], 'total': 0, 'page': 1, 'per_page': per_page, 'total_pages': 1}

    def get_snapshots_by_day_all(self, start_ts, end_ts):
        with self._lock:
            try:
                with self._get_connection() as conn:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("SELECT datetime, total_uploaded, total_size, count FROM snapshots WHERE timestamp BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY timestamp ASC", (start_ts, end_ts))
                    return [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
            except Exception:
                return []

================================================================================
FILE: core/history_migrations.py
================================================================================
# core/history_migrations.py
import os
import re
import csv
import json
import datetime
from utils.helpers import LOG_FOLDER, write_log, write_error_log

_DATE_COMMENT_RE = re.compile(r'^#\s*Дата выгрузки:\s*(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}\s+\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2})')
_FNAME_DATE_RE = re.compile(r'summary_hourly_(\d{8})_(\d{4})\.csv$', re.IGNORECASE)


def migrate_json_if_needed(get_conn_fn, old_json_file):
    if not os.path.exists(old_json_file):
        return
    try:
        with get_conn_fn() as conn:
            cur = conn.cursor()
            cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM snapshots")
            if cur.fetchone()[0] > 0:
                return

            write_log("summary", "Обнаружен старый summary_history.json. Начинаем миграцию в SQLite...")
            with open(old_json_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                data = json.load(f)

            if isinstance(data, list):
                for s in data:
                    ts = s.get('timestamp', 0)
                    dt = s.get('datetime', '')
                    tot_up = s.get('total_uploaded', 0)
                    tot_sz = s.get('total_size', 0)
                    count = s.get('count', 0)
                    torrents = s.get('torrents', {})

                    cur.execute(
                        "INSERT INTO snapshots (timestamp, datetime, total_uploaded, total_size, count) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                        (ts, dt, tot_up, tot_sz, count)
                    )
                    snap_id = cur.lastrowid
                    if torrents and isinstance(torrents, dict):
                        rows = [(snap_id, str(h).lower(), int(up)) for h, up in torrents.items()]
                        cur.executemany(
                            "INSERT INTO torrent_snapshots (snapshot_id, hash_id, uploaded) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                            rows
                        )

            conn.commit()
            write_log("summary", "Миграция JSON в SQLite успешно завершена.")

        bak_file = old_json_file + ".migrated"
        if not os.path.exists(bak_file):
            os.rename(old_json_file, bak_file)
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("Ошибка миграции summary_history.json в SQLite", e)


def migrate_old_csvs_if_needed(get_conn_fn, auto_folder):
    candidates = []
    for folder in (LOG_FOLDER, auto_folder):
        if not os.path.exists(folder):
            continue
        for fname in os.listdir(folder):
            low = fname.lower()
            if low.startswith('summary_hourly_') and low.endswith('.csv'):
                candidates.append(os.path.join(folder, fname))

    if not candidates:
        return

    imported = 0
    try:
        with get_conn_fn() as conn:
            cur = conn.cursor()
            for path in sorted(candidates):
                try:
                    with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8-sig', errors='ignore') as f:
                        lines = f.readlines()

                    snap_dt_str = None
                    data_lines = []
                    for line in lines:
                        if line.startswith('#'):
                            m = _DATE_COMMENT_RE.match(line.strip())
                            if m:
                                snap_dt_str = m.group(1)
                        elif line.strip():
                            data_lines.append(line)

                    if not data_lines:
                        continue

                    if snap_dt_str:
                        snap_ts = datetime.datetime.strptime(snap_dt_str, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S').timestamp()
                    else:
                        mf = _FNAME_DATE_RE.search(os.path.basename(path))
                        if mf:
                            dt_obj = datetime.datetime.strptime(mf.group(1) + mf.group(2), '%Y%m%d%H%M')
                            snap_ts = dt_obj.timestamp()
                            snap_dt_str = dt_obj.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
                        else:
                            snap_ts = os.path.getmtime(path)
                            snap_dt_str = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(snap_ts).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')

                    cur.execute("SELECT 1 FROM snapshots WHERE ABS(timestamp - ?) < 180 LIMIT 1", (snap_ts,))
                    if cur.fetchone():
                        continue

                    reader = csv.DictReader(data_lines, delimiter=';')
                    tor_rows = []
                    tot_up = 0
                    tot_sz = 0
                    for row in reader:
                        h_id = (row.get('Хэш') or row.get('hash_id') or '').strip().lstrip("'").lower()
                        if not h_id:
                            continue
                        try:
                            up = int(float(row.get('Отдано (байт)') or row.get('uploaded') or 0))
                            sz = int(float(row.get('Размер (байт)') or row.get('size') or 0))
                        except (ValueError, TypeError):
                            continue
                        tot_up += up
                        tot_sz += sz
                        tor_rows.append((h_id, up))

                    if not tor_rows:
                        continue

                    cur.execute(
                        "INSERT INTO snapshots (timestamp, datetime, total_uploaded, total_size, count) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                        (snap_ts, snap_dt_str, tot_up, tot_sz, len(tor_rows))
                    )
                    snap_id = cur.lastrowid
                    cur.executemany(
                        "INSERT INTO torrent_snapshots (snapshot_id, hash_id, uploaded) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                        [(snap_id, h, u) for h, u in tor_rows]
                    )
                    imported += 1
                except Exception as e:
                    write_error_log(f"Импорт старого CSV {os.path.basename(path)}", e)

            if imported > 0:
                conn.commit()
                write_log("summary", f"Импортировано старых почасовых CSV-снимков в SQLite: {imported}")
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("Ошибка _migrate_old_csvs_if_needed", e)

FILE: core/keep_awake.py

# core/keep_awake.py
import os
import threading
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import write_log, write_error_log

ES_CONTINUOUS = 0x80000000
ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED = 0x00000001
ES_DISPLAY_REQUIRED = 0x00000002

_ACTIVE_DL_STATES = ("downloading", "forceddl", "metadl", "allocating", "stalleddl")


class KeepAwakeController:
    def __init__(self):
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._active = False
        self._display_on = False
        self._want_keep = False
        self._want_display = False
        self._stop_event = threading.Event()
        self._wake_event = threading.Event()
        self._thread = None

    @staticmethod
    def is_supported():
        return os.name == "nt"

    def _set_win_state(self, keep, with_display):
        if not self.is_supported():
            return
        try:
            import ctypes
            flags = ES_CONTINUOUS
            if keep:
                flags |= ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED
                if with_display:
                    flags |= ES_DISPLAY_REQUIRED
            ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetThreadExecutionState(flags)
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("KeepAwake SetThreadExecutionState", e)

    def _ensure_thread(self):
        if not self.is_supported():
            return
        if self._thread and self._thread.is_alive():
            return

        def worker():
            while not self._stop_event.is_set():
                with self._lock:
                    w_keep = self._want_keep
                    w_disp = self._want_display
                if w_keep != self._active or (w_keep and w_disp != self._display_on):
                    self._set_win_state(w_keep, w_disp)
                    self._active = w_keep
                    self._display_on = w_disp if w_keep else False
                    write_log("app", f"Keep-Awake: {'ВКЛ' if w_keep else 'ВЫКЛ'} (display={w_disp})")
                elif w_keep:
                    # Поддерживаем актуальность состояния пока идёт активность
                    self._set_win_state(True, w_disp)
                self._wake_event.wait(timeout=30.0)
                self._wake_event.clear()

            if self._active:
                self._set_win_state(False, False)
                self._active = False
                self._display_on = False

        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True)
        self._thread.start()

    def evaluate(self, live_torrents):
        if not self.is_supported():
            return False

        enabled = cfg.getboolean("KeepAwake_Enabled", False)
        if not enabled:
            self.release()
            return False

        min_kb = max(0, cfg.getint("KeepAwake_Min_KB", 500))
        with_display = cfg.getboolean("KeepAwake_Display", False)

        has_dl = False
        total_up_bps = 0
        for info in (live_torrents or {}).values():
            st = str(info.get("state", "")).lower()
            amt_left = int(info.get("amount_left", 0) or 0)
            dl_spd = int(info.get("dlspeed", 0) or 0)
            if (st in _ACTIVE_DL_STATES and amt_left > 0) or dl_spd > 0:
                has_dl = True
            total_up_bps += int(info.get("upspeed", 0) or 0)

        should_keep = has_dl or (total_up_bps >= (min_kb * 1024))
        with self._lock:
            self._want_keep = should_keep
            self._want_display = with_display
        self._ensure_thread()
        self._wake_event.set()
        return should_keep

    def release(self):
        with self._lock:
            changed = self._want_keep
            self._want_keep = False
            self._want_display = False
        if changed and self._thread and self._thread.is_alive():
            self._wake_event.set()

    def stop(self):
        with self._lock:
            self._want_keep = False
            self._want_display = False
        self._stop_event.set()
        self._wake_event.set()
        if self._thread and self._thread.is_alive():
            self._thread.join(timeout=1.5)


keep_awake = KeepAwakeController()

# core/notifiers.py
import json
import html
import urllib.request
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import write_error_log


def send_telegram(text_html):
    token = cfg.get('TG_Bot_Token', '').strip()
    chat_id = cfg.get('TG_Chat_ID', '').strip()
    if not token or not chat_id:
        return False, "Не задан токен или Chat ID"

    url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{token}/sendMessage"
    payload = json.dumps({
        'chat_id': chat_id,
        'text': text_html,
        'parse_mode': 'HTML',
        'disable_web_page_preview': True
    }).encode('utf-8')
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=payload, headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=8) as resp:
            return resp.getcode() == 200, "Telegram: OK"
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("Telegram send error", e)
        return False, f"Telegram: {e}"


def send_discord(text_md):
    webhook = cfg.get('Discord_Webhook', '').strip()
    if not webhook:
        return False, "Не задан Discord Webhook"

    payload = json.dumps({'content': text_md[:1900]}).encode('utf-8')
    req = urllib.request.Request(
        webhook,
        data=payload,
        headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'User-Agent': 'tbr-pro/3.0'}
    )
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=8) as resp:
            return resp.getcode() in (200, 204), "Discord: OK"
    except Exception as e:
        write_error_log("Discord send error", e)
        return False, f"Discord: {e}"


def notify_all(title, lines, critical=False):
    if not critical:
        try:
            from core.dnd_digest import is_dnd_active_now, queue_dnd_message
            if is_dnd_active_now():
                queue_dnd_message(title, lines)
                return ["DND: отложено в тихий час"]
        except Exception:
            pass

    tg_msg = f"<b>{html.escape(title)}</b>\n" + "\n".join(html.escape(l) for l in lines)
    dc_msg = f"**{title}**\n" + "\n".join(lines)

    results = []
    if cfg.get('TG_Bot_Token', '').strip() and cfg.get('TG_Chat_ID', '').strip():
        _, res_tg = send_telegram(tg_msg)
        results.append(res_tg)
    if cfg.get('Discord_Webhook', '').strip():
        _, res_dc = send_discord(dc_msg)
        results.append(res_dc)
    return results


def test_notifications():
    has_tg = bool(cfg.get('TG_Bot_Token', '').strip() and cfg.get('TG_Chat_ID', '').strip())
    has_dc = bool(cfg.get('Discord_Webhook', '').strip())
    if not has_tg and not has_dc:
        return False, ["Не настроен ни Telegram, ни Discord."]

    res = notify_all(
        "⚡ tbr PRO — Тест уведомлений",
        ["Связь настроена! Бот готов присылать отчёты по правилам."],
        critical=True
    )
    return True, res

# core/orphan_scanner.py
import os
import re
import shutil
import datetime
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size, write_error_log
from core.torrent_parser import get_all_torrents
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db


def _norm(p):
    return os.path.normcase(os.path.normpath(os.path.abspath(str(p).strip())))


def _read_qb_default_save_path():
    if os.name == 'nt':
        conf = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~'), 'AppData', 'Local', 'qBittorrent', 'qBittorrent.conf')
    else:
        conf = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~'), '.config', 'qBittorrent', 'qBittorrent.conf')
    if not os.path.isfile(conf):
        return ''
    try:
        with open(conf, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
            txt = f.read()
        m = re.search(r'(?:SavePath|DefaultSavePath)=([^\r\n]+)', txt, re.IGNORECASE)
        return m.group(1).strip() if m else ''
    except Exception:
        return ''


def get_download_roots():
    roots = {}
    def add_root(p):
        if not p or not str(p).strip():
            return
        ap = os.path.abspath(str(p).strip())
        if os.path.isdir(ap):
            nk = os.path.normcase(ap)
            if nk not in roots:
                roots[nk] = ap

    def_sp = _read_qb_default_save_path()
    add_root(def_sp)

    for t in get_all_torrents(use_cache=True).values():
        add_root(t.get('save_path'))

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    for t in live.values():
        add_root(t.get('save_path'))

    # Сортируем от корней к вложенным и убираем подпапки внутри уже найденных корней
    sorted_paths = sorted(roots.values(), key=lambda x: len(os.path.normpath(x)))
    top_roots = []
    for p in sorted_paths:
        np = _norm(p)
        if not any(os.path.commonpath([_norm(r), np]) == _norm(r) for r in top_roots):
            top_roots.append(p)
    return top_roots


def _collect_active_paths():
    active = set()
    parsed = get_all_torrents(use_cache=True)
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}

    for t in parsed.values():
        sp = str(t.get('save_path') or '').strip()
        nm = str(t.get('name') or '').strip()
        if sp:
            active.add(_norm(sp))
            if nm:
                active.add(_norm(os.path.join(sp, nm)))

    for t in live.values():
        sp = str(t.get('save_path') or '').strip()
        cp = str(t.get('content_path') or '').strip()
        nm = str(t.get('name') or '').strip()
        if sp:
            active.add(_norm(sp))
        if cp:
            active.add(_norm(cp))
        if sp and nm:
            active.add(_norm(os.path.join(sp, nm)))

    return active


def _entry_size(path):
    if os.path.isfile(path) or os.path.islink(path):
        try:
            return os.path.getsize(path)
        except OSError:
            return 0
    total = 0
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(path, followlinks=False):
        for fname in files:
            try:
                total += os.path.getsize(os.path.join(root, fname))
            except OSError:
                pass
    return total


def _is_path_protected(entry_norm, active_paths):
    for ap in active_paths:
        try:
            common = os.path.commonpath([entry_norm, ap])
            if common == entry_norm or common == ap:
                return True
        except ValueError:
            continue
    return False


def scan_orphan_entries(custom_roots=None):
    valid_roots = get_download_roots()
    if custom_roots:
        roots = [os.path.abspath(str(r).strip()) for r in custom_roots if r and os.path.isdir(str(r).strip())]
    else:
        roots = valid_roots

    active_paths = _collect_active_paths()
    orphans = []
    total_bytes = 0

    for root_dir in roots:
        try:
            with os.scandir(root_dir) as it:
                for ent in it:
                    if ent.name.startswith('.') or ent.name in ('$RECYCLE.BIN', 'System Volume Information'):
                        continue
                    ent_abs = os.path.abspath(ent.path)
                    ent_norm = _norm(ent_abs)
                    if _is_path_protected(ent_norm, active_paths):
                        continue
                    sz = _entry_size(ent_abs)
                    try:
                        mtime = ent.stat().st_mtime
                    except OSError:
                        mtime = 0
                    total_bytes += sz
                    orphans.append({
                        'path': ent_abs,
                        'name': ent.name,
                        'root': root_dir,
                        'is_dir': ent.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False),
                        'size': sz,
                        'size_fmt': format_file_size(sz),
                        'mtime': int(mtime),
                        'date': datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(mtime).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') if mtime else '—'
                    })
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log(f"scan_orphan_entries {root_dir}", e)

    orphans.sort(key=lambda x: x['size'], reverse=True)
    return {
        'roots': roots,
        'orphans': orphans,
        'count': len(orphans),
        'total_size': total_bytes,
        'total_size_fmt': format_file_size(total_bytes)
    }


def delete_orphan_entries(paths, actor='admin'):
    allowed_roots = [_norm(r) for r in get_download_roots()]
    active_paths = _collect_active_paths()
    deleted = []
    errors = []

    for raw_p in paths:
        if not raw_p or not isinstance(raw_p, str):
            continue
        target_abs = os.path.abspath(raw_p.strip())
        target_norm = _norm(target_abs)

        # Защита от Path Traversal и выхода за пределы папок загрузок
        inside_allowed = False
        for r_norm in allowed_roots:
            try:
                if os.path.commonpath([r_norm, target_norm]) == r_norm and target_norm != r_norm:
                    inside_allowed = True
                    break
            except ValueError:
                continue

        if not inside_allowed:
            errors.append(f"Отклонено (вне корневых папок загрузок): {raw_p}")
            continue

        if _is_path_protected(target_norm, active_paths):
            errors.append(f"Отклонено (путь используется активным торрентом): {raw_p}")
            continue

        try:
            if os.path.isdir(target_abs) and not os.path.islink(target_abs):
                shutil.rmtree(target_abs)
            elif os.path.exists(target_abs) or os.path.islink(target_abs):
                os.remove(target_abs)
            else:
                errors.append(f"Не найдено: {raw_p}")
                continue
            deleted.append(target_abs)
        except Exception as e:
            errors.append(f"Ошибка удаления {os.path.basename(target_abs)}: {e}")

    if deleted:
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Удаление сирот', f'Удалено объектов: {len(deleted)}', actor=actor)
    return len(errors) == 0, deleted, errors

# core/private_vault.py
import time
from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db


def get_vault_settings():
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                rows = conn.execute("SELECT tag FROM private_vault_tags ORDER BY tag ASC").fetchall()
                tags = [r['tag'] for r in rows]
        except Exception:
            tags = []
    mode = cfg.get('Vault_Aggregate_Mode', 'exclude').strip().lower()
    if mode not in ('exclude', 'anonymize'):
        mode = 'exclude'
    return {'tags': tags, 'aggregate_mode': mode}


def save_vault_settings(tags_input, aggregate_mode='exclude'):
    if isinstance(tags_input, str):
        raw_list = tags_input.split(',')
    elif isinstance(tags_input, list):
        raw_list = tags_input
    else:
        raw_list = []

    clean_tags = sorted({str(t).strip().lower()[:32] for t in raw_list if str(t).strip()})
    mode = str(aggregate_mode or 'exclude').strip().lower()
    if mode not in ('exclude', 'anonymize'):
        mode = 'exclude'

    now = time.time()
    with app_state_db._lock:
        try:
            with app_state_db._get_conn() as conn:
                conn.execute("DELETE FROM private_vault_tags")
                if clean_tags:
                    conn.executemany(
                        "INSERT INTO private_vault_tags (tag, created_at) VALUES (?, ?)",
                        [(t, now) for t in clean_tags]
                    )
                conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            pass

    cfg.set('Vault_Aggregate_Mode', mode)
    cfg.save()
    return {'tags': clean_tags, 'aggregate_mode': mode}


def get_hidden_hashes(live_info):
    tags = set(get_vault_settings().get('tags') or [])
    if not tags or not live_info:
        return set()
    hidden = set()
    for t_hash, info in live_info.items():
        t_tags = {tg.strip().lower() for tg in str(info.get('tags', '') or '').split(',') if tg.strip()}
        if t_tags & tags:
            hidden.add(str(t_hash).lower())
    return hidden


def filter_torrents_for_role(items, role, hidden_hashes):
    if role == 'admin' or not hidden_hashes:
        return items
    return [
        it for it in items
        if str(it.get('hash') or it.get('hash_id') or '').lower() not in hidden_hashes
    ]


def filter_summary_stats_for_role(stats, role, hidden_hashes):
    if role == 'admin' or not hidden_hashes or not isinstance(stats, dict):
        return stats

    mode = get_vault_settings().get('aggregate_mode', 'exclude')
    all_items = stats.get('items') or []
    visible_items = [
        it for it in all_items
        if str(it.get('hash_id') or it.get('hash') or '').lower() not in hidden_hashes
    ]

    out = dict(stats)
    out['items'] = visible_items

    if mode == 'exclude':
        tot_up = sum(int(i.get('uploaded', 0) or 0) for i in visible_items)
        tot_sz = sum(int(i.get('size', 0) or 0) for i in visible_items)
        hidden_items = [
            it for it in all_items
            if str(it.get('hash_id') or it.get('hash') or '').lower() in hidden_hashes
        ]
        hid_day = sum(int(i.get('delta_24h', 0) or 0) for i in hidden_items)
        vis_day = max(0, int(out.get('global_day_volume', 0) or 0) - hid_day)
        out['total_torrents'] = len(visible_items)
        out['total_size'] = tot_sz
        out['total_size_fmt'] = format_file_size(tot_sz)
        out['total_uploaded'] = tot_up
        out['total_uploaded_fmt'] = format_file_size(tot_up)
        out['global_day_volume'] = vis_day
        out['global_day_volume_fmt'] = f"{format_file_size(vis_day)} / день"

    return out

================================================================================
FILE: core/profiles_db.py
