# web/routes/analytics_bp.py
import os
import io
import csv
import datetime
import urllib.parse
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, Response, g

from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import sanitize_csv_cell, format_file_size, LOG_FOLDER
from core.torrent_parser import get_global_torrent_summaries
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info
from core.private_vault import get_hidden_hashes, filter_summary_stats_for_role
from core.torrent_targets import list_targets
from core.health_score import enrich_live_metrics
from web.security import HASH_RE

analytics_bp = Blueprint('analytics_bp', __name__)


def _parse_day_bounds(date_str):
    try:
        d = datetime.datetime.strptime(str(date_str).strip(), '%Y-%m-%d')
    except ValueError:
        d = datetime.datetime.now().replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
    start_ts = d.replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp()
    end_ts = d.replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59, microsecond=999999).timestamp()
    return d.strftime('%Y-%m-%d'), start_ts, end_ts


def _is_strict_localhost():
    if os.path.exists('/.dockerenv') or bool(os.environ.get('TBR_DATA_DIR', '').strip()):
        return False
    if request.headers.get('X-Forwarded-For') or request.headers.get('X-Real-IP'):
        return False
    return (request.remote_addr or '').strip() in ('127.0.0.1', '::1')


def _get_live_dict():
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    return get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}


@analytics_bp.route('/api/summary')
def api_summary():
    summaries = get_global_torrent_summaries()
    stats = summary_tracker.get_stats(summaries)
    live = _get_live_dict()
    targets = list_targets()
    live_lower = {k.lower(): v for k, v in live.items()}

    for it in stats.get('items', []):
        hl = (it.get('hash_id') or it.get('hash') or '').lower()
        enrich_live_metrics(it, live_lower.get(hl), targets.get(hl))

    role = g.get('role') or 'viewer'
    if role != 'admin':
        hidden = get_hidden_hashes(live)
        stats = filter_summary_stats_for_role(stats, role, hidden)
    return jsonify(stats)


@analytics_bp.route('/api/analytics/history')
def api_analytics_history():
    t_hash = request.args.get('hash', 'all')
    if t_hash != 'all' and not HASH_RE.fullmatch(t_hash):
        return jsonify([])
    if t_hash != 'all' and g.get('role') != 'admin':
        if t_hash.lower() in get_hidden_hashes(_get_live_dict()):
            return jsonify([])
    limit = min(100, max(1, request.args.get('limit', 36, type=int)))
    return jsonify(summary_tracker.get_torrent_hourly_history(t_hash, limit_hours=limit))


@analytics_bp.route('/api/analytics/charts')
def api_analytics_charts():
    hours = request.args.get('hours', 24, type=int)
    top_n = request.args.get('top_n', 5, type=int)
    top_period = request.args.get('top_period', '1d')
    summaries = get_global_torrent_summaries()
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        hidden = get_hidden_hashes(_get_live_dict())
        if hidden:
            summaries = [s for s in summaries if (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash') or '').lower() not in hidden]
    return jsonify(summary_tracker.get_charts_data(summaries, hours=hours, top_n=top_n, top_period=top_period))


@analytics_bp.route('/api/smart_calc', methods=['POST'])
def api_smart_calc():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True)
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Неверный формат JSON'}), 400
    summaries = get_global_torrent_summaries()
    return jsonify(summary_tracker.calculate_smart_forecast(
        str(data.get('target_type', 'torrent')), str(data.get('hash', '')),
        str(data.get('target', ''))[:64], str(data.get('speed', ''))[:64], summaries
    ))


@analytics_bp.route('/api/smart_calc/export', methods=['POST'])
def api_smart_calc_export():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True)
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Ожидался JSON объект'}), 400
    forecast_data = data.get('forecast_data')
    if not isinstance(forecast_data, dict):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Отсутствует forecast_data'}), 400
    rows = forecast_data.get('forecast_rows')
    if not isinstance(rows, list) or len(rows) > 100 or not all(isinstance(r, dict) for r in rows):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные строки прогноза'}), 400
    filepath, filename = summary_tracker.export_forecast_csv(forecast_data)
    if not filepath:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Ошибка создания файла'}), 500
    with open(filepath, 'rb') as f:
        file_bytes = f.read()
    return Response(file_bytes, mimetype="text/csv", headers={"Content-Disposition": f"attachment; filename*=UTF-8''{urllib.parse.quote(filename)}"})


@analytics_bp.route('/api/export_csv', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_export_csv():
    if request.method == 'POST':
        req_data = request.get_json(silent=True)
        if not isinstance(req_data, dict):
            return jsonify({'error': 'Некорректный JSON'}), 400
        items = req_data.get('items')
        if not isinstance(items, list) or len(items) > 5000 or not all(isinstance(x, dict) for x in items):
            return jsonify({'error': 'Некорректный список или превышен лимит 5000 строк'}), 400
    else:
        items = summary_tracker.get_stats(get_global_torrent_summaries())['items']

    summary_tracker.save_manual_csv(items)
    output = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.writer(output, delimiter=';')
    writer.writerow(['Имя', 'Хэш', 'Размер (байт)', 'Размер (ГБ)', 'Отдано (байт)', 'Отдано (ГБ)', 'Ratio', 'Ср. скорость (всё время)', 'Скорость за 1ч', 'Скорость за 24ч', 'МБ/ч', 'МБ/день', 'Ratio/ч (всего)', 'Ratio/ч (1ч)', 'Ratio/ч (24ч)', 'Дата создания'])
    for s in items:
        sz = int(s.get('size', 0)) if isinstance(s.get('size'), (int, float)) else 0
        up = int(s.get('uploaded', 0)) if isinstance(s.get('uploaded'), (int, float)) else 0
        rt = float(s.get('ratio', 0.0)) if isinstance(s.get('ratio'), (int, float)) else 0.0
        writer.writerow([
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('name', '')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('hash_id', '')),
            sz, f"{sz / (1024 ** 3):.2f}", up, f"{up / (1024 ** 3):.2f}", f"{rt:.2f}",
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('speed_all_time_fmt', '0 B/s')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('speed_1h_fmt', '0 B/s')),
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('speed_24h_fmt', '0 B/s')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('vol_hour_fmt', '0.00 МБ/ч')),
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('vol_day_fmt', '0.00 МБ/день')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('ratio_h_all_fmt', '0.000 /ч')),
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('ratio_h_hour_fmt', '0.000 /ч')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('ratio_h_day_fmt', '0.000 /ч')),
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('created', ''))
        ])
    filename = f"summary_manual_{datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.csv"
    return Response(output.getvalue().encode('utf-8-sig'), mimetype="text/csv", headers={"Content-Disposition": f"attachment; filename={filename}"})


@analytics_bp.route('/api/logs/can_open_folder')
def api_logs_can_open_folder():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'can_open': False}), 403
    return jsonify({'can_open': _is_strict_localhost() and os.name == 'nt'})


@analytics_bp.route('/api/logs/open_folder', methods=['POST'])
def api_logs_open_folder():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin' or not _is_strict_localhost() or os.name != 'nt':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Доступно только локально с localhost (не в Docker)'}), 403
    try:
        os.startfile(LOG_FOLDER)
        return jsonify({'success': True})
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': str(e)}), 500


@analytics_bp.route('/api/logs/days')
def api_logs_days():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return jsonify({'days': summary_tracker.db.get_available_snapshot_days(limit=90)})


@analytics_bp.route('/api/logs/day')
def api_logs_by_day():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    date_str, start_ts, end_ts = _parse_day_bounds(request.args.get('date', ''))
    page = request.args.get('page', 1, type=int)
    per_page = request.args.get('per_page', 15, type=int)
    res = summary_tracker.db.get_snapshots_by_day_paginated(start_ts, end_ts, page=page, per_page=per_page)
    for it in res['items']:
        it['uploaded_fmt'] = format_file_size(it.get('total_uploaded', 0))
        it['size_fmt'] = format_file_size(it.get('total_size', 0))
    res['date'] = date_str
    return jsonify(res)


@analytics_bp.route('/api/logs/day/download')
def api_logs_by_day_download():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return "Только для Admin", 403
    date_str, start_ts, end_ts = _parse_day_bounds(request.args.get('date', ''))
    rows = summary_tracker.db.get_snapshots_by_day_all(start_ts, end_ts)
    output = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.writer(output, delimiter=';')
    writer.writerow(['Время снимка', 'Всего отдано (байт)', 'Всего отдано', 'Общий размер (байт)', 'Раздач'])
    for r in rows:
        writer.writerow([sanitize_csv_cell(r.get('datetime', '')), r.get('total_uploaded', 0), sanitize_csv_cell(format_file_size(r.get('total_uploaded', 0))), r.get('total_size', 0), r.get('count', 0)])
    return Response(output.getvalue().encode('utf-8-sig'), mimetype="text/csv", headers={"Content-Disposition": f"attachment; filename=snapshots_{date_str}.csv"})

# web/routes/automation_bp.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify

from utils.config import cfg
from core.automation import automation_engine, VALID_TIME_MODES, VALID_SPEED_OPS
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db

automation_bp = Blueprint('automation_bp', __name__)


def _parse_rule_payload(data):
    name = str(data.get('name', '')).strip()[:64] or 'Правило'
    time_mode = str(data.get('time_mode', 'seeding_time')).strip()
    if time_mode not in VALID_TIME_MODES:
        time_mode = 'seeding_time'

    try:
        min_ratio = max(0.0, float(data.get('min_ratio') or 0))
        min_days = max(0.0, float(data.get('min_seed_days') or 0))
        min_up_bytes = max(0, int(data.get('min_uploaded_bytes') or 0))
        speed_op = str(data.get('speed_op', '') or '').strip()
        if speed_op not in VALID_SPEED_OPS:
            speed_op = ''
        speed_bytes = max(0.0, float(data.get('speed_bytes') or 0))
        speed_win = max(1, min(10080, int(data.get('speed_window_min') or 60)))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return None, 'Некорректные числовые параметры правила'

    use_speed = (speed_op in ('>=', '<') and speed_bytes > 0)
    if min_ratio <= 0 and min_days <= 0 and min_up_bytes <= 0 and not use_speed:
        return None, 'Укажите хотя бы одно условие (Ratio, Время, Отдано или Среднюю скорость)'

    return {
        'name': name, 'time_mode': time_mode, 'min_ratio': min_ratio,
        'min_seed_days': min_days, 'min_uploaded_bytes': min_up_bytes,
        'speed_op': speed_op if use_speed else '', 'speed_bytes': speed_bytes if use_speed else 0.0,
        'speed_window_min': speed_win, 'action_pause': bool(data.get('action_pause', False)),
        'action_tag': str(data.get('action_tag', '')).strip()[:32],
        'action_remove_tag': str(data.get('action_remove_tag', '')).strip()[:32],
        'notify': bool(data.get('notify', True))
    }, None


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation', methods=['GET'])
def api_automation_get():
    return jsonify({
        'settings': {
            'enabled': cfg.getboolean('Auto_Enabled', False),
            'interval': cfg.getint('Auto_Interval_Min', 5),
            'has_tg_token': bool(cfg.get('TG_Bot_Token', '').strip()),
            'tg_chat_id': cfg.get('TG_Chat_ID', ''),
            'has_discord': bool(cfg.get('Discord_Webhook', '').strip()),
            'alert_disk_gib': cfg.getint('Alert_Disk_Min_GiB', 0),
            'alert_qb_down': cfg.getboolean('Alert_QB_Down', False),
            'auto_recheck_enabled': cfg.getboolean('AutoRecheck_Enabled', False)
        },
        'rules': automation_engine.get_rules()
    })


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/settings', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_settings():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    enabled = bool(data.get('enabled', False))
    try:
        interval = max(1, min(1440, int(data.get('interval', 5))))
        alert_disk = max(0, min(100000, int(data.get('alert_disk_gib', 0) or 0)))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные числовые параметры'}), 400

    tg_token = str(data.get('tg_token', '')).strip()
    tg_chat = str(data.get('tg_chat_id', '')).strip()
    discord = str(data.get('discord_webhook', '')).strip()

    if discord and not (discord.startswith('https://discord.com/api/webhooks/') or discord.startswith('https://discordapp.com/api/webhooks/')):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный URL Discord Webhook!'}), 400

    cfg.set('Auto_Enabled', str(enabled))
    cfg.set('Auto_Interval_Min', str(interval))
    cfg.set('Alert_Disk_Min_GiB', str(alert_disk))
    cfg.set('Alert_QB_Down', str(bool(data.get('alert_qb_down', False))))
    cfg.set('AutoRecheck_Enabled', str(bool(data.get('auto_recheck_enabled', False))))
    if tg_token:
        cfg.set('TG_Bot_Token', tg_token)
    cfg.set('TG_Chat_ID', tg_chat)
    if discord:
        cfg.set('Discord_Webhook', discord)
    cfg.save()

    app_state_db.add_audit_event('automation', 'Настройки автопилота обновлены', f'enabled={enabled}, interval={interval}m', actor='admin')
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Настройки автопилота сохранены!'})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/test', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_test():
    ok, results = automation_engine.test_notifications()
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': ' | '.join(results)})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/run_now', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_run_now():
    cnt = automation_engine.run_cycle()
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'triggered': cnt, 'message': f'Проверка завершена. Сработало раздач: {cnt}'})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/rules', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_add_rule():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    parsed, err = _parse_rule_payload(data)
    if err:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err}), 400
    rid = automation_engine.add_rule(**parsed)
    return jsonify({'success': bool(rid), 'id': rid})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/rules/update', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_update_rule():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    rid = data.get('id')
    if not rid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID правила'}), 400
    try:
        rid_int = int(rid)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный ID правила'}), 400
    parsed, err = _parse_rule_payload(data)
    if err:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err}), 400
    ok = automation_engine.update_rule(rid_int, **parsed)
    return jsonify({'success': ok}), (200 if ok else 404)


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/rules/toggle', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_toggle_rule():
    rid = (request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id')
    if not rid:
        return jsonify({'success': False}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': automation_engine.toggle_rule(rid)})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/rules/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_delete_rule():
    rid = (request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id')
    if not rid:
        return jsonify({'success': False}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': automation_engine.delete_rule(rid)})

# web/routes/insights_bp.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g
from utils.config import cfg
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info
from core.torrent_parser import get_global_torrent_summaries
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.private_vault import get_hidden_hashes
from core.analytics_insights import (
    fetch_torrent_peers_safe, get_peer_clients_breakdown,
    get_tracker_analytics, get_heatmap_365,
    get_records_and_comparisons, detect_isp_throttling
)
from web.security import HASH_RE

insights_bp = Blueprint('insights_bp', __name__)


@insights_bp.route('/api/torrents/peers')
def api_torrents_peers():
    t_hash = str(request.args.get('hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(t_hash):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if g.get('role') != 'admin' and port:
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port)
        if t_hash.lower() in get_hidden_hashes(live):
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Доступ запрещён'}), 403

    peers = fetch_torrent_peers_safe(ip, port, t_hash)
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'peers': peers})


@insights_bp.route('/api/torrents/chart')
def api_torrents_chart():
    t_hash = str(request.args.get('hash', '')).strip()
    period = str(request.args.get('period', '24h')).strip()
    if period not in ('24h', '7d', '30d'):
        period = '24h'
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(t_hash):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400

    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
        if t_hash.lower() in get_hidden_hashes(live):
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Доступ запрещён'}), 403

    pts = summary_tracker.get_torrent_chart_series(t_hash, period=period)
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'period': period, 'points': pts})


@insights_bp.route('/api/analytics/recalculate_records', methods=['POST'])
def api_recalculate_records():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    heatmap = get_heatmap_365(summary_tracker.db)
    records = get_records_and_comparisons(summary_tracker.db, heatmap_cells=heatmap, force_recalc=True)
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'records': records})


@insights_bp.route('/api/analytics/insights')
def api_analytics_insights():
    tracker_period = str(request.args.get('tracker_period', '7d')).strip()
    force_clients = request.args.get('force_clients', '0') == '1'

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    summaries = get_global_torrent_summaries()

    allowed_hashes = None
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        hidden = get_hidden_hashes(live)
        if hidden:
            allowed_hashes = [
                (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash') or '').lower()
                for s in summaries
                if (s.get('hash_id') or s.get('hash') or '').lower() not in hidden
            ]

    heatmap = get_heatmap_365(summary_tracker.db)
    records = get_records_and_comparisons(summary_tracker.db, heatmap_cells=heatmap)
    clients = get_peer_clients_breakdown(ip, port, live, force=force_clients)
    trackers = get_tracker_analytics(summary_tracker.db, summaries, live=live, period=tracker_period, allowed_hashes=allowed_hashes)
    throttle = detect_isp_throttling(summary_tracker.db, ip, port)

    return jsonify({
        'heatmap': heatmap,
        'records': records,
        'clients': clients,
        'trackers': trackers,
        'throttle': throttle
    })

# web/routes/profiles_bp.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g
from core.profiles_db import profiles_db
from core.qb_api import get_local_ips
from web.security import check_rate_limit, record_failed_login

profiles_bp = Blueprint('profiles_bp', __name__)


@profiles_bp.route('/api/me')
def api_me():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    if not prof:
        return jsonify({'authenticated': False, 'role': 'viewer', 'ui_settings': profiles_db.get_ui_settings(0)})
    return jsonify({
        'authenticated': True,
        'id': prof['id'],
        'name': prof['name'],
        'login': prof.get('login', ''),
        'role': prof['role'],
        'ui_settings': profiles_db.get_ui_settings(prof['id'])
    })


@profiles_bp.route('/api/auth/exchange', methods=['POST'])
def api_auth_exchange():
    ip = (request.headers.get('X-Forwarded-For') or request.remote_addr or '0.0.0.0').split(',')[0].strip()
    if not check_rate_limit(ip):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Слишком много попыток. Подождите 5 минут.'}), 429

    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    login = str(data.get('login', '')).strip()
    password = str(data.get('password', '')).strip()
    prof = profiles_db.authenticate_login(login, password)
    if not prof:
        record_failed_login(ip)
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Неверный логин или пароль'}), 401

    return jsonify({
        'success': True,
        'api_token': prof.get('api_token', ''),
        'role': prof['role'],
        'name': prof['name']
    })


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profile/ui', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_profile_ui():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    pid = prof['id'] if prof else 0
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return jsonify({'ui_settings': profiles_db.get_ui_settings(pid)})
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    saved = profiles_db.save_ui_settings(pid, data)
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'ui_settings': saved})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profile/password', methods=['POST'])
def api_self_password():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    if not prof:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не авторизован'}), 401
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    new_pwd = str(data.get('password', '')).strip()
    tok, err = profiles_db.change_password(prof['id'], new_password=new_pwd)
    if err:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': True})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/network/hosts')
def api_network_hosts():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    ips = get_local_ips()
    return jsonify({'ips': ips})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles')
def api_profiles_list():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return jsonify({'profiles': profiles_db.list_profiles()})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/create', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_create():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    pid, raw_pwd, api_tok, err = profiles_db.create_profile(
        data.get('name', ''), data.get('role', 'viewer'),
        login=data.get('login', ''), password=data.get('password', '')
    )
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err or 'Не удалось создать профиль'}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'id': pid, 'password': raw_pwd, 'api_token': api_tok})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/change_password', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_change_password():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    pid = data.get('id')
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID'}), 400
    new_pwd, err = profiles_db.change_password(pid, new_password=data.get('password', ''), new_login=data.get('login'))
    if err:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'password': new_pwd})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/invite', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_invite():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    pid = data.get('id')
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID'}), 400
    inv = profiles_db.create_invite(pid)
    if not inv:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не удалось создать инвайт'}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'invite_token': inv})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/reissue_api', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_reissue_api():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    pid = data.get('id')
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID'}), 400
    tok = profiles_db.reissue_api_token(pid)
    if not tok:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Профиль не найден'}), 404
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'api_token': tok})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_delete():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    pid = data.get('id')
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID'}), 400
    ok, msg = profiles_db.delete_profile(pid)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg, 'error': None if ok else msg}), (200 if ok else 400)

================================================================================

# web/routes/settings_bp.py
import os
import hmac
import datetime
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, render_template, redirect, session, current_app, Response, g

from utils.config import cfg
from core.version import APP_VERSION, GITHUB_RELEASES_URL
from core.qb_api import reset_qb_session, get_qb_executable_path
from core.autostart import is_autostart_enabled
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.scheduler import ALLOWED_INTERVALS, normalize_interval
from core.profiles_db import profiles_db
from core.backup_manager import create_backup_zip, stage_restore_zip
from core.keep_awake import keep_awake
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from web.security import get_csrf_token, is_allowed_qb_ip, check_rate_limit, record_failed_login

settings_bp = Blueprint('settings_bp', __name__)


@settings_bp.route('/invite/<token>', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def invite_accept(token):
    inv = profiles_db.get_valid_invite(token)
    if not inv:
        return render_template('invite.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error='Инвайт-ссылка недействительна, просрочена или уже использована.', invite=None), 410
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return render_template('invite.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error=None, invite=inv)
    expected, sent = session.get('csrf_token'), request.form.get('csrf_token')
    if not (expected and sent and hmac.compare_digest(str(expected), str(sent))):
        return render_template('invite.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error='CSRF ошибка, обновите страницу.', invite=inv), 403
    prof, err = profiles_db.consume_invite(token, request.form.get('login', '').strip(), request.form.get('password', '').strip())
    if err or not prof:
        return render_template('invite.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error=err or 'Ошибка активации', invite=inv), 400
    session['authenticated'] = True
    session['profile_id'] = prof['id']
    app_state_db.add_audit_event('auth', 'Активация инвайта', f"Профиль {prof['login']}", actor=prof['login'])
    return redirect('/')


@settings_bp.route('/login', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def login():
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return render_template('login.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error=None)
    ip = (request.headers.get('X-Forwarded-For') or request.remote_addr or '0.0.0.0').split(',')[0].strip()
    if not check_rate_limit(ip):
        return render_template('login.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error='Слишком много попыток входа. Подождите 5 минут.'), 429

    login_val, pwd = request.form.get('login', '').strip(), request.form.get('password', '').strip()
    real_pwd = cfg.get('Web_Password', '')
    if real_pwd and hmac.compare_digest(pwd, real_pwd) and (not login_val or login_val.lower() == 'admin'):
        session['authenticated'] = True
        adm = profiles_db.get_default_admin()
        if adm:
            session['profile_id'] = adm['id']
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('auth', 'Успешный вход', f'IP: {ip}', actor='admin')
        return redirect('/')

    prof = profiles_db.authenticate_login(login_val, pwd) if login_val else profiles_db.authenticate_api_token(pwd)
    if prof:
        session['authenticated'] = True
        session['profile_id'] = prof['id']
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('auth', 'Успешный вход', f'IP: {ip}', actor=prof.get('login') or prof['name'])
        return redirect('/')

    record_failed_login(ip)
    app_state_db.add_audit_event('auth', 'Неудачный вход', f"IP: {ip}, login={login_val or '-'}", actor=login_val or 'guest')
    return render_template('login.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error='Неверный логин или пароль!'), 401


@settings_bp.route('/logout', methods=['POST', 'GET'])
def logout():
    session.pop('authenticated', None)
    session.pop('profile_id', None)
    return redirect('/login')


@settings_bp.route('/')
def index():
    return render_template(
        'index.html',
        csrf_token=get_csrf_token(),
        auth_enabled=current_app.config.get('IS_EXPOSED', False),
        v=current_app.config.get('BUILD_VER', '1'),
        app_version=APP_VERSION,
        releases_url=GITHUB_RELEASES_URL
    )


@settings_bp.route('/api/backup/export')
def api_backup_export():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    ex_hist = request.args.get('exclude_history', '0') == '1'
    strip_sec = request.args.get('strip_secrets', '0') == '1'
    zip_bytes = create_backup_zip(exclude_history=ex_hist, strip_secrets=strip_sec)
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
    return Response(zip_bytes, mimetype='application/zip', headers={'Content-Disposition': f'attachment; filename=tbr_backup_{ts}.zip'})


@settings_bp.route('/api/backup/restore', methods=['POST'])
def api_backup_restore():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    f = request.files.get('file')
    if not f:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Файл не передан'}), 400
    ok, msg = stage_restore_zip(f.read())
    if ok:
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Восстановление бэкапа', msg, actor='admin')
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@settings_bp.route('/api/settings', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_settings():
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return jsonify({
            'web_port': cfg.getint('Web_Port', 5000), 'web_host': cfg.get('Web_Host', '127.0.0.1'),
            'has_password': bool(cfg.get('Web_Password', '').strip()),
            'qb_ip': cfg.get('WebUI_Address', ''), 'qb_port': cfg.get('WebUI_Port', ''),
            'qb_user': cfg.get('WebUI_User', 'admin'), 'has_qb_password': bool(cfg.get('WebUI_Password', '').strip()),
            'w1': cfg.getint('Weight_W1', 70), 'w2': cfg.getint('Weight_W2', 30), 'w3': cfg.getint('Weight_W3', 0),
            'snapshot_interval': normalize_interval(cfg.getint('Snapshot_Interval_Min', 60)),
            'autostart': is_autostart_enabled(),
            'is_windows': (os.name == 'nt'),
            'keep_awake_enabled': cfg.getboolean('KeepAwake_Enabled', False),
            'keep_awake_min_kb': cfg.getint('KeepAwake_Min_KB', 500),
            'keep_awake_display': cfg.getboolean('KeepAwake_Display', False),
            'auto_recheck_enabled': cfg.getboolean('AutoRecheck_Enabled', False),
            'watchdog_enabled': cfg.getboolean('Watchdog_Enabled', False),
            'watchdog_timeout_min': cfg.getint('Watchdog_Timeout_Min', 3),
            'watchdog_exe_path': cfg.get('Watchdog_Exe_Path', ''),
            'watchdog_exe_detected': get_qb_executable_path() if os.name == 'nt' else '',
            'manifest_weekly': cfg.getboolean('Manifest_Weekly', False),
            'max_channel_mbps': cfg.getint('Max_Channel_Mbps', 1000)
        })

    data = request.get_json(silent=True)
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные данные'}), 400

    old_p, old_h = cfg.getint('Web_Port', 5000), cfg.get('Web_Host', '127.0.0.1').strip() or '127.0.0.1'
    try:
        new_p = int(data.get('web_port', old_p))
        if not (1 <= new_p <= 65535):
            raise ValueError
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Порт должен быть числом от 1 до 65535'}), 400

    qb_ip = str(data.get('qb_ip', '')).strip()
    if not is_allowed_qb_ip(qb_ip):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Недопустимый IP qBittorrent (разрешены только локальные сети и NetBird)'}), 400

    qb_port_raw = str(data.get('qb_port', '')).strip()
    if qb_port_raw:
        try:
            if not (1 <= int(qb_port_raw) <= 65535):
                raise ValueError
        except ValueError:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Порт qBittorrent должен быть от 1 до 65535'}), 400

    try:
        w1, w2, w3 = int(data.get('w1', 70)), int(data.get('w2', 30)), int(data.get('w3', 0))
        if w1 < 0 or w2 < 0 or w3 < 0 or (w1 + w2 + w3) <= 0:
            raise ValueError
        snap_iv = int(data.get('snapshot_interval', 60))
        if snap_iv not in ALLOWED_INTERVALS:
            raise ValueError
        ka_min_kb = max(0, min(1000000, int(data.get('keep_awake_min_kb', 500))))
        wd_timeout = max(2, min(10, int(data.get('watchdog_timeout_min', 3))))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные числовые параметры'}), 400

    new_host = str(data.get('web_host', '127.0.0.1')).strip() or '127.0.0.1'
    new_pass = str(data.get('web_password', '')).strip()
    if new_host not in ('127.0.0.1', 'localhost') and not (new_pass or cfg.get('Web_Password', '').strip()):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Для внешнего хоста обязательно задайте пароль!'}), 400

    wd_exe = str(data.get('watchdog_exe_path', '')).strip()
    if wd_exe and ('..' in wd_exe or (os.name == 'nt' and not wd_exe.lower().endswith('.exe'))):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный путь к исполняемому файлу qBittorrent'}), 400

    old_snap_iv = normalize_interval(cfg.getint('Snapshot_Interval_Min', 60))
    cfg.set('Web_Port', new_p); cfg.set('Web_Host', new_host)
    if new_pass:
        cfg.set('Web_Password', new_pass)
    cfg.set('WebUI_Address', qb_ip); cfg.set('WebUI_Port', qb_port_raw)
    cfg.set('WebUI_User', str(data.get('qb_user', 'admin')).strip() or 'admin')
    if str(data.get('qb_password', '')):
        cfg.set('WebUI_Password', str(data.get('qb_password', '')))
    cfg.set('Weight_W1', w1); cfg.set('Weight_W2', w2); cfg.set('Weight_W3', w3)
    cfg.set('Snapshot_Interval_Min', snap_iv)

    ka_en = bool(data.get('keep_awake_enabled', False))
    cfg.set('KeepAwake_Enabled', str(ka_en))
    cfg.set('KeepAwake_Min_KB', str(ka_min_kb))
    cfg.set('KeepAwake_Display', str(bool(data.get('keep_awake_display', False))))
    cfg.set('Watchdog_Enabled', str(bool(data.get('watchdog_enabled', False))))
    cfg.set('Watchdog_Timeout_Min', str(wd_timeout))
    cfg.set('Watchdog_Exe_Path', wd_exe)
    if 'auto_recheck_enabled' in data:
        cfg.set('AutoRecheck_Enabled', str(bool(data.get('auto_recheck_enabled'))))
    if 'manifest_weekly' in data:
        cfg.set('Manifest_Weekly', str(bool(data.get('manifest_weekly'))))
    if 'max_channel_mbps' in data:
        cfg.set('Max_Channel_Mbps', str(max(10, min(100000, int(data.get('max_channel_mbps', 1000))))))
    cfg.save()

    if not ka_en:
        keep_awake.release()
    reset_qb_session()
    if old_snap_iv != snap_iv:
        summary_tracker.notify_interval_changed()

    app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Изменение настроек', f'host={new_host}:{new_p}', actor='admin')
    net_changed = (old_p != new_p) or (old_h != new_host)
    if net_changed:
        ws = current_app.config.get('WEB_SERVER')
        if ws:
            ws.restart(new_host, new_p)

    return jsonify({'success': True, 'port_changed': net_changed, 'new_port': new_p, 'message': 'Настройки сохранены! Веб-сервер перезапущен.' if net_changed else 'Настройки сохранены!'})

# web/routes/social_bp.py
import datetime
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from core.achievements import get_achievements_view

social_bp = Blueprint('social_bp', __name__)
_VALID_AUDIT_CATS = {'', 'auth', 'automation', 'merger', 'system'}


@social_bp.route('/api/tools/audit')
def api_tools_audit():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    cat = str(request.args.get('category', '')).strip().lower()
    if cat not in _VALID_AUDIT_CATS:
        cat = ''
    limit = max(10, min(200, request.args.get('limit', 100, type=int)))
    rows = app_state_db.get_audit_events(category=cat, limit=limit)
    for r in rows:
        ts = float(r.get('created_at') or 0)
        r['datetime'] = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(ts).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S') if ts else '—'
    return jsonify({'items': rows})


@social_bp.route('/api/achievements')
def api_achievements():
    return jsonify(get_achievements_view())

# web/routes/system_bp.py
import urllib.parse
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, Response, g

from utils.config import cfg
from core.version import APP_VERSION, GITHUB_RELEASES_URL
from core.updater import check_for_updates, download_and_apply_update, is_frozen_windows
from core.disaster_recovery import (
    execute_panic_killswitch, create_disaster_manifest,
    list_manifests, get_manifest_path
)

system_bp = Blueprint('system_bp', __name__)


def _actor_name():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    return (prof.get('login') or prof.get('name') or 'admin') if prof else 'admin'


@system_bp.route('/api/version')
@system_bp.route('/api/status')
def api_version():
    return jsonify({
        'name': 'tbr PRO',
        'version': APP_VERSION,
        'releases_url': GITHUB_RELEASES_URL,
        'can_auto_update': is_frozen_windows(),
        'autopilot_frozen_until': cfg.getint('Autopilot_Frozen_Until', 0),
        'role': g.get('role') or 'viewer'
    })


@system_bp.route('/api/update/check')

@system_bp.route('/api/update/apply', methods=['POST'])
def api_update_apply():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    ok, msg = download_and_apply_update(actor=_actor_name())
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@system_bp.route('/api/panic', methods=['POST'])
def api_panic_killswitch():

        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    kill_qb = bool(data.get('kill_qb', False))
    res = execute_panic_killswitch(kill_qb=kill_qb, actor=_actor_name())
    return jsonify(res)

def api_manifest_list():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return jsonify({

    })


@system_bp.route('/api/manifest/weekly', methods=['POST'])
def api_manifest_weekly():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    weekly = bool(data.get('weekly', False))
    cfg.set('Manifest_Weekly', str(weekly))
    cfg.save()
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'weekly': weekly})


@system_bp.route('/api/manifest/create', methods=['POST'])
def api_manifest_create():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    res = create_disaster_manifest(actor=_actor_name())
    return jsonify({'success': True, **res})


@system_bp.route('/api/manifest/download')
def api_manifest_download():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return "Только для Admin", 403
    fname = request.args.get('file', '')
    fpath = get_manifest_path(fname)
    if not fpath:
        return "Файл не найден", 404
    with open(fpath, 'rb') as f:
        data = f.read()
    clean_name = urllib.parse.quote(fname)
    return Response(
        data,
        mimetype='application/json',
        headers={'Content-Disposition': f"attachment; filename*=UTF-8''{clean_name}"}
    )

# web/routes/timers_bp.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g
from utils.config import cfg
from core.smart_timers import (
    get_freeze_status, freeze_active_torrents, unfreeze_torrents,
    get_snooze_status, start_snooze, cancel_snooze,
    list_expirations, set_expiration, remove_expiration
)
from core.smart_tagging import (
    list_tagging_rules, add_tagging_rule, toggle_tagging_rule, delete_tagging_rule
)
from core.torrent_targets import list_targets, set_target, delete_target
from core.dnd_digest import get_dnd_settings, get_digest_settings
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from web.security import HASH_RE

timers_bp = Blueprint('timers_bp', __name__)


def _actor():
    p = g.get('profile')
    return (p.get('login') or p.get('name') or 'admin') if p else 'admin'


@timers_bp.route('/api/freeze/status')
def api_freeze_status():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'frozen': False, 'count': 0}), 403
    return jsonify(get_freeze_status())


@timers_bp.route('/api/freeze/toggle', methods=['POST'])
def api_freeze_toggle():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    st = get_freeze_status()
    if st.get('frozen'):
        ok, msg, cnt = unfreeze_torrents(actor=_actor())
        return jsonify({'success': ok, 'frozen': False, 'count': cnt, 'message': msg})
    ok, msg, cnt = freeze_active_torrents(actor=_actor())
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'frozen': ok, 'count': cnt, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@timers_bp.route('/api/snooze/status')
def api_snooze_status():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'active': False}), 403
    return jsonify(get_snooze_status())


@timers_bp.route('/api/snooze/start', methods=['POST'])
def api_snooze_start():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    try:
        mins = int(data.get('minutes') or 30)
        if not (1 <= mins <= 1440):
            raise ValueError
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Укажите от 1 до 1440 минут'}), 400
    mode = str(data.get('mode', 'pause')).strip()
    ok, msg = start_snooze(mins, mode=mode, actor=_actor())
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@timers_bp.route('/api/snooze/cancel', methods=['POST'])
def api_snooze_cancel():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    ok, msg = cancel_snooze(actor=_actor(), auto_triggered=False)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg})


@timers_bp.route('/api/expirations')
def api_expirations_list():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'items': []}), 403
    return jsonify({'items': list_expirations()})


@timers_bp.route('/api/expirations/set', methods=['POST'])
def api_expirations_set():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    h = str(data.get('hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
    try:
        hours = float(data.get('hours') or 0)
        target_bytes = int(data.get('target_uploaded_bytes') or 0)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные числовые значения'}), 400
    delete_files = bool(data.get('delete_files', False))
    ok, msg = set_expiration(h, hours=hours, target_up_bytes=target_bytes, delete_files=delete_files, actor=_actor())
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@timers_bp.route('/api/expirations/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_expirations_delete():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    h = str((request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': remove_expiration(h)})


@timers_bp.route('/api/targets')
def api_targets_list():
    return jsonify({'targets': list_targets()})


@timers_bp.route('/api/targets/set', methods=['POST'])
def api_targets_set():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    h = str(data.get('hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
    try:
        tr = float(data.get('target_ratio') or 0)
        tb = int(data.get('target_bytes') or 0)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные числовые значения'}), 400
    ok, msg = set_target(h, target_ratio=tr, target_bytes=tb, actor=_actor())
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@timers_bp.route('/api/targets/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_targets_delete():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    h = str((request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(h):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный хэш'}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': delete_target(h)})


@timers_bp.route('/api/tagging/rules')
def api_tagging_list():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'rules': []}), 403
    return jsonify({'rules': list_tagging_rules()})


@timers_bp.route('/api/tagging/rules/add', methods=['POST'])
def api_tagging_add():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    rid = add_tagging_rule(
        data.get('name', ''), data.get('match_field', 'tracker'),
        data.get('pattern', ''), data.get('target_tag', '')
    )
    if not rid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Укажите паттерн и целевой тег'}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'id': rid})

        return jsonify({'success': False}), 403
    rid = (request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id')
    return jsonify({'success': bool(rid and toggle_tagging_rule(rid))})


@timers_bp.route('/api/tagging/rules/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_tagging_delete():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False}), 403
    rid = (request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id')
    return jsonify({'success': bool(rid and delete_tagging_rule(rid))})


@timers_bp.route('/api/notifications/schedule', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_notifications_schedule():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return jsonify({'dnd': get_dnd_settings(), 'digest': get_digest_settings()})

    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    dnd = data.get('dnd') or {}
    dig = data.get('digest') or {}

    cfg.set('DND_Enabled', str(bool(dnd.get('enabled', False))))
    cfg.set('DND_Start', str(dnd.get('start', '23:00'))[:5] or '23:00')
    cfg.set('DND_End', str(dnd.get('end', '08:00'))[:5] or '08:00')
    days = dnd.get('days') if isinstance(dnd.get('days'), list) else [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]
    cfg.set('DND_Days', ','.join(str(int(d)) for d in days if str(d).isdigit() and 0 <= int(d) <= 6))

    d_mode = str(dig.get('mode', 'off'))
    if d_mode not in ('off', 'daily', 'weekly'):
        d_mode = 'off'
    cfg.set('Digest_Mode', d_mode)
    cfg.set('Digest_Hour', str(max(0, min(23, int(dig.get('hour', 21) or 21)))))
    cfg.set('Digest_Weekday', str(max(0, min(6, int(dig.get('weekday', 6) or 6)))))
    cfg.save()

    app_state_db.add_audit_event('automation', 'Расписание DND/Дайджеста', f'dnd={dnd.get("enabled")}, digest={d_mode}', actor=_actor())
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Расписание DND и дайджеста сохранено!'})

# web/routes/tools_bp.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g
from core.orphan_scanner import get_download_roots, scan_orphan_entries, delete_orphan_entries
from core.private_vault import get_vault_settings, save_vault_settings
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db

tools_bp = Blueprint('tools_bp', __name__)


def _actor_name():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    return (prof.get('login') or prof.get('name') or 'admin') if prof else 'admin'


@tools_bp.route('/api/tools/orphans/roots')
def api_orphans_roots():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return jsonify({'roots': get_download_roots()})


@tools_bp.route('/api/tools/orphans/scan', methods=['POST'])
def api_orphans_scan():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    custom_roots = data.get('roots')
    if custom_roots is not None and not isinstance(custom_roots, list):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Ожидался список папок'}), 400
    res = scan_orphan_entries(custom_roots=custom_roots)
    return jsonify({'success': True, **res})


@tools_bp.route('/api/tools/orphans/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_orphans_delete():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    paths = data.get('paths')
    confirmed = bool(data.get('confirmed', False))
    if not confirmed or not isinstance(paths, list) or not paths:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Требуется явное подтверждение и непустой список путей'}), 400
    ok, deleted, errors = delete_orphan_entries(paths, actor=_actor_name())
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'deleted': deleted, 'errors': errors}), (200 if deleted or ok else 400)


@tools_bp.route('/api/vault/settings', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_vault_settings():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return jsonify(get_vault_settings())
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    saved = save_vault_settings(data.get('tags', ''), data.get('aggregate_mode', 'exclude'))
    app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Private Vault обновлён', f"tags={','.join(saved['tags'])}", actor=_actor_name())
    return jsonify({'success': True, **saved})

# web/routes/torrents_bp.py
import time
import urllib.parse
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g

from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size
from core.torrent_parser import get_all_torrents, force_clear_cache
from core.qb_api import (
    detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, send_qb_command,
    add_torrent_tags, remove_torrent_tags, delete_torrents,
    is_qb_running, close_qbittorrent, launch_qbittorrent, scan_dead_torrents
)
from core.autostart import set_autostart, is_autostart_enabled
from core.fastresume_merger import run_fastresume_merge, execute_source_deletion, VALID_DELETE_MODES
from core.torrent_diff import compute_torrent_diff, find_smart_merge_candidates
from core.private_vault import get_hidden_hashes, filter_torrents_for_role
from core.torrent_targets import list_targets
from core.health_score import enrich_live_metrics
from core.tracker_inspector import inspect_tracker_errors
from core.app_state_db import app_state_db
from web.security import HASH_RE

torrents_bp = Blueprint('torrents_bp', __name__)


def _build_torrents_list(ip, port):
    data_dict = get_all_torrents(use_cache=True)
    live_info = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    targets = list_targets()

    result = []
    for t_id, static in data_dict.items():
        live = live_info.get(t_id) or live_info.get(t_id.lower()) or live_info.get(t_id.upper()) or {}
        size = live.get('total_size', static.get('size', 1))
        uploaded = live.get('uploaded', static.get('uploaded', 0))
        ratio = round(uploaded / size, 4) if size > 0 else 0.0
        state = live.get('state', 'seeding' if static.get('active_seconds', 0) > 0 else 'unknown')
        save_path = live.get('save_path', static.get('save_path', ''))
        content_path = live.get('content_path', '')
        tags = str(live.get('tags', '') or '')

        tracker_host = ''
        tr_url = str(live.get('tracker', '') or '')
        if not tr_url and static.get('trackers'):
            tr_url = str(static['trackers'][0] or '')
        if tr_url:
            tracker_host = urllib.parse.urlparse(tr_url).netloc.lower()

        name = static.get('name', '')
        if not name or name.lower() == t_id.lower() or len(name) in (40, 64):
            if live.get('name'):
                name = live['name']
        if not name:
            name = t_id

        item = {
            'hash': t_id, 'name': name, 'size': size, 'size_fmt': format_file_size(size),
            'uploaded': uploaded, 'uploaded_fmt': format_file_size(uploaded),
            'ratio': ratio, 'state': state, 'save_path': save_path, 'content_path': content_path,
            'tags': tags, 'tracker': tracker_host,
            'active_seconds': static.get('active_seconds', 0)
        }
        enrich_live_metrics(item, live, targets.get(t_id.lower()))
        result.append(item)
    result.sort(key=lambda x: str(x['name']).lower())
    return result, live_info


@torrents_bp.route('/api/qb/status')
def api_qb_status():
    running = is_qb_running()
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    return jsonify({'running': running, 'ip': ip, 'port': port})

def api_qb_start():
    ok = launch_qbittorrent()
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': 'qBittorrent запущен' if ok else 'Не удалось запустить'})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/qb/stop', methods=['POST'])
def api_qb_stop():
    ok = close_qbittorrent()
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': 'qBittorrent остановлен' if ok else 'Не удалось остановить'})

    close_qbittorrent()
    time.sleep(1.5)
    ok = launch_qbittorrent()
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': 'qBittorrent перезапущен'})

    ok, msg = set_autostart(not curr)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'enabled': not curr if ok else curr, 'message': msg})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents')
def api_torrents():
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    result, live_info = _build_torrents_list(ip, port)
    role = g.get('role') or 'viewer'
    hidden = get_hidden_hashes(live_info)
    result = filter_torrents_for_role(result, role, hidden)
    return jsonify({'torrents': result, 'qb_connected': bool(port), 'qb_running': is_qb_running()})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents/tracker_errors')
def api_torrents_tracker_errors():
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    live_info = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}
    force = request.args.get('force', '0') == '1'
    err_map = inspect_tracker_errors(ip, port, live_info, force=force)
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        hidden = get_hidden_hashes(live_info)
        if hidden:
            err_map = {k: v for k, v in err_map.items() if k not in hidden}
    return jsonify({'errors': err_map})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents/refresh', methods=['POST'])
def api_torrents_refresh():
    force_clear_cache()
    return jsonify({'status': 'ok'})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents/scan_dead', methods=['POST'])
def api_torrents_scan_dead():
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if not port:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен'}), 400
    res = scan_dead_torrents(ip, port)
    if res.get('success'):
        for item in res.get('items', []):
            item['size_fmt'] = format_file_size(item.get('size', 0))
    return jsonify(res)


@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents/action', methods=['POST'])
def api_torrent_action():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    action = data.get('action')
    t_hash = data.get('hash', '')
    tag = str(data.get('tag', '')).strip()
    if action not in ('stop', 'start', 'setForceStart', 'addTags', 'removeTags'):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Неизвестная команда'}), 400

    hashes = [h.strip() for h in str(t_hash).split('|') if h.strip()]
    if not hashes or not all(HASH_RE.fullmatch(h) for h in hashes):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные хэши'}), 400

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if not port:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен'}), 400

    joined = '|'.join(hashes)
    if action == 'addTags':
        return jsonify({'success': add_torrent_tags(ip, port, joined, tag)})
    if action == 'removeTags':
        return jsonify({'success': remove_torrent_tags(ip, port, joined, tag)})
    return jsonify({'success': send_qb_command(ip, port, action, joined)})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_torrents_delete():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    t_hash = str(data.get('hash', '')).strip()
    delete_files = bool(data.get('delete_files', False))
    hashes = [h.strip() for h in t_hash.split('|') if h.strip()]
    if not hashes or not all(HASH_RE.fullmatch(h) for h in hashes):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные хэши'}), 400
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if not port:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен'}), 400
    ok = delete_torrents(ip, port, '|'.join(hashes), delete_files=delete_files)
    if ok:
        force_clear_cache()
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('system', 'Удаление торрента', f'count={len(hashes)}, files={delete_files}', actor='admin')
    return jsonify({'success': ok})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents/action_all', methods=['POST'])
def api_torrents_action_all():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    action = data.get('action', 'stop')
    if action not in ('stop', 'start'):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Неизвестная команда'}), 400
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if not port:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent не запущен'}), 400
    ok = send_qb_command(ip, port, action, 'all')
    msg = "Все раздачи поставлены на паузу" if action == 'stop' else "Все раздачи возобновлены"
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/merger/diff')
def api_merger_diff():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    old_hash = str(request.args.get('old_hash', '')).strip()
    new_hash = str(request.args.get('new_hash', '')).strip()
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(old_hash) or not HASH_RE.fullmatch(new_hash) or old_hash.lower() == new_hash.lower():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Укажите два разных корректных хэша'}), 400
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    return jsonify(compute_torrent_diff(ip, port, old_hash, new_hash))


@torrents_bp.route('/api/merger/candidates')
def api_merger_candidates():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'candidates': []}), 403
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    items, _ = _build_torrents_list(ip, port)
    return jsonify({'candidates': find_smart_merge_candidates(items)})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/merger/run', methods=['POST'])
def api_merger_run():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    old_hash = str(data.get('old_hash', '')).strip()
    new_hash = str(data.get('new_hash', '')).strip()
    delete_mode = str(data.get('delete_mode', 'none')).strip()
    if delete_mode not in VALID_DELETE_MODES:
        delete_mode = 'none'

    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(old_hash) or not HASH_RE.fullmatch(new_hash) or old_hash.lower() == new_hash.lower():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'log': ['❌ Некорректные или одинаковые хэши торрентов!']}), 400

    ok, log, confirm_info, code = run_fastresume_merge(old_hash, new_hash, delete_mode=delete_mode)
    if ok:
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('merger', 'Перенос .fastresume', f'{old_hash[:8]} -> {new_hash[:8]} (mode={delete_mode})', actor='admin')
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'log': log, 'needs_delete_confirm': bool(ok and confirm_info), 'confirm_info': confirm_info}), code


@torrents_bp.route('/api/merger/delete_source', methods=['POST'])
def api_merger_delete_source():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    old_hash, new_hash = str(data.get('old_hash', '')).strip(), str(data.get('new_hash', '')).strip()
    delete_mode, confirmed = str(data.get('delete_mode', '')).strip(), bool(data.get('confirmed', False))

    if not confirmed or delete_mode not in ('safe', 'force'):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'log': ['❌ Удаление не подтверждено!']}), 400
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(old_hash) or not HASH_RE.fullmatch(new_hash) or old_hash.lower() == new_hash.lower():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'log': ['❌ Некорректные или одинаковые хэши!']}), 400

    ok, log = execute_source_deletion(old_hash, new_hash, delete_mode)
    if ok:
        app_state_db.add_audit_event('merger', 'Удалён исходник', f'{old_hash[:8]} (mode={delete_mode})', actor='admin')
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'log': log}), (200 if ok else 400)

# web/routes/widget_bp.py
import io
import json
from PIL import Image
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, Response, g
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.profiles_db import profiles_db
from core.widget_service import get_widget_summary, render_widget_chart_png, get_pwa_icon_png

widget_bp = Blueprint('widget_bp', __name__)
_FAVICON_CACHE = None


@widget_bp.route('/favicon.ico')
def favicon_ico():
    global _FAVICON_CACHE
    if _FAVICON_CACHE is None:
        img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(get_pwa_icon_png(64)))
        buf = io.BytesIO()
        img.save(buf, format='ICO', sizes=[(32, 32), (64, 64)])
        _FAVICON_CACHE = buf.getvalue()
    return Response(_FAVICON_CACHE, mimetype='image/x-icon', headers={'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=86400'})


@widget_bp.route('/manifest.json')
def pwa_manifest():
    manifest = {
        "name": "tbr PRO — qBittorrent",
        "short_name": "tbr PRO",
        "start_url": "/",
        "display": "standalone",
        "background_color": "#121214",
        "theme_color": "#1a1a20",
        "icons": [
            {"src": "/icon-192.png", "sizes": "192x192", "type": "image/png", "purpose": "any maskable"},
            {"src": "/icon-512.png", "sizes": "512x512", "type": "image/png", "purpose": "any maskable"}
        ]
    }
    return Response(json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False), mimetype='application/manifest+json', headers={'Cache-Control': 'no-store'})


@widget_bp.route('/icon-192.png')
def pwa_icon_192():
    return Response(get_pwa_icon_png(192), mimetype='image/png')

def pwa_icon_512():
    return Response(get_pwa_icon_png(512), mimetype='image/png')


@widget_bp.route('/apple-touch-icon.png')
def pwa_apple_icon():
    return Response(get_pwa_icon_png(180), mimetype='image/png')


@widget_bp.route('/api/widget')
def api_widget():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    ui_settings = profiles_db.get_ui_settings(prof['id'] if prof else 0)
    top_period = request.args.get('top_period')
    top_scale = request.args.get('top_scale')
    return jsonify(get_widget_summary(summary_tracker.db, ui_settings=ui_settings, top_period=top_period, top_scale=top_scale))


@widget_bp.route('/api/widget/chart.png')
def api_widget_chart_png():
    hours = request.args.get('hours', 24, type=int)
    w = request.args.get('w', 560, type=int)
    h = request.args.get('h', 200, type=int)
    chart_type = request.args.get('type', 'speed')
    prof = g.get('profile')
    ui_settings = profiles_db.get_ui_settings(prof['id'] if prof else 0)
    png_bytes = render_widget_chart_png(summary_tracker.db, ui_settings, hours=hours, width=w, height=h, chart_type=chart_type)
    return Response(png_bytes, mimetype='image/png', headers={'Cache-Control': 'no-store'})

# web/security.py
import os
import re
import hmac
import time
import secrets
import threading
import ipaddress
import urllib.parse
from flask import request, jsonify, redirect, session, g
from utils.helpers import LOG_FOLDER
from core.summary_tracker import AUTO_FOLDER, MANUAL_FOLDER
from core.profiles_db import profiles_db

HASH_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-fA-F]{40}(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{24})?$')

_VIEWER_BLOCKED_GET_PREFIXES = ('/api/settings', '/api/automation', '/api/logs', '/api/profiles', '/api/network')
_PUBLIC_ASSET_PATHS = ('/favicon.ico', '/manifest.json', '/icon-192.png', '/icon-512.png', '/apple-touch-icon.png')

_RATE_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_FAILED_LOGINS = {}  # { ip: [timestamps] }


def check_rate_limit(ip, max_attempts=10, window_sec=300):
    now = time.time()
    with _RATE_LOCK:
        hist = [t for t in _FAILED_LOGINS.get(ip, []) if now - t < window_sec]
        _FAILED_LOGINS[ip] = hist
        return len(hist) < max_attempts


def record_failed_login(ip):
    now = time.time()
    with _RATE_LOCK:
        hist = [t for t in _FAILED_LOGINS.get(ip, []) if now - t < 300]
        hist.append(now)
        _FAILED_LOGINS[ip] = hist


def get_csrf_token():
    if 'csrf_token' not in session:
        session['csrf_token'] = secrets.token_hex(32)
    return session['csrf_token']


def is_allowed_qb_ip(ip_str):
    s = (ip_str or '').strip().lower()
    if s in ('', 'localhost'):
        return True
    try:
        ip_obj = ipaddress.ip_address(s)
        return ip_obj.is_loopback or ip_obj.is_private or (ip_obj in ipaddress.ip_network('100.64.0.0/10'))
    except ValueError:
        return False


def resolve_log_path(filename):
    if not filename or not isinstance(filename, str):
        return None
    clean = os.path.basename(filename.replace('\\', '/'))
    allowed_dirs = [LOG_FOLDER, AUTO_FOLDER, MANUAL_FOLDER]
    for directory in allowed_dirs:
        try:
            norm_dir = os.path.abspath(directory)
            full_path = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(norm_dir, clean))
            if os.path.commonpath([norm_dir, full_path]) == norm_dir and os.path.isfile(full_path):
                return full_path
        except ValueError:
            continue
    return None


def _resolve_request_profile(is_exposed):
    auth_hdr = request.headers.get('Authorization', '').strip()
    token = auth_hdr[7:].strip() if auth_hdr.lower().startswith('bearer ') else request.headers.get('X-Auth-Token', '').strip()
    if token:
        prof = profiles_db.authenticate_api_token(token)
        if prof:
            return prof, True

    pid = session.get('profile_id')
    if pid:
        prof = profiles_db.get_by_id(pid)
        if prof:
            return prof, False

    if session.get('authenticated') or not is_exposed:
        return profiles_db.get_default_admin(), False

    return None, False


def register_security(app):
    @app.before_request
    def security_middleware():
        if request.path.startswith('/static/') or request.path in _PUBLIC_ASSET_PATHS:
            return None

        if request.path.startswith('/invite/') or request.path == '/api/auth/exchange':
            return None

        is_exposed = app.config.get('IS_EXPOSED', False)
        prof, used_bearer = _resolve_request_profile(is_exposed)
        g.profile = prof
        g.role = prof['role'] if prof else None

        if request.path in ('/login', '/logout'):
            if request.method == 'POST' and request.path == '/login':
                expected_token = session.get('csrf_token')
                sent_token = request.form.get('csrf_token')
                if not (expected_token and sent_token and hmac.compare_digest(str(expected_token), str(sent_token))):
                    return jsonify({'error': 'CSRF ошибка'}), 403
            return None

        if is_exposed and not prof:
            if request.path.startswith('/api/'):
                return jsonify({'error': 'Требуется авторизация'}), 401
            return redirect('/login')

        if g.role == 'viewer':
            if request.path == '/api/profile/password' and request.method == 'POST':
                pass
            elif request.method in ('POST', 'PUT', 'DELETE', 'PATCH'):
                return jsonify({'error': 'Недостаточно прав (режим Viewer — только чтение)'}), 403
            elif any(request.path.startswith(p) for p in _VIEWER_BLOCKED_GET_PREFIXES):
                return jsonify({'error': 'Доступ закрыт для роли Viewer'}), 403

        if request.method == 'POST' and not used_bearer:
            expected_token = session.get('csrf_token')
            sent_token = request.headers.get('X-CSRF-Token') or request.form.get('csrf_token')
            origin = request.headers.get('Origin') or request.headers.get('Referer')
            allowed_hosts = {request.host}
            for hdr in ('X-Forwarded-Host', 'X-Original-Host'):
                if request.headers.get(hdr):
                    allowed_hosts.add(request.headers.get(hdr).split(',')[0].strip())

            origin_ok = True
            if origin:
                parsed = urllib.parse.urlparse(origin)
                if parsed.netloc and parsed.netloc not in allowed_hosts:
                    origin_ok = False

            token_ok = bool(expected_token and sent_token and hmac.compare_digest(str(expected_token), str(sent_token)))
            if not token_ok or not origin_ok:
                return jsonify({'error': 'CSRF проверка не пройдена. Обновите страницу.'}), 403

// web/static/analytics.js
let lastForecastData = null;
let chartDaily = null;
let chartTop5 = null;
let chartHourly = null;

async function loadAnalyticsTab() {
    syncAnalyticsControlsFromUi();
    loadAnalyticsCharts();
    if (typeof loadAnalyticsInsights === 'function') loadAnalyticsInsights(false);
    initDayLogs();
    if (!rawSummaryItems.length) await loadSummary();
    else populateAnalyticsSelectors();
}

function syncAnalyticsControlsFromUi() {
    const ui = typeof getUiSettings === 'function' ? getUiSettings() : {};
    const ch = ui.charts || {};
    const hSel = document.getElementById('chart-hourly-hours');
    if (hSel) hSel.value = String(ch.hourly_hours || 24);

    const yAuto = document.getElementById('chart-hourly-ymin-auto');
    const yInp = document.getElementById('chart-hourly-ymin');
    const hasYMin = ch.hourly_y_min !== null && ch.hourly_y_min !== undefined && ch.hourly_y_min !== '';
    if (yAuto && yInp) {
        yAuto.checked = !hasYMin;
        yInp.disabled = !hasYMin;
        yInp.value = hasYMin ? ch.hourly_y_min : '';
    }

    const topInp = document.getElementById('chart-topn-val');
    if (topInp) topInp.value = Math.max(1, Math.min(25, parseInt(ch.top_n) || 5));
    const perSel = document.getElementById('chart-top-period');
    if (perSel) perSel.value = ch.top_period || '1d';
    const scSel = document.getElementById('chart-top-scale');
    if (scSel) scSel.value = ch.top_scale || 'lin';
}

function onHourlyWindowChange() {
    const ui = getUiSettings(); ui.charts = ui.charts || {};
    ui.charts.hourly_hours = parseInt(document.getElementById('chart-hourly-hours').value) || 24;
    persistUiSettings(ui);
}

    ui.charts.hourly_y_min = isAuto ? null : Math.max(0, parseFloat(inp.value) || 0);
    persistUiSettings(ui);
}

    const ui = getUiSettings(); ui.charts = ui.charts || {};
    ui.charts.hourly_y_min = Number.isFinite(val) && val >= 0 ? val : 0;
    persistUiSettings(ui);
}
