function _computeHourMarkerFlags(meta) {
    if (!Array.isArray(meta) || !meta.length) return [];
    const bestByHour = new Map();
    meta.forEach((m, idx) => {
        if (!m || !m.ts) return;
        const dt = new Date(m.ts * 1000);
        const hKey = `${dt.getFullYear()}-${dt.getMonth()}-${dt.getDate()}-${dt.getHours()}`;
        const distSec = dt.getMinutes() * 60 + dt.getSeconds();
        const prev = bestByHour.get(hKey);
        if (!prev || distSec < prev.dist) bestByHour.set(hKey, { idx, dist: distSec });
    });
    const flags = new Array(meta.length).fill(false);
    bestByHour.forEach(v => { flags[v.idx] = true; });
    return flags;
}

function _buildTwoColorGradient(canvasId, c1Hex, c2Hex, opacity, isHorizontal = false) {
    const canvas = document.getElementById(canvasId);
    if (!canvas) return c1Hex;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const rgb1 = typeof hexToRgb === 'function' ? hexToRgb(c1Hex) : { r: 46, g: 204, b: 113 };
    const rgb2 = typeof hexToRgb === 'function' ? hexToRgb(c2Hex || c1Hex) : rgb1;
    const op = opacity ?? 0.75;
    const grad = isHorizontal
        ? ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, canvas.clientWidth || 600, 0)
        : ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, canvas.clientHeight || 220);
    grad.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${rgb1.r},${rgb1.g},${rgb1.b},${op})`);
    grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${rgb2.r},${rgb2.g},${rgb2.b},${Math.max(0.08, op * 0.45)})`);
    return grad;
}

async function loadAnalyticsCharts() {
    if (typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    try {
        const ui = typeof getUiSettings === 'function' ? getUiSettings() : {};
        const chCfg = ui.charts || {};
        const winHours = parseInt(chCfg.hourly_hours) || 24;
        const topN = Math.max(1, Math.min(25, parseInt(chCfg.top_n) || 5));
        const topPeriod = chCfg.top_period || '1d';
        const topScale = chCfg.top_scale || 'lin';
        const yMin = (chCfg.hourly_y_min !== null && chCfg.hourly_y_min !== undefined && chCfg.hourly_y_min !== '')
            ? parseFloat(chCfg.hourly_y_min) : null;

        const res = await api(`/api/analytics/charts?hours=${winHours}&top_n=${topN}&top_period=${encodeURIComponent(topPeriod)}`);
        const d = await res.json();

        const cs = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
        const tickColor = cs.getPropertyValue('--text-muted').trim() || '#95a5a6';
        const gridColor = cs.getPropertyValue('--border').trim() || 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';

        // 1. График по дням (Двухцветный вертикальный градиент)
        const cDaily = chCfg.daily || { color: '#2ecc71', color2: '#10b981', opacity: 0.75 };
        const dailyBg = cDaily.gradient !== false
            ? _buildTwoColorGradient('chart-daily', cDaily.color, cDaily.color2, cDaily.opacity, false)
            : cDaily.color;

        if (chartDaily) chartDaily.destroy();
        chartDaily = new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-daily'), {
            type: 'bar',
            data: {
                labels: d.daily?.labels || [],
                datasets: [{
                    data: d.daily?.values || [],
                    backgroundColor: dailyBg,
                    borderColor: cDaily.color,
                    borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 4, minBarLength: 2, barPercentage: 0.65
                }]
            },
            options: {
                responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
                scales: {
                    x: { ticks: { color: tickColor, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: gridColor } },
                    y: { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { color: tickColor, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: gridColor } }
                },
                plugins: {
                    legend: { display: false },
                    tooltip: { callbacks: { label: ctx => `${currentLang === 'en' ? 'Daily Upload' : 'Отдано за день'}: ${ctx.parsed.y} GiB` } }
                }
            }
        });

        // 2. Топ-N раздач + Столбик «Все остальные»
        const rawTopVals = [...(d.top5?.values || [])];
        const topLabels = [...(d.top5?.labels || [])];
        const topFullLabels = [...(d.top5?.full_labels || d.top5?.labels || [])];
        const others = d.top5?.others;
        const hasOthers = Boolean(others && (others.gb > 0 || others.count > 0));

        if (hasOthers) {
            const isEn = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'en');
            topLabels.push(isEn ? `Others (${others.count})` : `Все остальные (${others.count})`);
            topFullLabels.push(isEn ? `All other torrents (${others.count} pcs)` : `Все остальные раздачи (${others.count} шт.)`);
            rawTopVals.push(others.gb);
        }

        const topWrap = document.getElementById('chart-top5-wrap');
        const actualCount = topLabels.length || topN;
        if (topWrap) topWrap.style.height = `${Math.max(210, actualCount * 32 + 48)}px`;

        const isLog = (topScale === 'log10');
        const plotTopVals = isLog ? rawTopVals.map(v => Math.log10(1 + Math.max(0, v))) : rawTopVals;
        const maxReal = Math.max(1, ...rawTopVals);

        const topXScale = { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { color: tickColor, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: gridColor } };
        if (isLog) {
            const pwrTicks = [0, 1, 10, 100, 1000, 10000, 100000].filter(p => p === 0 || p <= maxReal * 2.5);
            if (pwrTicks.length < 2) pwrTicks.push(1, 10);
            topXScale.afterBuildTicks = axis => { axis.ticks = pwrTicks.map(p => ({ value: Math.log10(1 + p) })); };
            topXScale.ticks.callback = val => { const orig = Math.round(Math.pow(10, val) - 1); return orig <= 0 ? '0' : String(orig); };
        }

        const cTop = chCfg.top5 || { color: '#ff9f1c', color2: '#ff4d6d', opacity: 0.8 };
        const topGrad = _buildTwoColorGradient('chart-top5', cTop.color, cTop.color2, cTop.opacity, true);
        const topBgColors = plotTopVals.map((_, idx) => (hasOthers && idx === plotTopVals.length - 1) ? 'rgba(100, 116, 139, 0.55)' : topGrad);

        if (chartTop5) chartTop5.destroy();
        chartTop5 = new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-top5'), {
            type: 'bar',
            data: {
                labels: topLabels,
                datasets: [{
                    data: plotTopVals,
                    backgroundColor: topBgColors,
                    borderColor: cTop.color,
                    borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 4, minBarLength: 2, barPercentage: 0.72
                }]
            },
            options: {
                responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, indexAxis: 'y',
                scales: { x: topXScale, y: { ticks: { color: tickColor, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: gridColor } } },
                plugins: {
                    legend: { display: false },
                    tooltip: {
                        callbacks: {
                            title: items => (items.length ? (topFullLabels[items[0].dataIndex] || items[0].label) : ''),
                            label: ctx => {
                                const val = rawTopVals[ctx.dataIndex] ?? 0;
                                if (hasOthers && ctx.dataIndex === rawTopVals.length - 1) {
                                    const isEn = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'en');
                                    return `${isEn ? 'All other torrents' : 'Все остальные раздачи'} (${others.count} шт.): ${val} GiB`;
                                }
                                return `${currentLang === 'en' ? 'Uploaded' : 'Отдано'}: ${val} GiB`;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });

        // 3. График скорости нагрузки (Двухцветный вертикальный градиент)
        const cHour = chCfg.hourly || { color: '#00f5d4', color2: '#9b5de5', opacity: 0.35 };
        const hourlyBg = _buildTwoColorGradient('chart-hourly', cHour.color, cHour.color2, cHour.opacity, false);
        const hourlyMeta = d.hourly?.meta || d.detailed || [];
        const hourFlags = _computeHourMarkerFlags(hourlyMeta);
        const yScaleOpts = { ticks: { color: tickColor, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: gridColor } };
        if (yMin !== null && Number.isFinite(yMin)) yScaleOpts.min = yMin;
        else yScaleOpts.beginAtZero = true;

        if (chartHourly) chartHourly.destroy();
        chartHourly = new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-hourly'), {
            type: 'line',
            data: {
                labels: d.hourly?.labels || [],
                datasets: [{
                    data: d.hourly?.values || [],
                    borderColor: cHour.color,
                    backgroundColor: hourlyBg,
                    fill: true, tension: 0.35, cubicInterpolationMode: 'monotone', clip: true,
                    pointRadius: ctx => (hourFlags[ctx.dataIndex] ? (winHours <= 24 ? 3.5 : 1.5) : 0),
                    pointHoverRadius: 5, pointHitRadius: 10
                }]
            },
            options: {
                responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
                scales: { x: { ticks: { color: tickColor, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: gridColor } }, y: yScaleOpts },
                plugins: {
                    legend: { display: false },
                    tooltip: {
                        callbacks: {
                            label: function(ctx) {
                                const m = hourlyMeta[ctx.dataIndex];
                                const isEn = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'en');
                                const spd = (ctx.parsed.y !== undefined ? ctx.parsed.y : (m?.speed_mbs || 0)).toFixed(2);
                                const speedLine = `⚡ ${isEn ? 'Speed' : 'Скорость'}: ${spd} MiB/s`;
                                if (!m || !m.delta_fmt) return speedLine;
                                const intTxt = m.interval_min > 0 ? (isEn ? ` (${m.interval_min}m)` : ` (${m.interval_min}м)`) : '';
                                return [speedLine, `📦 ${isEn ? 'Uploaded' : 'Отдано'}: +${m.delta_fmt}${intTxt}`];
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
}

function populateAnalyticsSelectors() {
    const fill = (selId) => {
        const sel = document.getElementById(selId);
        if (!sel) return;
        const cur = sel.value;
        sel.innerHTML = '';
        const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
        const def = document.createElement('option');
        def.value = 'all'; def.textContent = t('whole_seedbox');
        frag.appendChild(def);
        rawSummaryItems.forEach(item => {
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = item.hash_id; opt.textContent = `📦 ${item.name}`;
            frag.appendChild(opt);
        });
        sel.appendChild(frag);
        if (cur) sel.value = cur;
    };
    fill('smart-calc-target-type');
    fill('history-torrent-select');
}

function onSmartCalcTargetChange() {
    const val = document.getElementById('smart-calc-target-type').value;
    document.getElementById('smart-calc-goal-label').textContent = val === 'all' ? t('goal_lbl_all') : t('goal_lbl_one');
    document.getElementById('smart-calc-goal').placeholder = val === 'all' ? t('goal_ph_all') : t('goal_ph_one');
}

async function runSmartCalc() {
    const term = document.getElementById('smart-calc-terminal');
    const targetVal = document.getElementById('smart-calc-target-type').value;
    const isAll = (targetVal === 'all');
    const goal = document.getElementById('smart-calc-goal').value.trim();
    const speed = document.getElementById('smart-calc-speed').value.trim();
    if (!goal) { term.textContent = t('err_no_goal'); return; }
    term.textContent = t('calc_running');
    let d;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/smart_calc', 'POST', { target_type: isAll ? 'all' : 'torrent', hash: isAll ? '' : targetVal, target: goal, speed });
        if (!res.ok) { term.textContent = t('http_err', { status: res.status }); return; }
        d = await res.json();
    } catch (err) { term.textContent = t('net_err', { msg: err.message }); return; }

    const btnTable = document.getElementById('btn-toggle-forecast-table');
    const btnCsv = document.getElementById('btn-export-forecast-csv');
    const wrapTable = document.getElementById('smart-res-table-wrap');
    if (!d.success) {
        btnTable.classList.add('hidden'); btnCsv.classList.add('hidden'); wrapTable.classList.add('hidden');
        term.textContent = d.need_manual ? `=================================================================\n${t('need_manual_msg', { avail: d.days_available, req: d.req_days })}\n=================================================================` : t('err_prefix', { msg: d.error });
        return;
    }

    lastForecastData = d;
    btnCsv.classList.remove('hidden');
    if (d.achieved) { btnTable.classList.add('hidden'); term.textContent = t('goal_achieved', { name: d.name, cur: d.current_up_fmt, tgt: d.target_fmt }); return; }

    btnTable.classList.remove('hidden');
    const modeTxt = d.is_manual ? t('mode_manual') : t('mode_smart', { w: d.weights_desc });
    let out = `=================================================================\n${t('res_header')}\n${t('obj_lbl')}        ${d.name}\n${t('up_now_lbl')} ${d.current_up_fmt}  -->  ${t('goal_lbl')} ${d.target_fmt}\n${t('rem_lbl')}      ${d.remaining_fmt}\n`;
    if (!d.is_seedbox) out += `${t('ratio_lbl', { cur: d.ratio_current, nxt: d.ratio_after_24h })}\n`;
    out += `-----------------------------------------------------------------\n${t('mode_lbl')} ${modeTxt}\n${t('f24_lbl')} +${d.forecast_24h_vol_fmt}\n${t('eta_lbl')}   ${d.eta_str}\n=================================================================`;
    term.textContent = out;

    const tbody = document.getElementById('smart-res-tbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    (d.forecast_rows || []).forEach(r => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        tr.innerHTML = `<td>${escapeHtml(r.date)}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(r.hour)}</strong></td><td class="purple-txt" style="font-weight:700;">+${escapeHtml(trBackend(r.volume_mb))}</td><td class="blue-txt" style="font-weight:600;">${escapeHtml(r.speed_fmt)}</td><td class="muted-text" style="font-size:11px;">${escapeHtml(r.basis)}</td>`;
        frag.appendChild(tr);
    });
    tbody.appendChild(frag);
}

function toggleForecastTable() {
    const isHidden = document.getElementById('smart-res-table-wrap').classList.toggle('hidden');
    document.getElementById('btn-toggle-forecast-table').textContent = isHidden ? t('btn_hourly_show') : t('btn_hourly_hide');
}

async function exportForecastCSV() {
    if (!lastForecastData) return;
    const res = await api('/api/smart_calc/export', 'POST', { forecast_data: lastForecastData });
    if (!res.ok) return alert(t('export_err'));
    const blob = await res.blob();
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = `forecast_24h_${new Date().toLocaleString('sv-SE').replace(/[- :]/g, '_')}.csv`;
    a.click();
}

// web/static/analytics_insights.js
let chartPeerClients = null;
let chartTrackers = null;
let chartTorrentPersonal = null;
let _inspHash = null;

async function loadAnalyticsInsights(forceClients = false) {
    try {
        const trPer = document.getElementById('chart-trackers-period')?.value || '7d';
        const res = await api(`/api/analytics/insights?tracker_period=${encodeURIComponent(trPer)}&force_clients=${forceClients ? '1' : '0'}`);
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        _renderRecords(d.records || {});
        _renderThrottleBadge(d.throttle || {});
        _renderHeatmap365(d.heatmap || []);
        _renderPeerClientsChart(d.clients || {});
        _renderTrackersChart(d.trackers || {});
    } catch (e) {
        console.error(e);
    }
}

function _renderRecords(r) {
    const pctFmt = p => (p === null || p === undefined) ? 'мало данных' : `${p >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${p}% к пред.`;
    document.getElementById('rec-today-val').textContent = r.today_fmt || '—';
    document.getElementById('rec-today-cmp').textContent = `Вчера: ${r.yesterday_fmt || '—'} (${pctFmt(r.day_pct)})`;
    document.getElementById('rec-week-val').textContent = r.this_week_fmt || '—';
    document.getElementById('rec-week-cmp').textContent = `Прошлая: ${r.prev_week_fmt || '—'} (${pctFmt(r.week_pct)})`;
    document.getElementById('rec-peak-val').textContent = r.peak_speed_fmt || '—';
    const cleanDate = String(r.peak_speed_date || '').replace('|', '').trim();
    document.getElementById('rec-peak-date').textContent = cleanDate || '—';
    document.getElementById('rec-best-val').textContent = r.best_day_fmt || '—';
    document.getElementById('rec-best-date').textContent = r.best_day_date || '—';
}

async function recalculateRecords() {
    if (!confirm('Пересчитать рекорды? Любые аномальные скачки скорости будут отброшены по порогу канала.')) return;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/analytics/recalculate_records', 'POST');
        if (!res.ok) return alert('Ошибка пересчёта');
        const d = await res.json();
        if (d.records) _renderRecords(d.records);
        alert('✅ Рекорды успешно пересчитаны без аномалий!');
    } catch (e) {
        alert(e.message);
    }
}

function _renderThrottleBadge(th) {
    const b = document.getElementById('isp-throttle-badge');
    if (!b) return;
    b.classList.toggle('hidden', !th.throttled);
    if (th.throttled) {
        b.textContent = `🚧 Полка скорости ~${th.tier_mbps} Мбит/с (${th.plateau_mbs} MiB/s)`;
    }
}

function _renderHeatmap365(cells) {
    const grid = document.getElementById('heatmap-grid');
    const wrap = document.getElementById('heatmap-scroll-wrap');
    if (!grid || !cells.length) return;
    grid.innerHTML = '';
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();

    const firstWd = cells[0].weekday || 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < firstWd; i++) {
        const emp = document.createElement('span');
        emp.className = 'hm-cell hm-empty';
        frag.appendChild(emp);
    }
    cells.forEach(c => {
        const sp = document.createElement('span');
        sp.className = `hm-cell hm-l${c.level}`;
        sp.title = `${c.date}: ${c.fmt}`;
        frag.appendChild(sp);
    });
    grid.appendChild(frag);
    if (wrap) wrap.scrollLeft = wrap.scrollWidth;
}

function _renderPeerClientsChart(c) {
    const tot = document.getElementById('peer-clients-total');
    const totalCount = c.total_peers || 0;
    if (tot) tot.textContent = `${totalCount} пиров`;
    if (typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    const ctx = document.getElementById('chart-peer-clients');
    if (!ctx) return;
    if (chartPeerClients) chartPeerClients.destroy();

    const cs = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
    const txtColor = cs.getPropertyValue('--text-muted').trim() || '#95a5a6';
    const ui = typeof getUiSettings === 'function' ? getUiSettings() : {};
    const colMap = ui.peer_client_colors || {};
    const defColMap = {
        "qBittorrent": "#2ecc71", "Transmission": "#e74c3c", "uTorrent": "#27ae60",
        "Deluge": "#3498db", "BiglyBT": "#9b59b6", "Aria2": "#1abc9c",
        "Tixati": "#f39c12", "Vuze": "#00bcd4", "libtorrent": "#95a5a6", "Прочие": "#64748b"
    };

    const labels = c.labels?.length ? c.labels : ['Нет пиров'];
    const values = c.values?.length ? c.values : [1];
    const bgColors = labels.map(name => colMap[name] || defColMap[name] || '#64748b');

    chartPeerClients = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'doughnut',
        data: {
            labels: labels,
            datasets: [{
                data: values,
                backgroundColor: c.values?.length ? bgColors : ['rgba(149,165,166,0.2)'],
                borderWidth: 0
            }]
        },
        options: {
            responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
            plugins: {
                legend: {
                    position: 'right',
                    labels: {
                        color: txtColor, boxWidth: 10, font: { size: 10 },
                        generateLabels: chart => {
                            const data = chart.data;
                            if (!data.labels.length) return [];
                            return data.labels.map((label, i) => {
                                const val = data.datasets[0].data[i] || 0;
                                const pct = totalCount > 0 ? ((val / totalCount) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0';
                                return {
                                    text: `${label}: ${val} (${pct}%)`,
                                    fillStyle: data.datasets[0].backgroundColor[i],
                                    hidden: false, index: i
                                };
                            });
                        }
                    }
                },
                tooltip: {
                    callbacks: {
                        label: ctxItem => {
                            const val = ctxItem.parsed || 0;
                            const pct = totalCount > 0 ? ((val / totalCount) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0';
                            return ` ${ctxItem.label}: ${val} (${pct}%)`;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    });
}

function _renderTrackersChart(tr) {
    if (typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    const ctx = document.getElementById('chart-trackers');
    if (!ctx) return;
    if (chartTrackers) chartTrackers.destroy();
    const cs = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
    const tickColor = cs.getPropertyValue('--text-muted').trim() || '#95a5a6';
    const gridColor = cs.getPropertyValue('--border').trim() || 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';

    chartTrackers = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'bar',
        data: {
            labels: tr.labels || [],
            datasets: [{
                data: tr.values || [],
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(155, 89, 182, 0.72)',
                borderColor: '#9b59b6',
                borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 4
            }]
        },
        options: {
            responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, indexAxis: 'y',
            scales: {
                x: { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { color: tickColor, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: gridColor } },
                y: { ticks: { color: tickColor, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: gridColor } }
            },
            plugins: { legend: { display: false } }
        }
    });
}

function openTorrentInspectorModal() {
    if (typeof selectedHash === 'undefined' || !selectedHash) return alert(t('err_select_torrent'));
    _inspHash = selectedHash;
    const item = (typeof torrentsData !== 'undefined' ? torrentsData : []).find(x => x.hash === _inspHash);
    document.getElementById('insp-title').textContent = item ? item.name : _inspHash;
    document.getElementById('inspector-modal').style.display = 'flex';
    loadInspectorChart('24h');
    loadInspectorPeers();
}

function closeInspectorModal() {
    document.getElementById('inspector-modal').style.display = 'none';
}

async function loadInspectorChart(period = '24h') {
    if (!_inspHash || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
    const res = await api(`/api/torrents/chart?hash=${encodeURIComponent(_inspHash)}&period=${encodeURIComponent(period)}`);
    if (!res.ok) return;
    const d = await res.json();
    const pts = d.points || [];
    const ctx = document.getElementById('chart-torrent-personal');
    if (!ctx) return;
    if (chartTorrentPersonal) chartTorrentPersonal.destroy();

    const cs = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
    const tickColor = cs.getPropertyValue('--text-muted').trim() || '#95a5a6';
    const gridColor = cs.getPropertyValue('--border').trim() || 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';

    chartTorrentPersonal = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'line',
        data: {
            labels: pts.map(p => p.label),
            datasets: [{
                data: pts.map(p => p.speed_mbs),
                borderColor: '#2ecc71',
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(46, 204, 113, 0.2)',
                fill: true, tension: 0.3, pointRadius: pts.length <= 40 ? 2.5 : 0
            }]
        },
        options: {
            responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
            scales: {
                x: { ticks: { color: tickColor, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: gridColor } },
                y: { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { color: tickColor, font: { size: 10 } }, grid: { color: gridColor } }
            },
            plugins: {
                legend: { display: false },
                tooltip: {
                    callbacks: {
                        label: ctxItem => `⚡ ${ctxItem.parsed.y} MiB/s (+${pts[ctxItem.dataIndex]?.delta_fmt || '0 B'})`
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    });
}

async function loadInspectorPeers() {
    if (!_inspHash) return;
    const tbody = document.getElementById('insp-peers-tbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg">⏳ Загрузка пиров...</td></tr>';
    const res = await api(`/api/torrents/peers?hash=${encodeURIComponent(_inspHash)}`);
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--danger);">${escapeHtml(d.error || 'Ошибка')}</td></tr>`;
        return;
    }
    const peers = d.peers || [];
    if (!peers.length) {
        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg">Активных пиров сейчас нет</td></tr>';
        return;
    }
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    peers.forEach(p => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        const ctry = p.country_code ? `[${escapeHtml(p.country_code)}] ` : '';
        tr.innerHTML = `
            <td>${ctry}<b>${escapeHtml(p.client)}</b></td>
            <td>${p.progress}%</td>
            <td class="accent-txt">${escapeHtml(p.up_speed_fmt)}</td>
            <td class="blue-txt">${escapeHtml(p.dl_speed_fmt)}</td>
            <td class="muted-text">${escapeHtml(p.flags || '—')}</td>
        `;
        frag.appendChild(tr);
    });
    tbody.appendChild(frag);
}

// web/static/analytics_logs.js
let dayLogsPage = 1;
let dayLogsTotalPages = 1;

async function loadTorrentHourlyHistory() {
    const hash = document.getElementById('history-torrent-select').value || 'all';
    const res = await api(`/api/analytics/history?hash=${encodeURIComponent(hash)}&limit=36`);
    const list = await res.json();
    const tbody = document.getElementById('torrent-history-tbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    if (!list.length) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg">${t('no_hourly_snaps')}</td></tr>`;
        return;
    }
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    list.forEach(r => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        const intLbl = r.interval_min > 0 ? ` (${r.interval_min}м)` : '';
        tr.innerHTML = `
            <td>${escapeHtml(r.datetime)}</td>
            <td>${escapeHtml(r.uploaded_fmt)}</td>
            <td class="purple-txt" style="font-weight:700;">+${escapeHtml(trBackend(r.delta_mb))}<span class="muted-text" style="font-size:10px;">${intLbl}</span></td>
            <td class="blue-txt" style="font-weight:600;">${escapeHtml(r.speed_fmt)}</td>
        `;
        frag.appendChild(tr);
    });
    tbody.appendChild(frag);
}

async function initDayLogs() {
    const card = document.getElementById('admin-day-logs-card');
    if (!card) return;
    if (typeof CURRENT_ROLE !== 'undefined' && CURRENT_ROLE === 'viewer') {
        card.classList.add('hidden');
        return;
    }
    try {
        const [canRes, daysRes] = await Promise.all([api('/api/logs/can_open_folder'), api('/api/logs/days')]);
        if (canRes.ok) {
            const d = await canRes.json();
            document.getElementById('btn-open-logs-folder')?.classList.toggle('hidden', !d.can_open);
        }
        if (daysRes.ok) {
            const dd = await daysRes.json();
            const sel = document.getElementById('logs-day-picker');
            if (sel) {
                sel.innerHTML = '';
                const ph = document.createElement('option');
                ph.value = ''; ph.textContent = `-- ${t('pick_day_hint')} --`;
                sel.appendChild(ph);
                (dd.days || []).forEach(day => {
                    const opt = document.createElement('option');
                    opt.value = day; opt.textContent = day;
                    sel.appendChild(opt);
                });
            }
        }
    } catch (e) {}
}

async function loadDayLogs(page = 1) {
    const picker = document.getElementById('logs-day-picker');
    const dateVal = picker ? picker.value : '';
    const dlBtn = document.getElementById('btn-dl-day-logs');
    if (!dateVal) {
        if (dlBtn) dlBtn.classList.add('hidden');
        return;
    }
    if (dlBtn) {
        dlBtn.href = `/api/logs/day/download?date=${encodeURIComponent(dateVal)}`;
        dlBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    const targetPage = Math.max(1, Math.min(dayLogsTotalPages || 1, page));
    const res = await api(`/api/logs/day?date=${encodeURIComponent(dateVal)}&page=${targetPage}&per_page=15`);
    if (!res.ok) return;
    const d = await res.json();
    dayLogsPage = d.page || 1;
    dayLogsTotalPages = d.total_pages || 1;
    document.getElementById('day-logs-page-info').textContent = `${dayLogsPage} / ${dayLogsTotalPages} (${d.total || 0})`;

    const tbody = document.getElementById('day-logs-tbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    if (!d.items || !d.items.length) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg">${t('no_snaps_day')}</td></tr>`;
        return;
    }
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    d.items.forEach(r => {
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        tr.innerHTML = `<td>${escapeHtml(r.datetime)}</td><td class="accent-txt"><b>${escapeHtml(r.uploaded_fmt)}</b></td><td>${escapeHtml(r.size_fmt)}</td><td>${r.count}</td>`;
        frag.appendChild(tr);
    });
    tbody.appendChild(frag);
}

async function openLogsFolderHost() {
    const res = await api('/api/logs/open_folder', 'POST');
    if (!res.ok) {
        const d = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        alert(d.error || 'Недоступно');
    }
}

// web/static/automation.js
let currentRules = [];
let editingRuleId = null;

function openBotGuide(type) {
    const modal = document.getElementById('bot-guide-modal');
    const title = document.getElementById('bot-guide-title');
    const body = document.getElementById('bot-guide-body');
    const isEn = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'en');

    if (type === 'tg') {
        title.textContent = isEn ? '📱 How to set up Telegram Bot' : '📱 Как подключить Telegram-бота';
        body.innerHTML = isEn ? `
            <ol style="padding-left:18px; line-height:1.6;">
                <li>Open <b>@BotFather</b> in Telegram, send <code>/newbot</code>, choose a name and copy the <b>Bot Token</b>.</li>
                <li>Open your new bot and press <b>Start</b> (or send any message).</li>
                <li>Open <b>@userinfobot</b> (or <b>@getmyid_bot</b>) to get your numeric <b>Chat ID</b>.</li>
                <li>Paste Token &amp; Chat ID here, click <b>Save</b> and press <b>Test</b>.</li>
            </ol>
        ` : `
            <ol style="padding-left:18px; line-height:1.6;">
                <li>Найди в Telegram <b>@BotFather</b>, отправь <code>/newbot</code>, задай имя и скопируй <b>Bot Token</b>.</li>
                <li>Зайди в своего нового бота и нажми <b>Запустить (Start)</b>.</li>
                <li>Напиши боту <b>@userinfobot</b> (или <b>@getmyid_bot</b>), чтобы узнать свой цифровой <b>Chat ID</b>.</li>
                <li>Вставь Token и Chat ID сюда, нажми <b>Сохранить</b> и проверь кнопкой <b>Тест</b>.</li>
            </ol>
        `;
    } else {
        title.textContent = isEn ? '💬 How to create a Discord Webhook' : '💬 Как создать Discord Webhook';
        body.innerHTML = isEn ? `
            <ol style="padding-left:18px; line-height:1.6;">
                <li>Open your Discord server → <b>Channel Settings</b> (gear icon on any text channel).</li>
                <li>Go to <b>Integrations</b> → <b>Webhooks</b> → <b>New Webhook</b>.</li>
                <li>Click <b>Copy Webhook URL</b>.</li>
                <li>Paste the URL here, click <b>Save</b> and press <b>Test</b>.</li>
            </ol>
        ` : `
            <ol style="padding-left:18px; line-height:1.6;">
                <li>Открой свой сервер Discord → <b>Настройки канала</b> (шестерёнка рядом с нужным каналом).</li>
                <li>Перейди в <b>Интеграции</b> → <b>Вебхуки</b> → <b>Новый вебхук</b>.</li>
                <li>Нажми <b>Копировать URL вебхука</b>.</li>
                <li>Вставь ссылку сюда, нажми <b>Сохранить</b> и проверь кнопкой <b>Тест</b>.</li>
            </ol>

    }
    modal.style.display = 'flex';
}

function closeBotGuide() {
    document.getElementById('bot-guide-modal').style.display = 'none';
}

async function loadAutomation() {
    const res = await api('/api/automation');
    const d = await res.json();
    const s = d.settings || {};

    document.getElementById('auto-enabled').checked = Boolean(s.enabled);
    document.getElementById('auto-interval').value = s.interval || 5;
    document.getElementById('auto-tg-token').value = '';
    document.getElementById('auto-tg-token').placeholder = s.has_tg_token ? t('token_set_ph') : '123456:ABC-DEF...';
    document.getElementById('auto-tg-chat').value = s.tg_chat_id || '';
    document.getElementById('auto-discord').value = '';
    document.getElementById('auto-discord').placeholder = s.has_discord ? t('webhook_set_ph') : 'https://discord.com/api/webhooks/...';

    const diskInp = document.getElementById('auto-alert-disk');
    if (diskInp) diskInp.value = s.alert_disk_gib || '';
    const qbChk = document.getElementById('auto-alert-qb');
    if (qbChk) qbChk.checked = Boolean(s.alert_qb_down);
    const recheckChk = document.getElementById('auto-recheck-io');
    if (recheckChk) recheckChk.checked = Boolean(s.auto_recheck_enabled);

    currentRules = d.rules || [];
    renderRules(currentRules);
    if (typeof loadDndDigestAndTagging === 'function') loadDndDigestAndTagging();
}

function _fmtBytesBinary(b) {
    if (!b || b <= 0) return '';
    if (b >= 1024 ** 4) return `${(b / (1024 ** 4)).toFixed(2).replace(/\.00$/, '')} TiB`;
    if (b >= 1024 ** 3) return `${(b / (1024 ** 3)).toFixed(2).replace(/\.00$/, '')} GiB`;
    return `${(b / (1024 ** 2)).toFixed(2).replace(/\.00$/, '')} MiB`;
}

function _fmtSpeedBinary(bps) {
    if (!bps || bps <= 0) return '';
    if (bps >= 1024 ** 2) return `${(bps / (1024 ** 2)).toFixed(2).replace(/\.00$/, '')} MiB/s`;
    return `${(bps / 1024).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '')} KiB/s`;
}

function renderRules(rules) {
    const tbody = document.getElementById('rules-tbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    if (!rules.length) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg">${t('no_rules')}</td></tr>`;
        return;
    }
    const isEn = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'en');
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    rules.forEach(r => {
        const conds = [];
        if (r.min_ratio > 0) conds.push(`Ratio >= ${r.min_ratio}x`);
        if (r.min_seed_days > 0) {
            const tmLbl = r.time_mode === 'time_active' ? (isEn ? 'Active' : 'Актив') : (isEn ? 'Seed' : 'Сид');
            conds.push(`${tmLbl} >= ${r.min_seed_days} ${t('rule_days_unit')}`);
        }
        if (r.min_uploaded_bytes > 0) conds.push(`${isEn ? 'Up' : 'Отдано'} >= ${_fmtBytesBinary(r.min_uploaded_bytes)}`);
        if (r.speed_op && r.speed_bytes > 0) {
            conds.push(`${isEn ? 'AvgSpd' : 'СрСкор'} ${r.speed_op} ${_fmtSpeedBinary(r.speed_bytes)} (${r.speed_window_min || 60}m)`);
        }

        const acts = [];
        if (r.action_pause) acts.push(t('chk_pause'));
        if (r.action_tag) acts.push(`🏷️ +${r.action_tag}`);
        if (r.action_remove_tag) acts.push(`✂️ -${r.action_remove_tag}`);
        if (r.notify) acts.push(t('rule_bot'));

        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        tr.innerHTML = `
            <td><strong>${escapeHtml(r.name)}</strong></td>
            <td style="white-space:normal;">${escapeHtml(conds.join(t('rule_and')))}</td>
            <td style="white-space:normal;">${escapeHtml(acts.join(', ') || '—')}</td>
            <td><span class="badge ${r.enabled ? 'badge-up' : 'badge-pause'}">${r.enabled ? t('rule_on') : t('rule_off')}</span></td>
            <td class="row-gap">
                <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" onclick="editRule(${r.id})">✏️</button>
                <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" onclick="toggleRule(${r.id})">${r.enabled ? t('rule_off') : t('rule_on')}</button>
                <button class="btn btn-danger btn-xs" onclick="deleteRule(${r.id})">🗑️</button>
            </td>
        `;
        frag.appendChild(tr);
    });
    tbody.appendChild(frag);
}

function editRule(id) {
    const r = currentRules.find(x => x.id === id);
    if (!r) return;

    editingRuleId = id;
    document.getElementById('rule-name').value = r.name || '';
    document.getElementById('rule-time-mode').value = r.time_mode || 'seeding_time';
    document.getElementById('rule-ratio').value = r.min_ratio > 0 ? r.min_ratio : '';
    document.getElementById('rule-days').value = r.min_seed_days > 0 ? r.min_seed_days : '';

    const upBytes = r.min_uploaded_bytes || 0;
    if (upBytes >= 1024 ** 4) {
        document.getElementById('rule-up-val').value = +(upBytes / (1024 ** 4)).toFixed(3);
        document.getElementById('rule-up-unit').value = 'TiB';
    } else if (upBytes >= 1024 ** 3) {
        document.getElementById('rule-up-val').value = +(upBytes / (1024 ** 3)).toFixed(3);
        document.getElementById('rule-up-unit').value = 'GiB';
    } else if (upBytes > 0) {
        document.getElementById('rule-up-val').value = +(upBytes / (1024 ** 2)).toFixed(2);
        document.getElementById('rule-up-unit').value = 'MiB';
    } else {
        document.getElementById('rule-up-val').value = '';
        document.getElementById('rule-up-unit').value = 'GiB';
    }

    document.getElementById('rule-speed-op').value = r.speed_op || '';
    const spBytes = r.speed_bytes || 0;
    if (spBytes >= 1024 ** 2) {
        document.getElementById('rule-speed-val').value = +(spBytes / (1024 ** 2)).toFixed(3);
        document.getElementById('rule-speed-unit').value = 'MiB';
    } else if (spBytes > 0) {
        document.getElementById('rule-speed-val').value = +(spBytes / 1024).toFixed(2);
        document.getElementById('rule-speed-unit').value = 'KiB';
    } else {
        document.getElementById('rule-speed-val').value = '';
        document.getElementById('rule-speed-unit').value = 'KiB';
    }
    document.getElementById('rule-speed-win').value = r.speed_window_min || 60;

    document.getElementById('rule-tag').value = r.action_tag || '';
    document.getElementById('rule-remove-tag').value = r.action_remove_tag || '';
    document.getElementById('rule-pause').checked = Boolean(r.action_pause);
    document.getElementById('rule-notify').checked = Boolean(r.notify);

    document.getElementById('rule-form-title').textContent = t('card_edit_rule', { id });
    document.getElementById('btn-rule-submit').textContent = t('btn_save');
    document.getElementById('btn-rule-cancel').classList.remove('hidden');
    document.getElementById('rule-form-card').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });
}

function cancelEditRule() {
    editingRuleId = null;
    document.getElementById('rule-name').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-time-mode').value = 'seeding_time';
    document.getElementById('rule-ratio').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-days').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-up-val').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-up-unit').value = 'GiB';
    document.getElementById('rule-speed-op').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-speed-val').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-speed-unit').value = 'KiB';
    document.getElementById('rule-speed-win').value = 60;
    document.getElementById('rule-tag').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-remove-tag').value = '';
    document.getElementById('rule-pause').checked = false;
    document.getElementById('rule-notify').checked = true;

    document.getElementById('rule-form-title').textContent = t('card_add_rule');
    document.getElementById('btn-rule-submit').textContent = t('btn_add');
    document.getElementById('btn-rule-cancel').classList.add('hidden');
}

async function saveAutomationSettings() {
    const payload = {
        enabled: document.getElementById('auto-enabled').checked,
        interval: parseInt(document.getElementById('auto-interval').value) || 5,
        alert_disk_gib: parseInt(document.getElementById('auto-alert-disk')?.value || 0) || 0,
        alert_qb_down: document.getElementById('auto-alert-qb')?.checked || false,
        auto_recheck_enabled: document.getElementById('auto-recheck-io')?.checked || false,
        tg_token: document.getElementById('auto-tg-token').value.trim(),
        tg_chat_id: document.getElementById('auto-tg-chat').value.trim(),
        discord_webhook: document.getElementById('auto-discord').value.trim()
    };
    const res = await api('/api/automation/settings', 'POST', payload);
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok) return alert(d.error || t('save_err'));
    document.getElementById('auto-status-msg').textContent = d.message;
    loadAutomation();
}

async function testNotifications() {
    const msgEl = document.getElementById('auto-status-msg');
    msgEl.textContent = t('auto_testing');
    const res = await api('/api/automation/test', 'POST');
    const d = await res.json();
    msgEl.textContent = d.message;
    if (!d.success) alert(d.message);
}

async function runAutomationNow() {
    const msgEl = document.getElementById('auto-status-msg');
    msgEl.textContent = t('auto_checking');
    const res = await api('/api/automation/run_now', 'POST');
    const d = await res.json();
    msgEl.textContent = d.message;
}

async function submitAutomationRule() {
    const upVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById('rule-up-val').value) || 0;
    const upUnit = document.getElementById('rule-up-unit').value;
    const upMult = upUnit === 'TiB' ? (1024 ** 4) : (upUnit === 'GiB' ? (1024 ** 3) : (1024 ** 2));

    const spVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById('rule-speed-val').value) || 0;
    const spUnit = document.getElementById('rule-speed-unit').value;
    const spMult = spUnit === 'MiB' ? (1024 ** 2) : 1024;

    const payload = {
        name: document.getElementById('rule-name').value.trim(),
        time_mode: document.getElementById('rule-time-mode').value,
        min_ratio: parseFloat(document.getElementById('rule-ratio').value) || 0,
        min_seed_days: parseFloat(document.getElementById('rule-days').value) || 0,
        min_uploaded_bytes: upVal > 0 ? Math.round(upVal * upMult) : 0,
        speed_op: document.getElementById('rule-speed-op').value,
        speed_bytes: spVal > 0 ? (spVal * spMult) : 0,
        speed_window_min: parseInt(document.getElementById('rule-speed-win').value) || 60,
        action_tag: document.getElementById('rule-tag').value.trim(),
        action_remove_tag: document.getElementById('rule-remove-tag').value.trim(),
        action_pause: document.getElementById('rule-pause').checked,
        notify: document.getElementById('rule-notify').checked
    };

    let url = '/api/automation/rules';
    if (editingRuleId !== null) {
        url = '/api/automation/rules/update';
        payload.id = editingRuleId;
    }

    try {
        const res = await api(url, 'POST', payload);
        const d = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok || d.success === false) {
            return alert(d.error || `HTTP ${res.status}`);
        }
        cancelEditRule();
        loadAutomation();
    } catch (e) {
        alert(e.message);
    }
}

function addAutomationRule() {
    return submitAutomationRule();
}

async function toggleRule(id) {
    await api('/api/automation/rules/toggle', 'POST', { id });
    loadAutomation();
}

async function deleteRule(id) {
    if (!confirm(t('rule_del_confirm'))) return;
    if (editingRuleId === id) cancelEditRule();
    await api('/api/automation/rules/delete', 'POST', { id });
    loadAutomation();
}

// web/static/common.js
const VALID_TABS = ['torrents', 'merger', 'summary', 'analytics', 'automation', 'tools'];
const CSRF_TOKEN = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]')?.content || '';

function escapeHtml(str) {
    if (str === null || str === undefined) return '';
    return String(str)
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
        .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
        .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
}

async function api(url, method = 'GET', body = null) {
    const opts = { method, headers: {} };
    if (method === 'POST') {
        opts.headers['X-CSRF-Token'] = CSRF_TOKEN;
        if (body !== null) {
            opts.headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
            opts.body = JSON.stringify(body);
        }
    }
    const res = await fetch(url, opts);
    if (res.status === 401) {
        window.location.href = '/login';
        throw new Error('Auth required');
    }
    return res;
}

function formatBytesJS(bytes) {
    if (!bytes || bytes <= 0) return '0 B';
    const units = ['B', 'KiB', 'MiB', 'GiB', 'TiB', 'PiB'];
    let val = parseFloat(bytes);
    let u = 0;
    while (val >= 1024 && u < units.length - 1) { val /= 1024; u++; }
    return `${val.toFixed(2)} ${units[u]}`;
}

function parseDateToTs(str) {
    if (!str || typeof str !== 'string' || str.includes('неизвестна')) return 0;
    const parts = str.trim().split(' ');
    if (parts.length < 2) return 0;
    const dmy = parts[0].split('.');
    const hm = parts[1].split(':');
    if (dmy.length !== 3 || hm.length !== 2) return 0;
    return new Date(parseInt(dmy[2]), parseInt(dmy[1]) - 1, parseInt(dmy[0]), parseInt(hm[0]), parseInt(hm[1])).getTime();
}

function switchTab(name, updateHash = true) {
    if (!VALID_TABS.includes(name)) name = 'torrents';
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.tab === name));
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(c => c.classList.toggle('active', c.id === 'tab-' + name));
    if (updateHash && window.location.hash !== '#' + name) window.location.hash = name;

    if (name === 'torrents') loadTorrents();
    if (name === 'merger') loadMergerLists();
    if (name === 'summary') loadSummary();
    if (name === 'analytics') loadAnalyticsTab();
    if (name === 'automation') loadAutomation();
    if (name === 'tools' && typeof loadToolsTab === 'function') loadToolsTab();
}

function refreshActiveTab() {
    const active = document.querySelector('.tab-btn.active')?.dataset.tab || 'torrents';
    if (active === 'torrents') loadTorrents(true);
    else if (active === 'summary') loadSummary(true);
    else if (active === 'analytics') loadAnalyticsTab();
    else if (active === 'automation') loadAutomation();
    else if (active === 'merger') loadTorrents(true);
    else if (active === 'tools' && typeof loadToolsTab === 'function') loadToolsTab();
    checkQbStatus();
}

function syncTabWithHash() {
    const rawHash = (window.location.hash || '').replace('#', '').trim();
    switchTab(VALID_TABS.includes(rawHash) ? rawHash : 'torrents', false);
}
window.addEventListener('hashchange', syncTabWithHash);

document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (typeof openCommandPalette === 'function') openCommandPalette();
    }
});

window.onload = async () => {
    applyI18n();
    await loadMeAndApplyRole();
    syncTabWithHash();
    if (typeof initSystemStatusAndUpdates === 'function') initSystemStatusAndUpdates();
    if (typeof initTimersUi === 'function') initTimersUi();
    setInterval(checkQbStatus, 5000);
};

function getUiSettings() {
    if (typeof CURRENT_UI_SETTINGS !== 'undefined' && CURRENT_UI_SETTINGS) return CURRENT_UI_SETTINGS;
    try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem('tbr_ui_settings');
        if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch (e) {}
    return {
        theme: 'dark',
        custom_colors: { bg_main: '#121214', bg_panel: '#1a1a20', text: '#ffffff', accent: '#2ecc71' },
        font: 'Segoe UI',
        allow_google_fonts: false,
        mobile_mode: true,
        charts: {
            daily:  { color: '#2ecc71', color2: '#10b981', gradient: true, opacity: 0.75 },
            top5:   { color: '#ff9f1c', color2: '#ff4d6d', gradient: true, opacity: 0.8 },
            hourly: { color: '#00f5d4', color2: '#9b5de5', gradient: true, opacity: 0.35 }
        },
        peer_client_colors: {
            "qBittorrent": "#2ecc71", "Transmission": "#e74c3c", "uTorrent": "#27ae60",
            "Deluge": "#3498db", "BiglyBT": "#9b59b6", "Aria2": "#1abc9c",
            "Tixati": "#f39c12", "Vuze": "#00bcd4", "libtorrent": "#95a5a6", "Прочие": "#64748b"
        }
    };
}

function hexToRgb(hex) {
    const m = String(hex || '#2ecc71').replace('#', '').match(/^([0-9a-f]{2})([0-9a-f]{2})([0-9a-f]{2})$/i);
    if (!m) return { r: 46, g: 204, b: 113 };
    return { r: parseInt(m[1], 16), g: parseInt(m[2], 16), b: parseInt(m[3], 16) };
}

function applyUiSettings(ui) {
    if (!ui) return;
    const root = document.documentElement;
    const theme = ui.theme || 'dark';
    let palette = THEME_PRESETS[theme] || THEME_PRESETS.dark;

    if (theme === 'custom' && ui.custom_colors) {
        const c = ui.custom_colors;
        const isDark = hexToRgb(c.bg_main).r * 0.299 + hexToRgb(c.bg_main).g * 0.587 + hexToRgb(c.bg_main).b * 0.114 < 128;
        palette = {
            bg_main: c.bg_main || '#121214', bg_panel: c.bg_panel || '#1a1a20',
            bg_input: isDark ? '#262630' : '#e8eaef', bg_hover: isDark ? '#22222a' : '#f4f6f8',
            text: c.text || '#ffffff', text_muted: isDark ? '#95a5a6' : '#6b7280',
            accent: c.accent || '#2ecc71', border: isDark ? '#2a2a34' : '#d1d5db',
            th_bg: isDark ? '#1e1e26' : '#f3f4f6'
        };
    }

    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(palette)) {
        root.style.setProperty(`--${k.replace('_', '-')}`, v);
    }
    document.body.dataset.theme = theme;
    document.body.classList.toggle('mobile-cards-on', ui.mobile_mode !== false);
    if (typeof updateMobileCardsBtn === 'function') updateMobileCardsBtn();

    const fontObj = AVAILABLE_FONTS.find(f => f.name === ui.font) || AVAILABLE_FONTS[0];
    root.style.setProperty('--app-font', fontObj.css);

    if (typeof syncAnalyticsControlsFromUi === 'function') syncAnalyticsControlsFromUi();
    if (typeof applySummarySortAndRender === 'function' && typeof rawSummaryItems !== 'undefined' && rawSummaryItems.length) {
        applySummarySortAndRender();
    }
    if (typeof loadAnalyticsCharts === 'function' && document.getElementById('tab-analytics')?.classList.contains('active')) {
        loadAnalyticsCharts();
        if (typeof loadAnalyticsInsights === 'function') loadAnalyticsInsights(false);
    }
}

async function persistUiSettings(ui) {
    if (typeof CURRENT_UI_SETTINGS !== 'undefined') CURRENT_UI_SETTINGS = ui;
    localStorage.setItem('tbr_ui_settings', JSON.stringify(ui));
    applyUiSettings(ui);
    if (typeof CURRENT_ROLE === 'undefined' || CURRENT_ROLE === 'admin') {
        try { await api('/api/profile/ui', 'POST', ui); } catch (e) {}
    }
}

function openCustomizeModal() {
    const ui = getUiSettings();
    document.getElementById('cust-theme').value = ui.theme || 'dark';
    const cc = ui.custom_colors || {};
    document.getElementById('cust-bg-main').value = cc.bg_main || '#121214';
    document.getElementById('cust-bg-panel').value = cc.bg_panel || '#1a1a20';
    document.getElementById('cust-text').value = cc.text || '#ffffff';
    document.getElementById('cust-accent').value = cc.accent || '#2ecc71';
    document.getElementById('cust-custom-colors').classList.toggle('hidden', (ui.theme || 'dark') !== 'custom');

    const fontSel = document.getElementById('cust-font');
    fontSel.innerHTML = '';
    AVAILABLE_FONTS.forEach(f => {
        const opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = f.name; opt.textContent = f.label;
        fontSel.appendChild(opt);
    });
    fontSel.value = ui.font || 'Segoe UI';
    document.getElementById('cust-allow-gf').checked = Boolean(ui.allow_google_fonts);
    document.getElementById('cust-mobile-mode').checked = (ui.mobile_mode !== false);

    renderChartEditors(ui.charts || {});
    renderClientColorEditors(ui.peer_client_colors || {});
    document.getElementById('customize-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}

function closeCustomizeModal() { document.getElementById('customize-modal').style.display = 'none'; }
function onCustThemeChange() {
    document.getElementById('cust-custom-colors').classList.toggle('hidden', document.getElementById('cust-theme').value !== 'custom');
}

function renderChartEditors(chartsCfg) {

        { key: 'hourly', title: '3. Скорость нагрузки', def1: '#00f5d4', def2: '#9b5de5' }
    ];
    defs.forEach(d => {
        const cfg = chartsCfg[d.key] || { color: d.def1, color2: d.def2, gradient: true, opacity: 0.7 };
        const c1 = cfg.color || d.def1;
        const c2 = cfg.color2 || cfg.color || d.def2;
        const div = document.createElement('div');
        div.style.cssText = 'background:var(--bg-input); padding:8px; border-radius:6px; margin-bottom:8px;';
        div.innerHTML = `
            <div class="row-between mb-8">
                <b style="font-size:12px;">${d.title}</b>
                <label class="row-gap lbl-xs pointer"><input type="checkbox" id="cc-${d.key}-grad" ${cfg.gradient !== false ? 'checked' : ''}> Градиент</label>
            </div>
            <div class="row-wrap" style="gap:8px;">
                <div class="row-gap">
                    <span class="lbl-xs">Цвет 1:</span>
                    <input type="color" id="cc-${d.key}-c1" value="${c1}">
                </div>
                <div class="row-gap">
                    <span class="lbl-xs">Цвет 2:</span>
                    <input type="color" id="cc-${d.key}-c2" value="${c2}">
                </div>
                <div class="row-gap">
                    <span class="lbl-xs">Прозрачность:</span>
                    <input type="range" id="cc-${d.key}-op" min="0.1" max="1" step="0.05" value="${cfg.opacity ?? 0.7}" style="width:75px;">
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="row-wrap mt-10" style="gap:4px;">
                <span class="lbl-xs muted-text" style="width:100%;">Неоновые пары:</span>
                ${NEON_PRESETS.map(p => `
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary btn-xs" style="font-size:10px; padding:2px 6px;" onclick="applyNeonPreset('${d.key}','${p.c1}','${p.c2}')">
                        <span style="display:inline-block;width:8px;height:8px;background:${p.c1};border-radius:2px;"></span>
                        <span style="display:inline-block;width:8px;height:8px;background:${p.c2};border-radius:2px;"></span> ${p.label}
                    </button>
                `).join('')}
            </div>
        `;
        box.appendChild(div);
    });
}

function applyNeonPreset(key, c1, c2) {
    const el1 = document.getElementById(`cc-${key}-c1`);
    const el2 = document.getElementById(`cc-${key}-c2`);
    if (el1) el1.value = c1;
    if (el2) el2.value = c2;
}

    grid.innerHTML = '';
    const defs = {
        "qBittorrent": "#2ecc71", "Transmission": "#e74c3c", "uTorrent": "#27ae60",

        "Tixati": "#f39c12", "Vuze": "#00bcd4", "libtorrent": "#95a5a6", "Прочие": "#64748b"

        row.style.cssText = 'background:var(--bg-input); padding:4px 8px; border-radius:5px; font-size:11px;';

        grid.appendChild(row);
    }
}

async function saveCustomization() {
    const ui = getUiSettings();
    ui.theme = document.getElementById('cust-theme').value;
    ui.custom_colors = {
        bg_main: document.getElementById('cust-bg-main').value,
        bg_panel: document.getElementById('cust-bg-panel').value,
        text: document.getElementById('cust-text').value,
        accent: document.getElementById('cust-accent').value
    };
    ui.font = document.getElementById('cust-font').value;
    ui.allow_google_fonts = document.getElementById('cust-allow-gf').checked;
    ui.mobile_mode = document.getElementById('cust-mobile-mode').checked;

    ui.charts = ui.charts || {};
    ['daily', 'top5', 'hourly'].forEach(k => {
        ui.charts[k] = {
            color: document.getElementById(`cc-${k}-c1`)?.value || '#2ecc71',
            color2: document.getElementById(`cc-${k}-c2`)?.value || '#10b981',
            gradient: document.getElementById(`cc-${k}-grad`)?.checked !== false,
            opacity: parseFloat(document.getElementById(`cc-${k}-op`)?.value) || 0.7
        };
    });

    ui.peer_client_colors = ui.peer_client_colors || {};
    document.querySelectorAll('.client-col-inp').forEach(inp => {
        ui.peer_client_colors[inp.dataset.client] = inp.value;
    });

    await persistUiSettings(ui);
    closeCustomizeModal();
}

// web/static/i18n.js
const I18N = { ru: I18N_RU, en: I18N_EN };
let currentLang = localStorage.getItem('tbr_lang') || 'ru';

function t(key, vars = {}) {
    let str = (I18N[currentLang] && I18N[currentLang][key]) || I18N.ru[key] || key;
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(vars)) {
        str = str.replace(new RegExp(`\\{${k}\\}`, 'g'), v);
    }
    return str;
}

function trBackend(str) {
    if (!str || currentLang === 'ru') return str || '';
    const s = String(str);
    if (s === 'ожидание замера') return 'waiting for snapshot';
    if (s === 'прошлый полный час (<5м нового)') return 'previous full hour (<5m elapsed)';
    if (s === 'по снимку ~1ч назад') return 'from snapshot ~1h ago';
    if (s === 'расчётное от суток') return 'estimated from 24h';
    const mMin = s.match(/^прошло\s+(\d+)\s+мин\s+с\s+техлога$/);
    if (mMin) return `${mMin[1]} min since last snapshot`;
    if (s === 'Дата неизвестна') return 'Unknown date';

    return s
        .replace(/МБ\/день/g, 'MB/day')
        .replace(/МБ\/ч/g, 'MB/h')
        .replace(/\/\s*час/g, '/ h')
        .replace(/\/\s*день/g, '/ day')
        .replace(/\/ч/g, '/h')
        .replace(/МБ/g, 'MB');
}

function applyI18n() {
    document.title = t('title');
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el => {
        el.textContent = t(el.dataset.i18n);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-ph]').forEach(el => {
        el.placeholder = t(el.dataset.i18nPh);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-title]').forEach(el => {
        el.title = t(el.dataset.i18nTitle);
    });
    const btn = document.getElementById('lang-toggle-btn');
    if (btn) btn.textContent = currentLang === 'ru' ? '🌐 RU' : '🌐 EN';
}

function toggleLang() {
    currentLang = currentLang === 'ru' ? 'en' : 'ru';
    localStorage.setItem('tbr_lang', currentLang);
    applyI18n();
    if (typeof checkQbStatus === 'function') checkQbStatus();
    if (typeof filterTorrents === 'function' && torrentsData.length) filterTorrents();
    if (typeof renderSummaryMeta === 'function') renderSummaryMeta();
    if (typeof applySummarySortAndRender === 'function' && rawSummaryItems.length) applySummarySortAndRender();
    if (typeof populateAnalyticsSelectors === 'function' && rawSummaryItems.length) populateAnalyticsSelectors();
    if (typeof onSmartCalcTargetChange === 'function') onSmartCalcTargetChange();
    if (typeof renderRules === 'function' && currentRules.length) renderRules(currentRules);
}

// web/static/merger_tools.js
function updateMergerDiffBtn() {
    const oldH = document.getElementById('merger-old')?.value || '';
    const newH = document.getElementById('merger-new')?.value || '';
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-merger-diff');
    if (btn) btn.disabled = (!oldH || !newH || oldH.toLowerCase() === newH.toLowerCase());
}

async function loadSmartMatchCandidates() {
    const box = document.getElementById('smart-match-list');
    if (!box) return;
    box.innerHTML = '<span class="muted-text">⏳ Поиск кандидатов на объединение...</span>';
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/merger/candidates');
        if (!res.ok) {
            box.innerHTML = '<span class="muted-text">Ошибка подбора кандидатов</span>';
            return;
        }
        const d = await res.json();
        const list = d.candidates || [];
        if (!list.length) {
            box.innerHTML = '<span class="muted-text">Совпадающих пар для объединения не найдено.</span>';
            return;
        }
        box.innerHTML = '';
        const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
        list.forEach(c => {
            const row = document.createElement('div');
            row.className = 'row-between';
            row.style.cssText = 'background:var(--bg-input); padding:6px 8px; border-radius:6px; gap:8px;';
            row.innerHTML = `
                <div style="min-width:0; flex:1;">
                    <div style="overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap;">
                        <b>${escapeHtml(c.old_name)}</b> <span class="accent-txt">(${escapeHtml(c.old_up_fmt)})</span>
                        → <b>${escapeHtml(c.new_name)}</b> <span class="muted-text">(${escapeHtml(c.new_up_fmt)})</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="lbl-xs muted-text">${escapeHtml(c.reason)}</div>
                </div>
                <button class="btn btn-xs" onclick="applySmartPair('${escapeHtml(c.old_hash)}', '${escapeHtml(c.new_hash)}')">Подставить</button>
            `;
            frag.appendChild(row);
        });
        box.appendChild(frag);
    } catch (e) {
        box.innerHTML = `<span class="muted-text">Ошибка: ${escapeHtml(e.message)}</span>`;
    }
}

function applySmartPair(oldHash, newHash) {
    const o = document.getElementById('merger-old');
    const n = document.getElementById('merger-new');
    if (o) o.value = oldHash;
    if (n) n.value = newHash;
    updateMergerDiffBtn();
}

async function openTorrentDiffModal() {
    const oldH = document.getElementById('merger-old')?.value || '';
    const newH = document.getElementById('merger-new')?.value || '';
    if (!oldH || !newH || oldH.toLowerCase() === newH.toLowerCase()) {
        return alert(t('err_same_torrent'));
    }

    const modal = document.getElementById('diff-modal');
    const tbody = document.getElementById('diff-tbody');
    const badges = document.getElementById('diff-summary-badges');
    const matchBadge = document.getElementById('diff-match-badge');

    modal.style.display = 'flex';
    matchBadge.textContent = '⏳ Анализ...';
    badges.innerHTML = '';
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg">⏳ Читаем списки файлов...</td></tr>';

    try {
        const res = await api(`/api/merger/diff?old_hash=${encodeURIComponent(oldH)}&new_hash=${encodeURIComponent(newH)}`);
        const d = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok || !d.success) {
            matchBadge.textContent = 'Ошибка';
            tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--danger);">${escapeHtml(d.error || 'Ошибка анализа')}</td></tr>`;
            return;
        }

        matchBadge.textContent = `Совпадение: ${d.match_percent}%`;
        const st = d.stats || {};
        badges.innerHTML = `
            <span class="badge badge-up">✅ Совпадают: ${st.exact || 0}</span>
            <span class="badge badge-stall">⚠️ Размер изменён: ${st.changed || 0}</span>
            <span class="badge badge-stop">➖ Удалены: ${st.removed || 0}</span>
            <span class="badge badge-dl">➕ Новые: ${st.added || 0}</span>
            <span class="badge badge-pause">${escapeHtml(d.old_total_fmt)} → ${escapeHtml(d.new_total_fmt)}</span>
        `;

        const files = d.files || [];
        if (!files.length) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg">Файлы не найдены</td></tr>';
            return;
        }
        tbody.innerHTML = '';
        const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
        const statusMap = {
            exact: { cls: 'diff-row-exact', badge: 'badge-up', txt: '100% ОК' },
            changed: { cls: 'diff-row-changed', badge: 'badge-stall', txt: 'Размер ≠' },
            removed: { cls: 'diff-row-removed', badge: 'badge-stop', txt: 'Удалён' },
            added: { cls: 'diff-row-added', badge: 'badge-dl', txt: 'Новый' }
        };
        files.forEach(f => {
            const s = statusMap[f.status] || statusMap.exact;
            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.className = s.cls;
            tr.innerHTML = `
                <td style="white-space:normal; word-break:break-all;">${escapeHtml(f.path)}</td>
                <td><span class="badge ${s.badge}">${s.txt}</span></td>
                <td>${escapeHtml(f.old_size_fmt)}</td>
                <td>${escapeHtml(f.new_size_fmt)}</td>
            `;
            frag.appendChild(tr);
        });
        tbody.appendChild(frag);
    } catch (e) {
        matchBadge.textContent = 'Ошибка';
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="4" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--danger);">${escapeHtml(e.message)}</td></tr>`;
    }
}

function closeTorrentDiffModal() {
    document.getElementById('diff-modal').style.display = 'none';
}

async function scanOrphans() {
    const tbody = document.getElementById('orphan-tbody');
    const summary = document.getElementById('orphan-summary');
    const rootsInfo = document.getElementById('orphan-roots-info');
    const delBtn = document.getElementById('btn-delete-orphans');
    const custom = document.getElementById('orphan-custom-root')?.value.trim();

    summary.textContent = '⏳ Сканирование...';
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg">⏳ Сверка файловой системы с торрентами...</td></tr>';
    delBtn.classList.add('hidden');

    try {
        const body = custom ? { roots: [custom] } : {};
        const res = await api('/api/tools/orphans/scan', 'POST', body);
        const d = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok || !d.success) {
            summary.textContent = 'Ошибка';
            tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--danger);">${escapeHtml(d.error || 'Ошибка')}</td></tr>`;
            return;
        }
        rootsInfo.textContent = 'Проверенные каталоги: ' + ((d.roots || []).join(', ') || 'не найдены');
        summary.textContent = `Найдено: ${d.count} (${d.total_size_fmt})`;

        const list = d.orphans || [];
        if (!list.length) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--accent);">🎉 Лишних файлов и папок не найдено!</td></tr>';
            return;
        }
        tbody.innerHTML = '';
        const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
        list.forEach(item => {
            const tr = document.createElement('tr');
            tr.innerHTML = `
                <td><input type="checkbox" class="orphan-chk" value="${escapeHtml(item.path)}" onchange="updateOrphanDelBtn()"></td>
                <td style="white-space:normal; word-break:break-all;" title="${escapeHtml(item.path)}"><b>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</b><div class="lbl-xs muted-text">${escapeHtml(item.root)}</div></td>
                <td>${item.is_dir ? '📁 Папка' : '📄 Файл'}</td>
                <td><b>${escapeHtml(item.size_fmt)}</b></td>
                <td class="muted-text">${escapeHtml(item.date)}</td>
            `;
            frag.appendChild(tr);
        });
        tbody.appendChild(frag);
    } catch (e) {
        summary.textContent = 'Ошибка';
        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="5" class="center-msg" style="color:var(--danger);">${escapeHtml(e.message)}</td></tr>`;
    }
}

function toggleAllOrphans(master) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.orphan-chk').forEach(c => { c.checked = master.checked; });
    updateOrphanDelBtn();
}

function updateOrphanDelBtn() {
    const cnt = document.querySelectorAll('.orphan-chk:checked').length;
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-delete-orphans');
    if (btn) {
        btn.classList.toggle('hidden', cnt === 0);
        btn.textContent = `🗑️ Удалить выбранные (${cnt}) навсегда`;
    }
}

async function deleteSelectedOrphans() {
    const paths = [...document.querySelectorAll('.orphan-chk:checked')].map(c => c.value);
    if (!paths.length) return;
    if (!confirm(`⚠️ ВНИМАНИЕ! Вы выбрали объектов: ${paths.length}.\nФайлы будут удалены НАВСЕГДА мимо корзины!\n\nПродолжить?`)) return;
    if (!confirm(`🚨 ПОСЛЕДНЕЕ ПРЕДУПРЕЖДЕНИЕ!\nТочно уничтожить ${paths.length} папок/файлов без возможности восстановления?`)) return;

    const res = await api('/api/tools/orphans/delete', 'POST', { paths, confirmed: true });
    const d = await res.json();
    const msg = `Удалено: ${(d.deleted || []).length}` + ((d.errors && d.errors.length) ? `\nОшибки:\n${d.errors.join('\n')}` : '');
    alert(msg);
    scanOrphans();
}

async function loadVaultSettings() {
    const inp = document.getElementById('vault-tags-input');
    const sel = document.getElementById('vault-aggregate-mode');
    if (!inp || !sel) return;
    try {
        const res = await api('/api/vault/settings');
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const d = await res.json();
        inp.value = (d.tags || []).join(', ');
        sel.value = d.aggregate_mode || 'exclude';
    } catch (e) {}
}

async function saveVaultSettings() {
    const tags = document.getElementById('vault-tags-input')?.value || '';
    const aggregate_mode = document.getElementById('vault-aggregate-mode')?.value || 'exclude';
    const res = await api('/api/vault/settings', 'POST', { tags, aggregate_mode });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка сохранения Private Vault');
    alert(`✅ Private Vault сохранён (${(d.tags || []).length} тегов).`);
}

// web/static/palette_targets.js
let _cmdItems = [];
let _cmdIdx = 0;

function renderTargetBarHtml(target) {
    if (!target) return '';
    const pct = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, parseFloat(target.percent || 0)));
    const color = target.achieved ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--info)';
    const goalTxt = target.target_ratio > 0 ? `${target.target_ratio}x` : target.target_bytes_fmt;
    return `
        <div style="margin-top:4px; width:100%;">
            <div class="row-between lbl-xs muted-text" style="font-size:10px; margin-bottom:2px;">
                <span>🎯 Цель: ${escapeHtml(goalTxt)}</span>
                <span style="color:${color}; font-weight:700;">${pct}%</span>
            </div>
            <div style="height:4px; background:var(--bg-main); border-radius:2px; overflow:hidden;">
                <div style="height:100%; width:${pct}%; background:${color}; transition:width 0.25s;"></div>
            </div>
        </div>
    `;
}

function openCommandPalette() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('cmd-palette-modal');
    const inp = document.getElementById('cmd-palette-input');
    if (!modal || !inp) return;
    modal.style.display = 'flex';
    inp.value = '';
    _cmdIdx = 0;
    renderCommandPalette();
    inp.focus();
}

function closeCommandPalette() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('cmd-palette-modal');
    if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
}

function _buildCommandActions() {
    const actions = [
        { label: '⚡ Вкладка: Раздачи', run: () => switchTab('torrents') },
        { label: '📊 Вкладка: Сводка', run: () => switchTab('summary') },
        { label: '📈 Вкладка: Аналитика', run: () => switchTab('analytics') },
        { label: '🛠️ Вкладка: Инструменты', run: () => switchTab('tools') },
        { label: '🎨 Тема: Dark (Тёмная)', run: () => _quickSetTheme('dark') },
        { label: '☀️ Тема: Light (Светлая)', run: () => _quickSetTheme('light') },
        { label: '🖤 Тема: OLED (Чёрная)', run: () => _quickSetTheme('oled') },
    ];
    if (typeof CURRENT_ROLE === 'undefined' || CURRENT_ROLE === 'admin') {
        actions.push(
            { label: '🔀 Вкладка: Подменщик', run: () => switchTab('merger') },
            { label: '🤖 Вкладка: Автопилот', run: () => switchTab('automation') },
            { label: '❄️ Умная заморозка / Разморозка', run: () => toggleSmartFreeze() },
            { label: '😴 Таймер сна (Snooze)', run: () => openSnoozeModal() },
            { label: '⚙️ Настройки системы', run: () => openSettingsModal() }
        );
    }
    return actions;
}

function _quickSetTheme(theme) {
    if (typeof getUiSettings === 'function' && typeof persistUiSettings === 'function') {
        const ui = getUiSettings();
        ui.theme = theme;
        persistUiSettings(ui);
    }
}

function renderCommandPalette() {
    const q = (document.getElementById('cmd-palette-input')?.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const listEl = document.getElementById('cmd-palette-list');
    if (!listEl) return;

    const actions = _buildCommandActions().filter(a => !q || a.label.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    const source = (typeof torrentsData !== 'undefined' && torrentsData.length)
        ? torrentsData
        : (typeof rawSummaryItems !== 'undefined' ? rawSummaryItems : []);

    const matchedTorrents = [];
    for (const item of source) {
        const nm = String(item.name || '');
        const hs = String(item.hash || item.hash_id || '');
        if (!q || nm.toLowerCase().includes(q) || hs.toLowerCase().includes(q)) {
            matchedTorrents.push({
                label: `📦 ${nm}`,
                sub: `${hs.slice(0, 10)} • ${item.uploaded_fmt || formatBytesJS(item.uploaded)}`,
                run: () => {
                    switchTab('torrents');
                    selectedHash = hs;
                    const t = torrentsData.find(x => x.hash === hs);
                    if (t) {
                        document.getElementById('selected-title').textContent = `${t.name} (${t.size_fmt})`;
                        renderTorrents(visibleTorrents);
                    }
                }
            });
        }
        if (matchedTorrents.length >= 20) break;
    }

    _cmdItems = [...actions, ...matchedTorrents].slice(0, 25);
    if (_cmdIdx >= _cmdItems.length) _cmdIdx = 0;

    listEl.innerHTML = '';
    if (!_cmdItems.length) {
        listEl.innerHTML = '<div class="center-msg muted-text">Ничего не найдено</div>';
        return;
    }
    _cmdItems.forEach((it, idx) => {
        const row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'row-between pointer';
        row.style.cssText = `padding:8px 10px; border-radius:6px; background:${idx === _cmdIdx ? 'rgba(46,204,113,0.18)' : 'var(--bg-input)'}; border:1px solid ${idx === _cmdIdx ? 'var(--accent)' : 'transparent'};`;
        row.innerHTML = `<span style="font-size:12.5px; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap;">${escapeHtml(it.label)}</span>` +
            (it.sub ? `<span class="lbl-xs muted-text">${escapeHtml(it.sub)}</span>` : '');
        row.onclick = () => { closeCommandPalette(); it.run(); };
        listEl.appendChild(row);
    });
}

function onCmdPaletteKey(e) {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { closeCommandPalette(); return; }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (_cmdItems.length) _cmdIdx = (_cmdIdx + 1) % _cmdItems.length;
        renderCommandPalette();
    } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (_cmdItems.length) _cmdIdx = (_cmdIdx - 1 + _cmdItems.length) % _cmdItems.length;
        renderCommandPalette();
    } else if (e.key === 'Enter' && _cmdItems[_cmdIdx]) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const chosen = _cmdItems[_cmdIdx];
        closeCommandPalette();
        chosen.run();
    }
}

function openTargetModal() {
    if (typeof selectedHash === 'undefined' || !selectedHash) return alert(t('err_select_torrent'));
    const item = (typeof torrentsData !== 'undefined' ? torrentsData : []).find(x => x.hash === selectedHash);
    document.getElementById('tgt-torrent-name').textContent = item ? item.name : selectedHash;
    const tgt = item?.target || null;
    document.getElementById('tgt-ratio').value = tgt && tgt.target_ratio > 0 ? tgt.target_ratio : '';
    document.getElementById('tgt-gib').value = tgt && tgt.target_bytes > 0 ? Math.round(tgt.target_bytes / (1024 ** 3)) : '';
    document.getElementById('btn-tgt-remove').classList.toggle('hidden', !tgt);
    document.getElementById('target-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}
function closeTargetModal() { document.getElementById('target-modal').style.display = 'none'; }

async function saveTargetForSelected() {
    const tr = parseFloat(document.getElementById('tgt-ratio').value) || 0;
    const gib = parseFloat(document.getElementById('tgt-gib').value) || 0;
    const res = await api('/api/targets/set', 'POST', {
        hash: selectedHash, target_ratio: tr, target_bytes: gib > 0 ? Math.round(gib * (1024 ** 3)) : 0
    });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok || !d.success) return alert(d.error || 'Ошибка');
    closeTargetModal();
    loadTorrents(false);
}

async function removeTargetForSelected() {
    const res = await api('/api/targets/delete', 'POST', { hash: selectedHash });
    if (res.ok) { closeTargetModal(); loadTorrents(false); }
}
